JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>、大変よ！<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルが…<line-break>エステルがさらわれたの。<line-break>シャリに連れ去られたのよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3<value:$28>、一大事じゃ！<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルがさらわれた。<line-break>シャリに連れ去られたらしい！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28>、<line-break>ひょっとして大事件かも！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステル様が誘拐されたって。<line-break>シャリに連れ去られたっていう話。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>、<line-break>こんな所にいたのかよ！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>大変だ！<line-break>エステルがさらわれた。<line-break>シャリに連れ去られちまった！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…ここにいたか。<line-break>エステルがさらわれた。<line-break>シャリの仕業らしい。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、大変だよ！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルがさらわれたんだ！<line-break>また、あのシャリの仕業だぜ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>、一大事じゃ！<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルがさらわれた！<line-break>シャリに連れ去られたそうじゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>大変だよ！　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルが…<line-break>エステルが連れ去られたんだ。<line-break>シャリにさらわれたんだよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17<value:$28>！　捜してよ！<line-break>まったく、アタクシに捜させるなんて<line-break>下等生物のくせに５００年早くてよ！<line-break>エステルが、さらわれちゃったのよ。<line-break>シャリに連れ去られたって話なのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>、<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>大変だよぉ！　　　　　　　　　<line-break>エステルお姉ちゃんが…<line-break>エステルお姉ちゃんがね！<line-break>シャリにさらわれちゃったって。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>27大変だ、<value:$28>！<line-break>エステルがシャリにさらわれた！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26大変じゃ！　大変じゃ！<line-break>エステルがシャリにさらわれた！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>さ～ん！<line-break>たたた大変です！<line-break>　<line-break>エステルさんがシャリさんに<line-break>さらわれちゃいました！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191<value:$28>大変よ！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルがさらわれたらしいわ。<line-break>シャリの仕業よ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190こんなところにいたのか！<line-break>いいか、落ち着いて聞け。<line-break>　<line-break>エステルがシャリにさらわれた。<end>ねぇ、<value:$28>！<line-break>大変だよぉ、大変だったらぁ！　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルが、<line-break>シャリにさらわれちゃったんだよ！ <end>"

KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>, 큰일이야! 에스텔이… 샤리에게 납치됐어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3<value:$28>, 큰일이다! 에스텔이 납치됐다. 샤리가 데려간 모양이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28>, 큰 사건일지도! 에스텔 님이 납치됐대. 샤리가 데려갔다는 이야기야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>, 여기 있었냐! 큰일이야! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…여기 있었나. 에스텔이 납치됐다. 샤리 짓인 모양이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 큰일이야! 에스텔이 납치됐어! 또 샤리 짓이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>, 큰일이다! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐다더군!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, <value:$28>, 큰일이야! 에스텔이… 샤리에게 끌려갔어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17<value:$28>! 찾아봐! 고귀한 아타쿠시에게 찾게 하다니 하등생물 주제에 500년은 일러! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐대!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, <value:$28>! 큰일이야아! 에스텔 언니가 샤리에게 납치됐대!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27큰일이다, <value:$28>! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26큰일이다! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28> 씨~! 크, 큰일이에요! 에스텔 씨가 샤리 씨에게 납치됐어요!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191<value:$28>, 큰일이야! 에스텔이 납치됐대. 샤리 짓이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190여기 있었나! 잘 들어. 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐다.<end>있잖아, <value:$28>! 큰일이야아! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐어!<end>"

<end><if><value:$01><equal>10さすが姉ちゃん！<line-break>その水晶があれば<line-break>エステルの居場所がわかるよな！<line-break>見ろよ！　何か映ってるぜ。<line-break>これ…竜骨の砂漠だよな？<line-break>ほら、ラドラスだよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15エステルの水晶！<line-break>それで、エステルの居場所が<line-break>わかるんだよね。<line-break>竜骨の砂漠が映ってる。<line-break>ラドラスのあたりだ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27エステルの水晶？<line-break>これは…竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>そうか、ラドラスか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26エステルの水晶か<line-break>これは、竜骨の砂漠…<line-break>ラドラスじゃな<end><if><value:$01><equal>193エステルさんの水晶…。<line-break>なるほど！<line-break>これで居場所がわかりますよ！<line-break>うーん、何か映ってますね…<line-break>これは…竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>ここ、きっとラドラスです！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191さすが<value:$28>。<line-break>この水晶を使えば<line-break>エステルの居場所がわかるわね。<line-break>何か映ってるわ。<line-break>これは竜骨の砂漠…。<line-break>エステルはラドラスにいるのね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190エステルの水晶？<line-break>それでエステルの居場所が<line-break>わかるのか。<line-break>何か見えるぞ。<line-break>これは竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>そうか、ラドラスか！<end>エステルの水晶！　　　<line-break>そっか、これでエステルの<line-break>居場所がわかるね。<line-break>見て、見て！　何か映ってる。<line-break>竜骨の砂漠だよ、これ。<line-break>ほらラドラスがある…！ EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Esther's Crystal? So this will show us where Esther is. That's the Dragonbone Desert... somewhere near Ladras?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Esther's Crystal...! See? There's Ladras...!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2에스텔의 수정? 이걸로 에스텔의 위치를 알 수 있네. 이건 용골 사막… 라드라스 근처…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3에스텔의 수정인가…! 이걸로 에스텔의 위치를 알 수 있겠군! 용골 사막…! 라드라스 근처다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4에스텔 님의 수정? 그렇구나, 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있겠어! 꺅, 뭔가 보여! 용골 사막…? 라드라스 근처인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5에스텔의 수정? 그래, 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있었지. 이건… 용골 사막… 라드라스잖아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13에스텔의 수정인가…. 전에도 썼지. 이번엔 어디 잡혀 있나? 용골 사막이 보인다. …라드라스인가!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10역시 누나! 그 수정이 있으면 에스텔 위치를 알 수 있지! 봐, 뭔가 보여. 이거 용골 사막이지? 저기 라드라스야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14에스텔의 수정인가? 하하하, 잊고 있었군! 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있겠어. 용골 사막이 보이는구나! 알았다, 라드라스다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15에스텔의 수정! 그걸로 위치를 알 수 있지. 용골 사막이 보여. 라드라스 근처야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17수정? 에스텔의? 그런 게 정말 쓸모 있을까? 어머, 뭔가 보이네…. 이건 용골 사막이야! 알았어, 라드라스구나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19에스텔 언니의 수정! 그걸로 위치를 알 수 있지. 와, 봐! 용골 사막이야. 저기 라드라스야아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27에스텔의 수정? 이건… 용골 사막…? 그래, 라드라스인가!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26에스텔의 수정인가. 이건 용골 사막… 라드라스군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193에스텔 씨의 수정…. 그렇군요! 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있어요! 뭔가 보이네요… 용골 사막…? 여긴 분명 라드라스예요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191역시 <value:$28>. 이 수정을 쓰면 에스텔 위치를 알 수 있겠네. 뭔가 보여. 용골 사막…. 에스텔은 라드라스에 있어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190에스텔의 수정? 그걸로 위치를 알 수 있나. 뭔가 보인다. 용골 사막…? 그래, 라드라스인가!<end>에스텔의 수정! 그렇구나, 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있네. 봐, 뭔가 보여. 용골 사막이야. 저기 라드라스가 있어…!<end>"

## 890084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2シャリはいよいよ、<line-break>ラドラスで事を起こす気ね。<line-break>急ぎましょ。エステルが危ないわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3エステルもシャリもラドラスじゃな。<line-break><value:$28>、急ごうぞ。<line-break>一刻も早くラドラスへ行かねば。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4エステル様は捕らわれの身。　　　　<line-break>ラドラスで、王子様の助けを<line-break>待っているのね…。きゃっ！<line-break>でも、運命は残酷かも…。<line-break>助けに行くのは、王子様じゃなくて<line-break><value:$28>だったりするのね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5行こうぜ、<value:$28>！<line-break>エステルを助けるんだ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>きっとエステルだって、<line-break>ラドラスで<line-break>俺たちの助けを待ってるぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…やめておけ。　　　　　　<line-break>足手まといなのが<line-break>いなくなっただけだ…。<line-break>…と言っても、<line-break>お前は助けに行くのだろう？<line-break>仕方あるまい。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、急ごうぜ。　　　　　<line-break>早くしないと、<line-break>エステルが危ない。<line-break>何しろ、相手はシャリだからな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14一刻も早く、<line-break>ラドラスへ行かにゃならんぞ。　<line-break>エステルを助けにの。<line-break>何しろ、相手はシャリじゃて。<line-break>ラドラスの魔道技術を使って<line-break>何をやらかしおるか…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15行こうよ、ラドラスへ。<line-break>エステルを助けなきゃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17わかったわ！　ピンときたわ！<line-break>さすが、アタクシは高貴なエルフだわ！<line-break>シャリのねらいはラドラスよ！<line-break>そう、その魔道技術を手に入れるため<line-break>エステルをさらったのだわ！<line-break>行くのよー！　ラドラスへ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19急ごうよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>エステルお姉ちゃんを　　<line-break>助けなくちゃ！<line-break>ラドラスに行かなくちゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27急ごう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ラドラスへ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26急ぐのじゃ！<line-break>シャリめ、何をするつもりじゃ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193急ぎましょう<value:$28>さん！<line-break>絶対にエステルさんを助けないと！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191行きましょ、<value:$28>。<line-break>早くエステルを助けないと<line-break>シャリが何をするか…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190エステルを助けにいくぞ！<line-break>急がないとシャリが何をするか…。<end>ラドラスへ行くんでしょ？<line-break>エステルを助けに。<line-break>　<line-break>急がないとね。<line-break>相手はシャリだから。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Shalli is finally about to make his move in Ladras. Let's hurry. Esther is in danger.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Esther and Shalli are both in Ladras. We must hurry, <value:$28>. We have to reach Ladras as quickly as we can.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Lady Esther is a captive, waiting in Ladras for a prince to rescue her... Ah! But fate can be cruel. Perhaps it won't be a prince who comes to her rescue, but <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Come on, <value:$28>! We're going to save Esther. I'm sure she's waiting for us to rescue her in Ladras.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Leave it. We're merely rid of some deadweight... Though you'll go to the rescue regardless, won't you? Very well.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Come on, sis, we have to hurry. Esther will be in danger if we don't get there soon. She's dealing with Shalli, after all.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14We must make haste for Ladras and rescue Esther. Shalli is our foe, after all. Who knows what he might do with Ladras's magical technology...<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Come on, let's go to Ladras. We have to save Esther.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17I've got it! It all makes sense! Of course it does―I'm a noble Elf! Shalli is after Ladras! Yes, he kidnapped Esther so he could seize its magical technology! To Ladras!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19We have to hurry, <value:$28>. We have to save Esther! We have to get to Ladras!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Let's hurry, <value:$28>. To Ladras!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Make haste! What is that wretch Shalli planning?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193We have to hurry, <value:$28>! We absolutely must save Esther!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Let's go, <value:$28>. We have to hurry and save Esther. Who knows what Shalli might do...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190We're going to save Esther! We have to hurry. Who knows what Shalli might do...<end>We're going to Ladras to save Esther, aren't we? We'd better hurry. We're dealing with Shalli, after all.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2샤리가 마침내 라드라스에서 일을 벌이려는 것 같아. 서두르자. 에스텔이 위험해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3에스텔도 샤리도 라드라스에 있군. <value:$28>, 서두르자. 한시라도 빨리 라드라스로 가야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4에스텔 님은 붙잡힌 몸. 라드라스에서 왕자님의 구원을 기다리는구나…. 꺅! 하지만 운명은 잔혹할지도…. 구하러 가는 건 왕자님이 아니라 <value:$28>라니.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5가자, <value:$28>! 에스텔을 구하는 거야. 분명 라드라스에서 우리 도움을 기다릴 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…그만둬라. 짐이 하나 없어진 것뿐이다…. …그래도 너는 구하러 가겠지? 어쩔 수 없군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 서두르자. 늦으면 에스텔이 위험해. 상대는 샤리라고.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14한시라도 빨리 라드라스로 가야 한다. 에스텔을 구하러 말이지. 상대가 샤리니까. 라드라스의 마도 기술로 무슨 짓을 할지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>15라드라스로 가자. 에스텔을 구해야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17알았어! 감이 왔어! 역시 고귀한 엘프인 아타쿠시야! 샤리의 목표는 라드라스야! 마도 기술을 손에 넣으려고 에스텔을 납치한 거야! 가는 거야, 라드라스로!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19서두르자, <value:$28>. 에스텔 언니를 구해야 해! 라드라스로 가야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27서두르자, <value:$28>. 라드라스로!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26서두르자! 샤리 녀석, 무슨 짓을 하려는 게냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193서둘러요 <value:$28> 씨! 반드시 에스텔 씨를 구해야 해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191가자, <value:$28>. 빨리 에스텔을 구하지 않으면 샤리가 무슨 짓을 할지…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190에스텔을 구하러 간다! 서두르지 않으면 샤리가 무슨 짓을 할지….<end>라드라스로 가는 거지? 에스텔을 구하러. 서둘러야 해. 상대는 샤리니까.<end>"

## 890085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流しましょ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流じゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4エステル様…<line-break>なんか…かわいそうかも！<line-break>きゃっ！<line-break>それじゃ、準備してこよっと！<line-break>町を出たところで合流ね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流だ！<line-break>お前も急げよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13急げ。<line-break>町を出るときに<line-break>合流する。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10それじゃ、準備してくる！<line-break>町を出たところで合流だな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14それでは、<line-break>準備してくるぞい！<line-break>町を出たところで合流じゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流だね！<line-break>急いで！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17…何よ。その、当たり前だ、<line-break>少しは人の話も聞きなさい、<line-break>って感じの冷ややかなマナザシは…。<line-break>ハハン、アタクシの才能への嫉妬？<line-break>とにかく、グズグズしてないで<line-break>早く準備してくるのよぉ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19それじゃあ、いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流だね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27それじゃあ、いつものように、<line-break>町から出るときに合流だね<end><if><value:$01><equal>26いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流じゃな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>193いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流しましょう！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>191いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流するわよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>190いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流するぞ！ <end>それじゃあ、いつものように<line-break>町を出るときに合流しよう！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3We'll meet when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Lady Esther... I kind of feel sorry for her! Oh! I'd better go get ready! We'll meet just outside town!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual! You'd better hurry too!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hurry. We'll meet when we leave town.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10I'll go get ready! We'll meet just outside town!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Well then, I'll go get ready! We'll meet just outside town!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual! Hurry!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What? What's with that cold look that says, “Obviously. And try listening to someone else for once”? Hah! Jealous of my talent? In any case, stop dawdling and hurry up and get ready!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Then we'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Then we'll meet up when we leave town, as usual.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26We'll meet when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end>Then we'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하세!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4에스텔 님… 뭔가… 불쌍할지도! 꺅! 그럼 준비하고 올게! 마을 밖에서 합류해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류한다! 너도 서둘러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13서둘러. 마을을 나갈 때 합류한다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그럼 준비하고 올게! 마을 밖에서 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그럼 준비하고 오마! 마을 밖에서 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자! 서둘러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17…뭐야. 당연하지, 남의 말도 좀 들어, 라고 말하는 듯한 차가운 눈빛은…. 하하, 내 재능이 질투 나? 아무튼 꾸물대지 말고 빨리 준비하고 와ー!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그럼 평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그럼 평소처럼 마을에서 나갈 때 합류하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류할 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류한다!<end>그럼 평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end>"

## 890086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2エステルの水晶？<line-break>これで、エステルの居場所が<line-break>わかるの？<line-break>これは…竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>何かしら、この塔は…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3エステルの水晶か…！<line-break>こいつで、エステルの<line-break>居場所がわかるかもしれんな。<line-break>竜骨の砂漠…じゃと…？<line-break>この塔の群はなんじゃ…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4エステル様の水晶？<line-break>そっか、これでエステル様の<line-break>居場所がわかるかも！<line-break>きゃっ、何か映ってる！<line-break>…ええと、竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>なんか、変な塔がいっぱい！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5エステルの水晶？　　　<line-break>こんなんでエステルの<line-break>居場所がわかるのかよ。<line-break>これは…、<line-break>ええと、竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>おい、これであってんのかよ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13水晶だと…？<line-break>それで、あのうざったい女の<line-break>行方がわかるのか？<line-break>竜骨の砂漠が映っている。<line-break>…なんだ、この塔の群は。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10さすが姉ちゃん！<line-break>その水晶で<line-break>エステルを捜すんだ！　　　<line-break>やったぜ！　<line-break>何か映ってるぜ。<line-break>ええと、これ…竜骨の砂漠？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14エステルの水晶？<line-break>こんなんで、いったい、<line-break>エステルの居場所がわかるのかの。<line-break>したり！<line-break>竜骨の砂漠が映っておるわい！<line-break>ここにいるのじゃな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15エステルの水晶？<line-break>それで、エステルの居場所が　　　<line-break>わかるんだね。<line-break>…竜骨の砂漠が映ってる。<line-break>あと、塔の群も…。<line-break>ねえ、彼女はここにいるのかな…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17水晶？　エステルの？<line-break>そんなものが<line-break>ホントに役に立つかしら？<line-break>あら、確かに何か映っている…。<line-break>これは…、竜骨の砂漠だわ！<line-break>塔の群…？　何よ、これ！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19エステルお姉ちゃんの水晶！　<line-break>それで、お姉ちゃんの居場所が<line-break>わかるかもしれないよ。<line-break>わぁ、見て！　何か映ってるよ！<line-break>えっと、これ竜骨の砂漠だよぉ！　　<line-break>たくさん、塔が立ってる。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>27エステルの水晶？<line-break>映っているのは…竜骨の砂漠だな。<line-break>しかし…塔の群はいったい… <end><if><value:$01><equal>26エステルの水晶じゃと？<line-break>何か映っているな。<line-break> <line-break>これは…、竜骨の砂漠じゃ！<line-break>しかし…なんなのじゃ？<line-break>この塔の群は…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>193エステルさんの水晶…。<line-break>なるほど！<line-break>これで居場所がわかりますよ！<line-break>うーん、何か映ってますね…<line-break>これは…竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>でもなんでしょう、この塔…<end><if><value:$01><equal>191さすが<value:$28>。<line-break>この水晶を使えば<line-break>エステルの居場所がわかるわね。<line-break>何か映ってるわ。<line-break>これは竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>でもなに、この塔の群れは…？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190エステルの水晶？<line-break>それでエステルの居場所が<line-break>わかるのか。<line-break>何か見えるぞ。<line-break>これは竜骨の砂漠…？<line-break>だが、なんだこの塔の群は…。<end>エステルの水晶！　　　　　　<line-break>そっか、これでエステルの<line-break>居場所がわかるかもね。<line-break>見て、見て！　何か映ってる。<line-break>竜骨の砂漠だよ。それに、塔の群…。<line-break>神話の歌にある空中都市かも…！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Esther's Crystal? Can this tell us where Esther is? Is this... the Dragonbone Desert? What are these towers...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Esther's Crystal...! This might tell us where Esther is. The Dragonbone Desert...? And what is this cluster of towers...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Esther's Crystal? Oh, right! Maybe this can tell us where Lady Esther is! Ah! Something's appearing! Um... is that the Dragonbone Desert? There are strange towers everywhere!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Esther's Crystal? This little thing can tell us where Esther is? That's... um, the Dragonbone Desert? Hey, are we sure this thing's right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13A crystal...? That can tell us where that irritating woman went? It's showing the Dragonbone Desert. What is this cluster of towers?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10I knew you could do it, Sis! You're going to use that crystal to find Esther! All right! Something's showing up. Uh, is this... the Dragonbone Desert?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Esther's Crystal? Can such a thing truly tell us where Esther is? Aha! It shows the Dragonbone Desert! So that is where she is!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Esther's Crystal? So this can tell us where Esther is. It's showing the Dragonbone Desert... and a cluster of towers. Do you think she's there...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17A crystal? Esther's? Can such a thing truly be of any use? Oh, something really is appearing... This is the Dragonbone Desert! A cluster of towers...? What is this!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Esther's Crystal! Maybe this can tell us where she is. Wow, look! Something's appearing! Um, that's the Dragonbone Desert! And there are lots of towers.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Esther's Crystal? It's showing... the Dragonbone Desert. But what is that cluster of towers...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Esther's Crystal, you say? Something is appearing. Why, this is the Dragonbone Desert! But what is this cluster of towers...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Esther's Crystal... Of course! This will tell us where she is! Hmm, something's appearing... Is that the Dragonbone Desert? But what is this tower...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I knew you could do it, <value:$28>. We can use Esther's Crystal to find her. Something's appearing. Is that the Dragonbone Desert? But what is this cluster of towers...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Esther's Crystal? Can it tell us where Esther is? I can see something. Is that the Dragonbone Desert? But what is this cluster of towers...?<end>Esther's Crystal! Oh, right! Maybe this can tell us where Esther is. Look, look! Something's appearing. It's the Dragonbone Desert, and there's a cluster of towers... Could it be the Floating City sung of in legend...?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2에스텔의 수정? 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있어? 이건… 용골 사막…? 이 탑들은 뭐지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>3에스텔의 수정인가…! 이걸로 위치를 알지도 모르겠군. 용골 사막…? 이 탑 무리는 뭐지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>4에스텔 님의 수정? 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있겠어! 꺅, 뭔가 보여! 용골 사막…? 이상한 탑이 잔뜩 있어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5에스텔의 수정? 이런 걸로 위치를 알 수 있나. 이건… 용골 사막…? 야, 이거 맞아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13수정이라고…? 그걸로 그 성가신 여자의 행방을 알 수 있나? 용골 사막이 보인다. …이 탑들은 뭐지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10역시 누나! 그 수정으로 에스텔을 찾는 거야! 뭔가 보여. 이거 용골 사막이지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14에스텔의 수정? 이런 걸로 위치를 알 수 있나. 오! 용골 사막이 보인다! 여기에 있구나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15에스텔의 수정? 그걸로 위치를 알 수 있지. 용골 사막과 탑들이 보여…. 여기 있는 걸까….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17수정? 에스텔의? 정말 쓸모 있나? 어머, 뭔가 보이네…. 용골 사막이야! 탑 무리…? 뭐야 이게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19에스텔 언니의 수정! 이걸로 위치를 알지도 몰라. 와, 뭔가 보여! 용골 사막이야아! 탑도 잔뜩 있어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27에스텔의 수정? 보이는 건… 용골 사막이군. 하지만 이 탑들은 대체….<end><if><value:$01><equal>26에스텔의 수정인가? 뭔가 보인다. 이건 용골 사막이다! 하지만 이 탑들은….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193에스텔 씨의 수정…. 그렇군요! 이걸로 위치를 알 수 있어요! 뭔가 보이네요… 용골 사막…? 그런데 이 탑은 뭐죠….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191역시 <value:$28>. 이 수정을 쓰면 에스텔 위치를 알 수 있어. 뭔가 보여. 용골 사막…? 그런데 이 탑 무리는 뭐지…?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190에스텔의 수정? 그걸로 위치를 알 수 있나. 뭔가 보인다. 용골 사막…? 그런데 이 탑들은 뭐지….<end>에스텔의 수정! 그렇구나, 이걸로 위치를 알지도 몰라. 봐, 뭔가 보여. 용골 사막과 탑 무리…. 신화의 노래에 나오는 공중도시일지도…!<end>"

## 890087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>、急ぎましょ。<line-break>エステルが危ないわ。<line-break>…なんだか、そんな予感がするの。　　<line-break>誘拐されてるから危ないのは当然だけど<line-break>それだけじゃなくて…。<line-break>この塔で大変なことが起こりそうな…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3<value:$28>、急ごうぞ。<line-break>一刻も早くこの塔の群へ行かねば。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4エステル様は捕らわれの身。　　　　<line-break>この塔で、王子様の助けを<line-break>待っているのね…。きゃっ！<line-break>でも、運命は残酷かも…。<line-break>助けに行くのは、王子様じゃなくて<line-break><value:$28>だったりするのね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5行こうぜ、<value:$28>！<line-break>エステルを助けるんだ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>きっとエステルだって、<line-break>竜骨の砂漠の真ん中で<line-break>俺たちの助けを待ってるぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…やめておけ。　　　　　　<line-break>足手まといなのが<line-break>いなくなっただけだ…。<line-break>…と言っても、<line-break>お前は助けに行くのだろう？<line-break>仕方あるまい。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、急ごうぜ。　　　　　<line-break>早くしないと、<line-break>エステルが危ない。<line-break>何しろ、相手はシャリだからな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14一刻も早く、<line-break>あそこへ行かにゃならんぞ。　　<line-break>エステルを助けにの。<line-break>何しろ、相手はシャリじゃて。<line-break>何をやらかしおるか…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15とにかく、行こうよ。<line-break>エステルを助けなきゃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17わかったわ！　ピンときたわ！<line-break>さすが、アタクシは高貴なエルフだわ！<line-break>シャリのねらいはあの塔よ！<line-break>そして、そうよそうよ、そうなのよ！<line-break>エステルもあの塔と何か関係があるの！<line-break>行くのよぉ！　ラドラスへ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19急ごうよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>エステルお姉ちゃんを　　<line-break>助けなくちゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27急ごう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ラドラスへ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26急ぐのじゃ！<line-break>シャリめ、何をするつもりじゃ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193急ぎましょう<value:$28>さん！<line-break>絶対にエステルさんを助けないと！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191行きましょ、<value:$28>。<line-break>早くエステルを助けないと<line-break>シャリが何をするか…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190エステルを助けにいくぞ！<line-break>急がないとシャリが何をしだすか…。<end>竜骨の砂漠へ行くんでしょ？<line-break>エステルを捜しに。<line-break>　<line-break>急がないとね。<line-break>相手はシャリだから。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2We have to hurry, <value:$28>. Esther's in danger. I just have this feeling. Of course she's in danger if she's been kidnapped, but there's more to it than that... I feel as if something terrible is about to happen in this tower...<end><if><value:$01><equal>3We must hurry, <value:$28>. We must reach those towers as swiftly as we can.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Lady Esther is a captive, waiting in that tower for a prince to rescue her... Oh! But fate can be so cruel. Instead of a prince, she may find <value:$28> coming to her rescue.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Let's go, <value:$28>! We've got to save Esther. I'm sure she's waiting for us to rescue her, right in the middle of the Dragonbone Desert.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Forget it. We're simply rid of dead weight... Still, you're going to rescue her anyway, aren't you? Fine.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Let's hurry, Sis. Esther will be in danger if we don't get there soon. Shalli's the one we're dealing with, after all.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14We must get there as swiftly as we can and rescue Esther. Shalli is the one we're dealing with. Who knows what he might do...<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Come on, let's go. We have to save Esther.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17I've got it! It just came to me! Of course it did―I am a noble Elf! Shalli is after that tower! And yes, yes, that's it! Esther has some connection to it too! We're going to Ladras!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Let's hurry, <value:$28>. We have to save Esther!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Let's hurry, <value:$28>. To Ladras!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Hurry! What is that wretch Shalli plotting?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193We have to hurry, <value:$28>! We absolutely must save Esther!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Let's go, <value:$28>. If we don't save Esther quickly, who knows what Shalli might do...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190We're going to rescue Esther! If we don't hurry, who knows what Shalli might do...<end>You're going to the Dragonbone Desert, aren't you? To look for Esther. We'd better hurry. We're up against Shalli.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>, 서두르자. 에스텔이 위험해. 왠지 그런 예감이 들어. 납치됐으니 위험한 건 당연하지만 그뿐만이 아니라…. 이 탑에서 큰일이 벌어질 것 같은….<end><if><value:$01><equal>3<value:$28>, 서두르자. 한시라도 빨리 저 탑들로 가야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4에스텔 님은 붙잡힌 몸. 이 탑에서 왕자님의 구원을 기다리는구나…. 꺅! 하지만 운명은 잔혹할지도. 구하러 가는 건 왕자님이 아니라 <value:$28>라니.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5가자, <value:$28>! 에스텔을 구하는 거야. 분명 용골 사막 한가운데서 우리 도움을 기다리고 있을 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…그만둬라. 짐 하나가 없어진 것뿐이다…. …그래도 넌 구하러 가겠지? 어쩔 수 없군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 서두르자. 늦으면 에스텔이 위험해. 상대는 샤리라고.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14한시라도 빨리 저곳으로 가야 해. 에스텔을 구하러 말이다. 상대가 샤리니 무슨 짓을 할지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>15아무튼 가자. 에스텔을 구해야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17알았어! 감이 왔어! 역시 고귀한 엘프인 아타쿠시야! 샤리의 목표는 저 탑이야! 그리고 에스텔도 저 탑과 뭔가 관련이 있어! 가는 거야, 라드라스로!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19서두르자, <value:$28>. 에스텔 언니를 구해야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27서두르자, <value:$28>. 라드라스로!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26서두르자! 샤리 녀석, 무슨 짓을 하려는 게냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193서둘러요 <value:$28> 씨! 반드시 에스텔 씨를 구해야 해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191가자, <value:$28>. 빨리 에스텔을 구하지 않으면 샤리가 무슨 짓을 할지…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190에스텔을 구하러 간다! 서두르지 않으면 샤리가 무슨 짓을 할지….<end>용골 사막으로 가는 거지? 에스텔을 찾으러. 서둘러야 해. 상대는 샤리니까.<end>"

## 890088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流しましょ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流じゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4エステル様…<line-break>なんか…かわいそうかも！<line-break>きゃっ！<line-break>それじゃ、準備してこよっと！<line-break>街を出たところで合流ね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流だ！<line-break>お前も急げよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13急げ。<line-break>町を出るときに<line-break>合流する。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10それじゃ、準備してくる！<line-break>街を出たところで合流だな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14それでは、<line-break>準備してくるぞい！<line-break>街を出たところで合流じゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流だね！<line-break>急いで！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17…何よ。<line-break>その、当てずっぽうじゃない？<line-break>って感じの冷ややかなマナザシは…。<line-break>ハハン、アタクシの才能への嫉妬？<line-break>とにかく、グズグズしてないで<line-break>早く準備してくるのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19それじゃあ、いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流だね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27それでは、いつものように、<line-break>町から出るときに合流しよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流じゃな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>193いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流しましょう！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>191いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流するわよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>190いつものように、<line-break>町を出るときに合流するぞ！ <end>それじゃあ、いつものように<line-break>町を出るときに合流しよう！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3We'll meet when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Lady Esther... I kind of feel sorry for her! Oh! I'd better go get ready! We'll meet just outside town!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual! You'd better hurry too!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hurry. We'll meet when we leave town.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10I'll go get ready! We'll meet just outside town!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Well then, I'll go get ready! We'll meet just outside town!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual! Hurry!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What? What's with that cold look that says, “You're just guessing, aren't you?” Hah! Jealous of my talent? In any case, stop dawdling and hurry up and get ready!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Then we'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Then we'll meet up when we leave town, as usual.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26We'll meet when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190We'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end>Then we'll meet up when we leave town, as usual!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하세!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4에스텔 님… 뭔가… 불쌍할지도! 꺅! 그럼 준비하고 올게! 마을 밖에서 합류해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류한다! 너도 서둘러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13서둘러. 마을을 나갈 때 합류한다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그럼 준비하고 올게! 마을 밖에서 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그럼 준비하고 오마! 마을 밖에서 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자! 서둘러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17…뭐야. 그거 그냥 찍은 거 아니야? 하는 듯한 차가운 눈빛은…. 하하, 내 재능이 질투 나? 아무튼 꾸물대지 말고 빨리 준비하고 와ー!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그럼 평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그럼 평소처럼 마을에서 나갈 때 합류하지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류할 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류한다!<end>그럼 평소처럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자!<end>"

## 890089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、<value:$28>さんだね？<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're <value:$28>, right?<end>"
KO: "당신, <value:$28> 씨지?<end>"

## 890090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じ冒険者のよしみで、<line-break>ひとつ教えてやろうと思ってな。　<line-break>　<line-break>エステルって娘、<line-break>あんたの仲間だろう？<end>"
EN: "As one adventurer to another, I thought I'd let you know something. Esther's one of your companions, right?<end>"
KO: "같은 모험자끼리 하나 알려 주려고. 에스텔이라는 애, 네 동료지?<end>"

## 890091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女、どうやら、<line-break>厄介ごとに巻き込まれたようだぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>シャリって魔道士が、<line-break>彼女を無理やり連れていくのを<line-break>見かけたよ。<end>"
EN: "Looks like she's gotten mixed up in some trouble. I saw a mage named Shalli forcing her to go with him.<end>"
KO: "그녀가 골치 아픈 일에 휘말린 모양이야. 샤리라는 마도사가 억지로 데려가는 걸 봤어.<end>"

## 890092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の話はそれだけだ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That's all I have to say.<end>"
KO: "내 이야기는 그게 전부다.<end>"

## 890093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エステル…！）<end>"
EN: "(Esther...!)<end>"
KO: "(에스텔…!)<end>"

## 890094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エステルの水晶に<line-break>　何か映ってる。）<line-break>　<line-break>（…竜骨の砂漠…<line-break>　塔の群…。）<end>"
EN: "(Something's appearing in Esther's Crystal.) (The Dragonbone Desert... a cluster of towers...)<end>"
KO: "(에스텔의 수정에 뭔가 비치고 있다.) (…용골의 사막… 탑 무리….)<end>"

## 890095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（とにかく、助けに行かないと…！）<end>"
EN: "(I have to go save her...!)<end>"
KO: "(어쨌든 구하러 가야 해…!)<end>"

## 890096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님!!<end>"

## 890097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>エステル様が、エステル様が！<line-break>　<line-break>シャリという魔術師に<line-break>さらわれてしまいました！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Esther―Esther's been abducted by a sorcerer named Shalli!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님! 에스텔 님이, 에스텔 님이! 샤리라는 마술사에게 납치됐습니다!<end>"

## 890098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々がいつものように<line-break>エステル様を迎えに行った<line-break>その目の前で……！　　　　　　<line-break>我ながら、なんという…失態…！<end>"
EN: "We went to meet Esther as usual, and she was abducted right before our eyes...! What an... utter failure on our part...!<end>"
KO: "우리가 평소처럼 에스텔 님을 모시러 갔는데 바로 눈앞에서……! 나도 참, 무슨… 실태를…!<end>"

## 890099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もはや、頼みの綱は<line-break><value:$28>様だけなのです！！<end>"
EN: "You are our only hope now, <value:$28>!!<end>"
KO: "이제 의지할 분은 <value:$28>님뿐입니다!!<end>"

## 890100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです！<line-break>エステル様を助け出してください！！<end>"
EN: "Please! You must rescue Esther!!<end>"
KO: "부탁입니다! 에스텔 님을 구해 주세요!!<end>"

## 890101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは…エステル様の水晶…。<line-break>すばらしい！　そこに映る風景で<line-break>エステル様の居場所がわかるかも！！<end>"
EN: "That is Esther's crystal... Wonderful! The scene reflected in it may show us where she is!!<end>"
KO: "그건… 에스텔 님의 수정…. 훌륭해! 거기에 비치는 풍경을 보면 에스텔 님이 어디 계신지 알 수 있을지도 몰라!!<end>"

## 890102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…竜骨の砂漠……。<line-break>バカな！<line-break>我らのラドラスに……！？<end>"
EN: "This is... the Dragonbone Desert... Impossible! In our Ladras...!?<end>"
KO: "이건… 용골의 사막…… 말도 안 돼! 우리 라드라스에……!?<end>"

## 890103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、この水晶がラドラスを映すなら<line-break>我々はラドラスに戻るべきだろう。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様も、是非<line-break>ラドラスまで来て、我々に<line-break>力をお貸しください。<end>"
EN: "No. If the crystal is showing Ladras, then we should return there. Please come to Ladras as well, <value:$28>, and lend us your aid.<end>"
KO: "아니, 이 수정이 라드라스를 비춘다면 우리는 라드라스로 돌아가야겠지. <value:$28> 님도 꼭 라드라스까지 와서 저희에게 힘을 빌려 주십시오.<end>"

## 890104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、では行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Good. Let us be off.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 간다.<end>"

## 890105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エステルの水晶に<line-break>　何か映ってる。）<line-break>　<line-break>（…竜骨の砂漠…<line-break>　ラドラス…！）<end>"
EN: "(Something's showing in Esther's Crystal.) (...The Dragonbone Desert... Ladras...!)<end>"
KO: "(에스텔의 수정에 뭔가 비치고 있어.) (…용골 사막… 라드라스…!)<end>"

## 890106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…ここに<line-break>　エステルがいる！？）<end>"
EN: "(...Esther's here?!)<end>"
KO: "(…여기에 에스텔이 있어!?)<end>"

## 890107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　 　　汝は…。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　なるほど。<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者か…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　汝に頼みがある。<end>"
EN: "You are... I see. The Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Bearer of the Infinite Soul, I have a favor to ask of you.<end>"
KO: "그대는…. 그렇군. 무한의 소울을 가진 자인가…. 무한의 소울을 가진 자여…. 그대에게 부탁이 있다.<end>"

## 890108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　ここより先に、<line-break>　　フルーヴという巨人がおる。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　汝の力で葬ってくれ。<line-break>　 　あ奴は我の力を妨げる。<line-break>　　　不要な存在なのだ…。<end>"
EN: "Beyond this place dwells a giant called Fleuve. Bury it with your power. That creature impedes my power. Its existence serves no purpose...<end>"
KO: "이 앞에 프루브라는 거인이 있다. 네 힘으로 묻어 버려라. 그 녀석은 나의 힘을 방해한다. 불필요한 존재다…<end>"

## 890109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "알았어<end>"

## 890110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "関係ない<end>"
EN: "Not my problem.<end>"
KO: "상관없어<end>"

## 890111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　　よし。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　では我は、汝を<line-break>　　　　　　 精霊の座へと導かん。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　 　汝、気高き地の光を越え<line-break>　　　　 　　彼の巨人に挑むがよい。<line-break>　　　　  　挑み、打ち破るがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Good. I shall guide you to the Seat of the Spirits. Pass beyond the noble light of earth and face that giant. Challenge it, and strike it down...<end>"
KO: "좋다. 그럼 내가 그대를 정령의 자리로 인도하리라. 그대, 고귀한 땅의 빛을 넘어 저 거인에게 도전하라. 도전하여 쓰러뜨려라….<end>"

## 890112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　そうか。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ならば無理強いはすまい。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　うつろいやすき人の子よ。<line-break>　　　　　　気が変わったならば<line-break>　　　　　　また我を訪ねるがよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I shall not force the matter. Inconstant child of man, if you change your mind, come and seek me again.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그렇다면 억지로 강요하진 않겠다. 변덕스러운 인간의 아이여. 마음이 바뀌면 다시 나를 찾아오거라.<end>"

## 890113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　どうした？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　巨人を前に臆したか？<end>"
EN: "What is it? Have you lost your nerve before the giant?<end>"
KO: "왜 그러느냐? 거인을 앞에 두고 겁먹었느냐?<end>"

## 890114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはない<end>"
EN: "Not at all.<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아<end>"

## 890115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておこうかな<end>"
EN: "Maybe I should call it off.<end>"
KO: "그만둘까<end>"

## 890116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　ならば行くがよい。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Then go.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 가라.<end>"

## 890117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フン…貴様、<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者か…。<line-break>　<line-break>グラジェオンめ…、<line-break>闇の力の高まりを知り<line-break>我を滅ぼさんと欲したか。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... So you are the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Curse you, Grajeon... You sensed the growing power of darkness and sought to destroy me?<end>"
KO: "흥… 네놈, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자인가…. 그라제온 녀석… 어둠의 힘이 커지는 걸 알고 나를 없애려 한 건가.<end>"

## 890118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、汝ごときに倒される<line-break>このフルーヴではない。<line-break>来るがよい！！<end>"
EN: "But Fleuve will not fall to the likes of you. Come!<end>"
KO: "하지만 네놈 따위에게 쓰러질 이 프루브가 아니다. 덤벼라!!<end>"

## 890119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かなり、無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>精霊神に躍らされ、<line-break>我を撃ち、闇を呼ぶか…。<line-break>愚かなり…愚かなり…。<end>"
EN: "Foolish Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Made to dance to the Spirit God's tune, you strike me down and call forth darkness...? Foolish... How foolish.<end>"
KO: "어리석구나, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 정령신에게 놀아나 나를 치고 어둠을 부르는가…. 어리석구나… 어리석어….<end>"

## 890120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　よくやった…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<end>"
EN: "Well done... Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "잘했다… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 890121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　よくぞ、巨人フルーヴを倒した。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　礼を言うぞ。<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<end>"
EN: "You have done well to defeat Giant Fleuve. You have my thanks, Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "잘했다, 거인 프루브를 쓰러뜨렸구나. 감사한다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 890122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　さあ、再び行くがよい。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　汝のなすべき旅の道へ。<line-break>　　　　　　　　さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "Now go forth once more, along the path you are meant to travel. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "자, 다시 가거라. 네가 걸어야 할 여행의 길로. 잘 가라.<end>"

## 890123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: " 　　　来たか、無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。　　<line-break> 　　　　ならば、我は今一度汝に乞う。<line-break>  　　　彼の巨人を倒してはくれまいか？<end>"
EN: "So, you have come, Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Then I beseech you once more: will you defeat that giant?<end>"
KO: "왔느냐, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여…. 그렇다면 다시 한번 청하노라. 저 거인을 쓰러뜨려 주지 않겠느냐?<end>"

## 890124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょうがない<end>"
EN: "Fine.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없지<end>"

## 890125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "関係ない<end>"
EN: "Not my problem.<end>"
KO: "상관없어<end>"

## 890126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "族長不在のとき、ラドラスが沈むのも<line-break>族長が巫女の血を引く<line-break>ラドラスの守だからさ。<end>"
EN: "Ladras sinks whenever the chieftain is away because the chieftain is the Guardian of Ladras, descended from a priestess's bloodline.<end>"
KO: "족장이 없을 때 라드라스가 가라앉는 것도 족장이 무녀의 피를 이은 라드라스의 수호자이기 때문이야.<end>"

## 890136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は…ああ…あ、あぁあ。<end>"
EN: "Hah... ah... a-ahhh...<end>"
KO: "하… 아아… 아, 아아아.<end>"

## 890137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、来ないで。<line-break>たぶん、ふぐ、あ、あぁああ、<line-break>だいじょ…ぶだから。<end>"
EN: "D-don't come near me. I think... ngh, a-ahhh... I'll be all right.<end>"
KO: "오, 오지 마. 아마, 후우, 아, 아아아, 괜찮…을 테니까.<end>"

## 890138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫よ。<line-break>もう、おさまったから…<end>"
EN: "I'm all right. It's passed now...<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 이제 진정됐으니까….<end>"

## 890139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私のこと、知っているの？<end>"
EN: "Do you know me?<end>"
KO: "나를 알고 있어?<end>"

## 890140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい<end>"
EN: "Yes<end>"
KO: "네<end>"

## 890141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ<end>"
EN: "No<end>"
KO: "아니요<end>"

## 890142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…<line-break>なんだか…<line-break>どうなってもいい気分だわ…<end>"
EN: "Oh... Somehow... I feel as though I don't care what happens to me...<end>"
KO: "그래… 왠지… 어떻게 돼도 상관없을 것 같은 기분이야….<end>"

## 890143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…<line-break>なんだか…<line-break>どうなってもいい気分だわ…<end>"
EN: "Oh... Somehow... I feel as though I don't care what happens to me...<end>"
KO: "그래… 왠지… 어떻게 돼도 상관없을 것 같은 기분이야….<end>"

## 890144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この歌？　忘れたわ…<line-break>誰かが、私が眠りに落ちるまで<line-break>ずっと歌ってくれた歌…<end>"
EN: "This song? I'd forgotten it... Someone sang it to me until I fell asleep...<end>"
KO: "이 노래? 잊었어… 누군가가 내가 잠들 때까지 계속 불러 준 노래….<end>"

## 890145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、歌がうまいのね。<line-break>それに覚えも早いわ。<end>"
EN: "Heh, you sing well. And you're a quick study.<end>"
KO: "후후, 노래를 잘하네. 게다가 배우는 것도 빨라.<end>"

## 890146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この歌を聞いてるととても落ち着くの。<line-break>フフ、こんな状態で部下の前には<line-break>出られないものね。<end>"
EN: "This song always puts me at ease. Heh, I couldn't let my subordinates see me like this.<end>"
KO: "이 노래를 듣고 있으면 정말 마음이 편안해져. 후후, 이런 상태로 부하들 앞에는 나갈 수 없으니까.<end>"

## 890147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう！　<value:$28><line-break>手紙が届いているぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>! There's a letter for you.<end>"
KO: "오! <value:$28>, 편지가 왔어.<end>"

## 890148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　<value:$28>殿<line-break>　　依頼いたしたき件あり。<line-break>　　至急、エンシャント政庁まで<line-break>　　お越しくださるよう。<line-break>　　　ザギヴ・ディンガル<end>"
EN: "To <value:$28>: I have a matter requiring your assistance. Please come to Ancient Government Hall at once. Zagiv Dyneskal<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 공께. 의뢰드리고 싶은 일이 있습니다. 지체 없이 엔샨트 정청으로 와 주십시오. 자기브 딘갈<end>"

## 890149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう！　<value:$28><line-break>手紙が届いているぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>! A letter came for you.<end>"
KO: "오! <value:$28>, 편지가 왔어.<end>"

## 890150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "               <value:$28>殿<line-break>                依頼いたしたき件あり。　　<line-break>                至急、エンシャント政庁まで<line-break>                お越しくださるよう。<line-break>                ザギヴ・ディンガル<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I have a matter requiring your assistance. Please come to Ancient Government Hall at once. Zagiv Dyneskal<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 공께. 의뢰드리고 싶은 일이 있습니다. 지체 없이 엔샨트 정청으로 와 주십시오. 자기브 딘갈<end>"

## 890151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様でありますか！<line-break>ザギヴ将軍よりお話は承っております！<line-break>どうぞ！　ご案内いたします！<end>"
EN: "Are you <value:$28>? General Zagiv told us to expect you! Right this way―I'll show you in!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이십니까! 자기브 장군님께 말씀 들었습니다! 이쪽으로 안내하겠습니다!<end>"

## 890152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様<line-break>ザギヴ将軍よりお話は承っております。<line-break>ご案内いたしますぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. General Zagiv told us to expect you. Allow me to show you in.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 자기브 장군께 말씀 들었습니다. 안내해 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 890153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…東方領土すべてを、ひとりの人間が<line-break>排他的に支配しているという事態は、<line-break>帝国にとって歓迎すべきものではない。<end>"
EN: "It is not in the Empire's interest to have the entire Eastern Territory under the exclusive control of a single person.<end>"
KO: "…동방 영토 전부를 한 인간이 배타적으로 지배하고 있는 상황은 제국으로서는 반길 일이 아니다.<end>"

## 890154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ、承知しております。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I understand.<end>"
KO: "예, 알고 있습니다.<end>"

## 890155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…特に、カルラのように、軍を<line-break>私物化する女の手に預けておくのはな。<end>"
EN: "Especially when that person is a woman like Karula, who treats the army as her private property.<end>"
KO: "…특히 칼라처럼 군을 사유물로 삼는 여자의 손에 맡겨 두는 건 말이지.<end>"

## 890156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご期待には必ずや沿えると存じます。<end>"
EN: "I shall not disappoint you.<end>"
KO: "기대에 반드시 부응하겠습니다.<end>"

## 890157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<end>"
EN: "Ah...<end>"
KO: "아…<end>"

## 890158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍。君だけが頼りだ。<end>"
EN: "General of the Black Tortoise. You are my only hope.<end>"
KO: "현무장군. 그대만 믿겠다.<end>"

## 890159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は…はい…<end>"
EN: "Y-yes...<end>"
KO: "네… 네…<end>"

## 890160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あなた、<line-break>いつからそこにいたの！？<end>"
EN: "Y-you! How long have you been standing there?!<end>"
KO: "다, 당신… 언제부터 거기 있었어!?<end>"

## 890161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なら、いいわ。<line-break>こっちに来て。<end>"
EN: "Then it's all right. Come here.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 됐어. 이쪽으로 와.<end>"

## 890162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は、どうしても<line-break>あなたにお礼が言いたかったの。<end>"
EN: "There was something I especially wanted to do today: thank you.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 꼭 네게 고맙다고 말하고 싶었어.<end>"

## 890163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがギルドで受けていた<line-break>モンスター退治の依頼の中には、<line-break>闇の怪物も含まれていたのよ。<line-break>私もずっと追っていたの。<line-break>一匹でも多くこの世から消すためにね。<end>"
EN: "Some of the monster-hunting jobs you took through the Guild involved creatures of darkness. I've been hunting them for a long time myself, trying to rid the world of as many as I can.<end>"
KO: "당신이 길드에서 받은 몬스터 퇴치 의뢰 중에는 어둠의 괴물도 포함되어 있었어. 나도 계속 쫓고 있었지. 한 마리라도 더 이 세상에서 없애려고.<end>"

## 890164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのおかげで、<line-break>ずいぶんと気分が楽になったわ。<line-break>ありがとう、殺してくれて。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, a great weight has been lifted from me. Thank you for killing them.<end>"
KO: "네 덕분에 훨씬 마음이 편해졌어. 고마워, 죽여 줘서.<end>"

## 890165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは私からの気持ちよ、<line-break>受け取っておいて。<end>"
EN: "This is a token of my gratitude. Please take it.<end>"
KO: "이건 내 마음이야. 받아 둬.<end>"

## 890166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうひとつ、あなたを見込んで<line-break>依頼したいことがあるんだけど、<line-break>引き受けていただけないかしら？<end>"
EN: "There's one more thing I'd like to entrust to you. Would you accept the assignment?<end>"
KO: "한 가지 더, 당신을 믿고 부탁하고 싶은 일이 있는데 맡아 주지 않겠어요?<end>"

## 890167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 890168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 890169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本日、本職は帝国東方領土の<line-break>巡察に出発します。<line-break>貴殿にその警護を依頼します。<end>"
EN: "Today I depart on an official inspection of the Empire's Eastern Territory. I request that you serve as my escort.<end>"
KO: "오늘 본관은 제국 동방 영토 순찰을 떠납니다. 귀하에게 그 호위를 의뢰합니다.<end>"

## 890170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けていただける、かしら？<end>"
EN: "Will you accept?<end>"
KO: "맡아 주실 수 있을까요?<end>"

## 890171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>それでは、状況を説明するわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Then I'll explain the situation.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 그럼 상황을 설명할게.<end>"

## 890172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラはロセンに利権を集中させ、<line-break>巨利を得、自軍を強化している。<line-break>…帝国にいつ牙を剥くかわからない。<line-break>だから、ロセンの領内視察を名目に<line-break>その辺を内密に調査するつもりなの。<line-break>カルラの野望をくじくためにね。<end>"
EN: "Karula has concentrated lucrative interests in Rocen under her control, reaping enormous profits and strengthening her army. We have no way of knowing when she might turn on the Empire. So I intend to use an inspection of Rocen as a pretext to investigate the matter discreetly and thwart Karula's ambitions.<end>"
KO: "카를라는 로센에 이권을 집중시켜 막대한 이익을 얻고 자기 군을 강화하고 있어. …언제 제국에 이를 드러낼지 몰라. 그래서 로센 영내 시찰을 명목으로 그 주변을 은밀히 조사할 생각이야. 카를라의 야망을 꺾기 위해서.<end>"

## 890173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その私の警護をカルラが担当するの。<line-break>カルラだってこっちの意図は覚ってる…<line-break>絶対に何かしかけてくるわ。<line-break>と言って、こちらから大々的な<line-break>警護部隊をしつらえるわけにも<line-break>いかないの。<end>"
EN: "Karula herself will be responsible for my protection. She must realize what we're planning, and she'll certainly try something. But I cannot arrive with a conspicuously large escort of my own.<end>"
KO: "그런 내 경호를 카를라가 맡아. 카를라도 우리 의도를 눈치챘을 거야… 분명 뭔가 꾸밀 거야. 그렇다고 이쪽에서 대규모 경호대를 꾸릴 수도 없어.<end>"

## 890174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、警護に不満があるのか、<line-break>と疑われでもすれば、それを<line-break>視察拒否の口実とされるかもしれない。<line-break>相手はあのカルラ。<line-break>少しでもぬかりがあれば、<line-break>たちまち足元をすくわれるわ。<end>"
EN: "If she suspected that I found her protection inadequate, for example, she might use that as grounds to refuse the inspection. This is Karula we're dealing with. One misstep, and she'll have us at her mercy.<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 경호에 불만이 있냐는 의심이라도 받으면 그걸 시찰 거부의 구실로 삼을지도 몰라. 상대는 그 카를라야. 조금이라도 빈틈이 있으면 금세 발밑을 파고들 거야.<end>"

## 890175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、あなたに私の警護を<line-break>お願いしたいの。<line-break>ごく私的な警護を、ね。<end>"
EN: "That is why I want you to guard me―as a strictly private escort.<end>"
KO: "그래서 당신에게 제 경호를 부탁하고 싶어요. 지극히 사적인 경호를요.<end>"

## 890176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、うれしいわ。<line-break>こっちの用意はできています。<line-break>すぐ出発しましょう。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm glad. Everything is ready on my end. Let's leave at once.<end>"
KO: "후후, 기쁘네. 이쪽 준비는 끝났어. 바로 출발하자.<end>"

## 890177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは帝国ではお尋ね者じゃ。<line-break>また、内務統括殿とはいろいろあった。<line-break>すまんが、わしは参加できん…<end>"
EN: "I'm wanted in the Empire, and I have a history with the Director of Internal Affairs. Sorry, but I can't take part...<end>"
KO: "나는 제국에서 수배 중인 몸이야. 게다가 내무총괄과도 이런저런 일이 있었지. 미안하지만 나는 참가할 수 없네….<end>"

## 890178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまんの、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "미안하네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らは反ディンガルの闘士！<line-break>ロセン王国復興を志す者だ！<end>"
EN: "We are fighters against Dyneskal! We seek the restoration of the Kingdom of Rocen!<end>"
KO: "우리는 반 딘갈의 투사! 로센 왕국의 부흥을 뜻하는 자들이다!<end>"

## 890180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの侵略者！<line-break>その巨魁ザギヴ・ディンガル！<end>"
EN: "Dyneskal invader! Zagiv Dyneskal, ringleader of them all!<end>"
KO: "딘갈의 침략자! 그 우두머리 자기브 딘갈!<end>"

## 890181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日がその命運のつきる時だ！<line-break>死をもって侵略と非道の罪をあがなえ！<end>"
EN: "Today your fate is sealed! Pay for your invasion and your atrocities with your life!<end>"
KO: "오늘이 그 운명이 다하는 날이다! 죽음으로 침략과 만행의 죄를 갚아라!<end>"

## 890182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>？<line-break>貴様、<value:$28>ではないか！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>? You... You're <value:$28>, aren't you?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>? 네놈, <value:$28> 아니냐!<end>"

## 890183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、どういうつもりだ。<line-break>ディンガルに寝返ったのか！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, what are you doing? Have you gone over to Dyneskal?!<end>"
KO: "큭, 무슨 속셈이지. 딘갈로 돌아선 건가!<end>"

## 890184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ややこしい話が出てきたんで<line-break>確認させてもらうけど、<line-break>あなた、私を守ってくれるのよね？<end>"
EN: "This has become rather complicated, so let me make sure of one thing: you are still going to protect me, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "복잡한 이야기가 나와서 확인하는 건데, 너 나를 지켜 줄 거지?<end>"

## 890185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "了解したわ。今の会話は、<line-break>カルラの演出の可能性もあるから、<line-break>私の記憶から抹消することにします。<end>"
EN: "Understood. That exchange may have been staged by Karula, so I shall erase it from my memory.<end>"
KO: "알겠어. 방금 대화는 카를라가 꾸민 연출일 가능성도 있으니 내 기억에서 지워 버리겠어.<end>"

## 890186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、<value:$28>、<line-break>行くわ！<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. We're leaving!<end>"
KO: "자, <value:$28>, 간다!<end>"

## 890187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶん簡単にかたがついたわね。 <line-break>カルラがこんなにマヌケだとは<line-break>思えないのだけど…。<end>"
EN: "That was settled remarkably easily. I can't believe Karula would be this foolish...<end>"
KO: "꽤 간단히 끝났네. 카를라가 이렇게 얼빠졌다고는 생각하기 어려운데….<end>"

## 890188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らを…侮辱するな…。<line-break>我らはカルラの手先などでは…<line-break>ない…。<end>"
EN: "Do not... insult us... We are not... Karula's lackeys...<end>"
KO: "우리를… 모욕하지 마라… 우리는 칼라의 앞잡이 따위가… 아니다…<end>"

## 890189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。正確には、<line-break>カルラに利用されていただけね。<end>"
EN: "True. To be precise, she was merely using you.<end>"
KO: "그래. 정확히는 칼라에게 이용당했을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 890190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…、ま、ま…だ言う…か…。<end>"
EN: "D-damn you... You're still... saying that...?<end>"
KO: "큭… 아, 아직도… 말하나….<end>"

## 890191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ククク…<line-break>リベルダムのアンティノ商会で<line-break>新開発された戦闘モンスターだ。　<line-break>こいつは打撃も魔法も吸収する…<line-break>お前でも、か、勝てん…ぞ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... It's a newly developed combat monster from the Antino Trading Company in Liberdam. It absorbs both physical blows and magic... Even you c-can't defeat it...<end>"
KO: "크크크… 리벨덤의 안티노 상회에서 새로 개발한 전투 몬스터다. 이 녀석은 타격도 마법도 흡수한다…. 네놈이라도 이, 이길 수… 없어….<end>"

## 890192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

Take her all the way back to the inn in Ancient and nurse her? Why should a noble Elf such as myself have to do any such thing?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Yeah. I'm worried about Big Sis Zagiv. If you intend to return to the inn in Ancient, you had best make haste.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Aye. This is no time to concern ourselves with that. Let us return to Ancient for now.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27It's not as though I have a weakness for stern women.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22Then we'll work out what comes next at the inn. So, retreeeat!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Understood. Let us take her back to Ancient.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193U-understood. I'm sorry. I was the one who lost composure... Zagiv needs treatment first. We must hurry back to Ancient...<end><if><value:$01><equal>194No, pay no heed to what I said. This is her fate. In any event, the inspection is no longer possible. Let us withdraw to Ancient.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I hope my intuition is wrong. If it isn't, then she's in very grave danger. We should get somewhere safe... Yes, I think we should return to Ancient.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190What I meant doesn't matter. Whatever happens, you'll take the burden on yourself anyway. You're that sort too, aren't you? For now, we're going back to Ancient so we can tend to her.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4그래. 자기브 씨를 데리고 엔샨트까지 돌아가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5…알았어, 이 녀석을 돌보자는 거지. 난 얘가 마음에 안 들지만 네가 그렇다면 엔샨트 여관까지 데려다줄게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6응, 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아가자. 지금 카를라 품으로 들어가는 건 죽으러 가는 거나 같아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13이 여자를 돌보려고 엔샨트로 돌아가겠다는 건가? 마음대로 해라. 너나 로이라면 할 법한 말이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그래. 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아가 자기브를 돌봐야겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…그래. 지금 이 상태라면 카를라 먹잇감이야. <value:$28> 말에 찬성해. 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아가 태세를 정비하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐라고? 엔샨트 여관까지 데려가 돌보겠다고? 고귀한 엘프인 아타쿠시가 왜 그런 걸 해야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19응. 자기브 언니가 걱정돼. <value:$28> 말대로 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…좋을 대로 해라. 네 여행이다. 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아갈 거라면 빠를수록 좋겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26그래. 지금은 그런 일에 신경 쓸 때가 아니군. 일단 엔샨트로 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…별로 나는 강한 느낌의 여자에게 약한 게 아니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22그럼 대책은 여관에서 생각하자. 철수~!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192알겠습니다. 그녀를 데리고 엔샨트로 돌아가죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193아, 알겠습니다. 죄송해요. 제가 더 동요했네요…. 우선 자기브 씨를 치료해야죠. 엔샨트까지 빨리 돌아가요….<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…아니, 내 말은 신경 쓰지 마. 이건 그녀의 운명이야. 어쨌든 시찰은 무리겠지. 엔샨트까지 철수하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…내 감이 틀렸으면 좋겠지만 아니면 그녀는 아주 위험한 상태야. 일단 안전한 엔샨트까지 돌아가는 게 좋겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190내 말의 의미는 됐어. 무슨 일이 생겨도 결국 짊어질 녀석이잖아, 너도? 어쨌든 그녀를 돌보려면 일단 엔샨트로 돌아가자.<end>"

## 890203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>4ザギヴはショック状態みたいだわ。<line-break>どうしよっかな…<end><if><value:$01><equal>5ちっ、使えない奴だな。<line-break>いきなりショック状態だなんて…<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…ショック状態だな。<line-break>…危険な状態だ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14どうしたことじゃ。<line-break>あのスライムがでてきよったら、<line-break>いきなりこの有様じゃ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17なぁーに？　<line-break><end>あきれちゃうわ！　<line-break>信じられないわ！！<line-break>あのスライムが出てきたら<line-break>いきなり錯乱状態？<line-break>これだから下等生物はっ！<end>………………<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Zagiv seems to be in shock. What should I do...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Tch. Useless. Going into shock all of a sudden...<end><if><value:$01><equal>13She's in shock. This is serious.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What happened? That Slime appeared, and suddenly she ended up like this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Whaaat?<end>How appalling! I can't believe it! That Slime shows up and she suddenly becomes delirious? That's the trouble with lower life-forms!<end>...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4자기브가 쇼크 상태인 것 같아. 어떡하지…<end><if><value:$01><equal>5쳇, 쓸모없는 녀석이군. 갑자기 쇼크 상태라니…<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…쇼크 상태군. …위험한 상태다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14무슨 일인 게야. 저 슬라임이 나오자마자 갑자기 이 꼴이 되다니.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐어야?<end>기가 막혀! 믿을 수가 없네!! 저 슬라임이 나오자마자 갑자기 착란 상태라고? 이래서 하등생물이란!<end>………………<end>"

## 890204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>4そうね。ザギヴさんを連れて、<line-break>エンシャントまで引き上げましょ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5…わかったよ、<line-break>こいつを介抱するんだろ。<line-break>ただ、俺、こいつが気に入らねえんだ。<line-break>だけど、お前がそう言うんなら、<line-break>エンシャントの宿屋まで<line-break>こいつを運んでってやるよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13この女を介抱するために、<line-break>エンシャントへ戻ろうというのか？<line-break> <line-break>勝手にするがいい。<line-break>おまえやロイが言い出しそうなことだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14そうじゃな。<line-break>エンシャントの宿屋にでも戻って<line-break>ザギヴを介抱せにゃあな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17え、えぇーっ？<line-break>エンシャントの宿屋まで<line-break>連れ帰って介抱するですって？<line-break>高貴なエルフのアタクシが<line-break>どうしてそんなことしなきゃ<line-break>いけないわけ！<end>…好きにしろ。うぬの旅だ。<line-break>エンシャントの宿屋に戻るならば<line-break>早い方がいいだろう。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Right. Let's take Zagiv with us and withdraw to Ancient!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5All right, I get it. We have to look after her. I don't like her, though. But if that's what you want, I'll carry her back to the inn in Ancient.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You would have us return to Ancient to tend to this woman? Do as you please. It is just the sort of thing you or Roy would suggest.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Aye. We'd best return to the inn in Ancient and tend to Zagiv.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17W-what?! You expect me to take her all the way back to the inn in Ancient and nurse her? Why should a noble Elf such as myself have to do anything of the sort?!<end>Do as you please. It is your journey. If you mean to return to the inn in Ancient, you would do well to hurry.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4그래. 자기브 씨를 데리고 엔샨트까지 돌아가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5…알았어. 이 녀석을 간호하자는 거지. 다만 난 얘가 마음에 안 들어. 그래도 네가 그렇게 말한다면 엔샨트 여관까지 데려다주지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13이 여자를 간호하려고 엔샨트로 돌아가겠다고? 마음대로 해라. 너나 로이라면 그럴 줄 알았다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그렇군. 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아가 자기브를 간호해야겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐라고요? 엔샨트 여관까지 데려가서 간호한다고요? 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 왜 그런 일을 해야 하죠!<end>…좋을 대로 해라. 네 여행이다. 엔샨트 여관으로 돌아갈 거라면 빠를수록 좋겠지.<end>"

## 890205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、どうしよう。<line-break>ザギヴはショック状態みたいだ。<line-break>　<line-break>…ここに、置いてっちゃおうか？<end>"
EN: "Sis, what do we do? Zagiv looks like she's in shock. Should we... leave her here?<end>"
KO: "누나, 어떡하지. 자기브는 충격에 빠진 것 같아. …여기 두고 갈까?<end>"

## 890206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いってえ！　冗談だよ、冗談！<line-break>緊張をほぐそうってしただけだろ！<line-break>　<line-break>もう、すぐ手ぇ出すんだからな。<line-break>俺の頭がこれ以上悪くなったら、<line-break>姉ちゃんのせいだぜ！<end>"
EN: "Ow! I was joking! Just trying to ease the tension! You always hit first. If I get any stupider, it'll be your fault, Sis!<end>"
KO: "아야! 농담이야, 농담! 긴장을 풀어 주려던 것뿐이잖아! 정말 손이 먼저 나간다니까. 내 머리가 더 나빠지면 누나 탓이야!<end>"

## 890207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャカは少し頭が悪くなった。<line-break>チャカのＩＮＴが１下がった。<end>"

KO: "차카는 조금 머리가 나빠졌다. 차카의 INT가 1 내려갔다.<end>"

## 890208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャカは少し打たれ強くなった。<line-break>チャカのＶＩＴが１上がった。<end>"
EN: "Chaka got a little tougher. Chaka's ＶＩＴ rose by 1.<end>"
KO: "차카는 조금 더 맷집이 좋아졌다. 차카의 VIT가 1 올랐다.<end>"

## 890209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ、わかったよ。<line-break>ザギヴをエンシャントまで連れ帰って<line-break>宿屋で介抱しよう。<end>"
EN: "Fine, fine. We'll take Zagiv back to Ancient and nurse her at the inn.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 알았어. 자기브를 엔샨트까지 데려가서 여관에서 간호하자.<end>"

## 890210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>多分、もう、大丈…夫…だから…<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I think I'll be all ri...ght now...<end>"
KO: "고마워. 아마 이제 괜…찮을… 거야….<end>"

## 890211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>あんなことになって…しまって…<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... for what happened...<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 그런 일이… 벌어져 버려서….<end>"

## 890212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのときの…記憶が…<line-break>甦って…<end>"
EN: "The memories... of that day... came back...<end>"
KO: "그때의… 기억이… 되살아나서…<end>"

## 890213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妖術宰相ゾフォルは予言したわ…。<line-break>ザギヴがディンガルを支配する、<line-break>って…<line-break>それで、私の家族は魔王バロルに<line-break>捕らえられて…ウルグの円卓騎士、<line-break>棲みつくものマゴスの餌食に…<end>"
EN: "Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, made a prophecy... that Zagiv would rule Dyneskal. Because of it, my family was captured by Balor, Demon King... and fed to Magos the Indweller, one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table...<end>"
KO: "요술 재상 조포르는 예언했어…. 자기브가 딘갈을 지배한다고…. 그래서 내 가족은 마왕 발로르에게 붙잡혀… 울그의 원탁기사, 깃든 자 마고스의 먹이가….<end>"

## 890214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスは昼のあの怪物兵器によく似た<line-break>外見をしていたの…。そして…<line-break>やはり魔法も…打撃も…効かなかった。<end>"
EN: "Magos looked much like that monstrous weapon we saw today... And just as before, neither magic nor physical blows had any effect.<end>"
KO: "마고스는 낮에 본 그 괴물병기와 아주 비슷한 모습이었어…. 그리고… 역시 마법도… 타격도… 통하지 않았어.<end>"

## 890215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の…目の前で…家族が…私の家族が…<line-break>な、なぶり…殺されて…いった。<line-break>　<line-break>私が…ザギヴという名前…だった…<line-break>ばっかりに…<end>"
EN: "Before my very eyes... my family... My family was tortured to death... all because my name was Zagiv...<end>"
KO: "내… 눈앞에서… 가족이… 내 가족이… 괴롭힘당하며 죽어 갔어. 내가… 자기브라는 이름이었던 것 때문에….<end>"

## 890216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのとき…、<line-break>ネメア様が助けに来て…くださって…、<line-break>私だけ…助かった…<end>"
EN: "Then Lord Nemea came to save us... and I alone survived...<end>"
KO: "그때… 네메아 님이 구하러 와… 주셔서… 나만… 살았어….<end>"

## 890217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…だけ…<end>"
EN: "Only me...<end>"
KO: "나…만…<end>"

## 890218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…だけ…。私のせいなのに…<line-break>私…だけ…むざむざと…<line-break>生き残ってしまった！<line-break>私、生まれなければ…よかった…！<end>"
EN: "Only me... Even though it was my fault, I alone survived... I alone came through alive! I wish I'd never been born!<end>"
KO: "나…만…. 내 탓인데… 나만… 뻔뻔하게… 살아남아 버렸어! 나, 태어나지 않았으면… 좋았을 텐데…!<end>"

## 890219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "近づかないで！<end>"
EN: "Stay away from me!<end>"
KO: "가까이 오지 마!<end>"

## 890220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスは、その時、私の中に潜んだの。<line-break>それ以来、マゴスは私の中に…！<line-break>私は汚れているの！<end>"
EN: "Magos hid inside me that day. Ever since then, Magos has been within me... I'm tainted!<end>"
KO: "마고스는 그때 내 안에 숨어들었어. 그 뒤로 마고스는 내 안에…! 나는 더럽혀졌어!<end>"

## 890221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私なんて…ザギヴなんて、<line-break>いてはいけないの！<line-break>いてはいけなかったのよ！<line-break>生きとし生けるものすべてが<line-break>呪わしい！　恨めしい！<end>"
EN: "Someone like me... Zagiv should never exist! I should never have existed! Every living thing is hateful to me! I resent them all!<end>"
KO: "나 같은 건… 자기브 같은 건 존재하면 안 돼! 존재하지 말았어야 했어! 살아 있는 모든 것이 저주스러워! 원망스러워!<end>"

## 890222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって私は、生きていては<line-break>いけない存在なのだもの！<end>"
EN: "Because I am something that must not be allowed to live!<end>"
KO: "왜냐하면 나는 살아 있어서는 안 되는 존재니까!<end>"

## 890223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃ、生きていてはいけない…<line-break>お前はそう世界に否定されたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. You must not live... That is how the world rejected you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 살아 있어선 안 된다… 세상이 너를 그렇게 부정한 것이다.<end>"

## 890224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "システィーナの伝道師のひとり、<line-break>妖術宰相ゾフォル。<line-break>闇の誕生にあわせ、参上した。<end>"
EN: "I am Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, one of Sistina's missionaries. I have come to attend the birth of darkness.<end>"
KO: "시스티나의 전도사 중 하나, 요술재상 조포르. 어둠의 탄생에 맞춰 등장했다.<end>"

## 890225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分と少しでも異質なものには、<line-break>恐怖と拒絶を抱かずにおられぬ。<line-break>それが人の性じゃ。<line-break>さぞお前は疎外感にさいなまれた<line-break>はずじゃの。<line-break>…かわいそうに。<end>"
EN: "People cannot help but fear and reject anything even slightly different from themselves. Such is human nature. You must have suffered terribly from feeling so alienated... Poor child.<end>"
KO: "자신과 조금이라도 다른 존재에게는 두려움과 거부감을 품지 않을 수 없다. 그것이 인간의 본성이니라. 너도 분명 심한 소외감에 시달렸겠구나. …가엾게도.<end>"

## 890226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…い、痛い。<end>"
EN: "I-it hurts...<end>"
KO: "…아, 아파.<end>"

## 890227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かわいそうにな。<line-break>お前は何も悪いことはしておらぬ。<line-break>　<line-break>お前が存在すること自体が悪じゃと、<line-break>この世界に否定されただけじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Poor child. You have done nothing wrong. The world merely rejected you, declaring your very existence an evil.<end>"
KO: "가엾구나. 너는 아무 잘못도 하지 않았다. 네가 존재하는 것 자체가 악이라고 이 세계가 부정했을 뿐이다.<end>"

## 890228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛い、痛いよ…<end>"
EN: "It hurts... It hurts...<end>"
KO: "아파, 아프다고…<end>"

## 890229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、お前の痛みを解放するがいい。<line-break>世界がお前に産みつけた痛みを、<line-break>この世界にわからせてやるのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Come. Set your pain free. Make this world understand the pain it forced upon you at birth.<end>"
KO: "자, 네 고통을 풀어놓거라. 세상이 네게 안긴 고통을 이 세계에 똑똑히 알려 주는 게다.<end>"

## 890230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふ、うう…、ア…、アアア！<end>"
EN: "Nngh... Ah... Aaaah!<end>"
KO: "으, 으으…, 아…, 아아아!<end>"

## 890231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このままではお前は無だ。<line-break>お前の胸の内の怪物を孵してやれ。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、力を手に入れろ。<line-break>皇帝になれ。<line-break>さすれば誰もお前を否定できぬ！<end>"
EN: "As you are now, you are nothing. Hatch the monster within your breast. Take its power. Become Emperor. Then no one will ever deny you again!<end>"
KO: "이대로면 넌 무다. 네 가슴속의 괴물을 부화시켜라. 그리고 힘을 얻어라. 황제가 되어라. 그러면 누구도 너를 부정하지 못한다!<end>"

## 890232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウウッ、ウグッ、ウガアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Ggh... GRAAAAAH!<end>"
KO: "우윽, 우욱, 우가아아앗!<end>"

## 890233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士マゴスが孵化する。<line-break>お主ひとりでは奴には勝てぬぞ。<line-break>逃げぬのか？<end>"
EN: "Magos, Knight of the Round Table, is about to hatch. You cannot defeat Magos alone. Will you not flee?<end>"
KO: "원탁기사 마고스가 부화한다. 너 혼자서는 놈을 이길 수 없어. 도망치지 않을 텐가?<end>"

## 890234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔、助けられた恩義もある。<line-break>今なら逃げても追わぬぞ。<end>"
EN: "I owe you for helping me long ago. Flee now, and I will not pursue you.<end>"
KO: "예전에 도움받은 은혜도 있다. 지금 도망친다면 쫓지 않겠다.<end>"

## 890235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げる<end>"
EN: "Flee<end>"
KO: "도망친다<end>"

## 890236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げない<end>"
EN: "Don't flee<end>"
KO: "도망치지 않아<end>"

## 890237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぅ…ぐぅっ…<line-break>…<value:$28>…？<end>"
EN: "Nngh... <value:$28>...?<end>"
KO: "으윽… 크윽… …<value:$28>…?<end>"

## 890238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げおったわ。フフ、あきらめろ。<line-break>こうなることは<line-break>わかっておったはずじゃ。<line-break>さぁ、絶望に身をゆだねよ。<line-break>お前の中で育てられた<line-break>かわいい悪魔をかえすのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "So your companion fled. Heh heh... Give up. You knew this would happen. Yield yourself to despair. Hatch the precious little demon nurtured inside you.<end>"
KO: "도망쳤군. 후후, 포기해라. 이렇게 될 줄 알고 있었을 텐데. 자, 절망에 몸을 맡겨라. 네 안에서 자라난 귀여운 악마를 돌려보내는 거다.<end>"

## 890239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はあっ！　苦しい、よ…<line-break>あぁ…はあああっ！<end>"
EN: "Ah! It hurts... Aah... Aaaaah!<end>"
KO: "하악! 괴로워… 아아… 하아아악!<end>"

## 890240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんじゃ？<end>"
EN: "Wh-what is this?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐냐?<end>"

## 890241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この…歌…<end>"
EN: "This... song...<end>"
KO: "이…노래…<end>"

## 890242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶望を、押し返しおったか…<end>"
EN: "You pushed back the despair...<end>"
KO: "절망을 밀어냈단 말인가…<end>"

## 890244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は皇帝にならない。<line-break>皇帝になっても否定されないのは、<line-break>皇帝という地位だけ。…私じゃない。<end>"
EN: "I will not become Emperor. If I did, it would only be the title of Emperor that no one could deny―not me.<end>"
KO: "나는 황제가 되지 않아. 황제가 되어도 부정당하지 않는 건 황제라는 지위뿐이야. …내가 아니야.<end>"

## 890245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フォフォ…。<line-break>いくら強がっても運命には勝てぬよ。<line-break>どれ、お前の未来を語ってやろうか。<end>"
EN: "Ho ho... Brave words cannot overcome fate. Shall I tell you what your future holds?<end>"
KO: "호호…. 아무리 허세를 부려도 운명은 이길 수 없어. 어디, 네 미래를 이야기해 줄까.<end>"

## 890246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は<value:$28>と<line-break>猫屋敷へ行く。<line-break>　<line-break>あそこの結界に守られていれば<line-break>マゴスの復活を遅らせられるからな。<end>"
EN: "You will go with <value:$28> to Cat Manor. Its barrier can delay Magos's return.<end>"
KO: "넌 <value:$28>과 고양이 저택으로 가라. 그곳의 결계 안에 있으면 마고스의 부활을 늦출 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 890248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴさんですが、<line-break>とりあえず眠ってもらいました。<end>"
EN: "As for Zagiv, I have put her to sleep for the time being.<end>"
KO: "자기브 씨는 일단 잠들게 했습니다.<end>"

## 890249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルもやりますね。<line-break>彼女が心を閉ざしてしまうように、<line-break>うまいこと持っていっている…<end>"
EN: "Zofor is quite capable, skillfully maneuvering Zagiv into closing off her heart...<end>"
KO: "조포르도 제법이군요. 그녀가 마음을 닫아 버리도록 아주 교묘하게 몰고 가고 있어요….<end>"

## 890250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴさんが自ら心を開き、<line-break>運命に立ち向かう気にならないかぎり<line-break>手の施しようはありませんね。<end>"
EN: "There is nothing we can do unless Zagiv opens her heart of her own accord and resolves to face her fate.<end>"
KO: "자기브 씨가 스스로 마음을 열고 운명에 맞설 생각이 들지 않는 한 손쓸 방법이 없군요.<end>"

## 890251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>彼女を旅に連れていってくれませんか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, would you take her with you on your journey?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그녀를 여행에 데려가 주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 890252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結界を張って浄化している<line-break>猫屋敷でも危険な状態なのに、<line-break>外に出るなんて…無茶よ！<end>"
EN: "She's in danger even here at Cat Manor, within a purifying barrier. Taking her outside would be madness!<end>"
KO: "결계를 치고 정화하고 있는 고양이 저택에서도 위험한 상태인데 밖으로 나가다니… 무모해!<end>"

## 890253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>人とは、どんな時であっても、<line-break>挑み続けなくてはならない<line-break>ものなのですよ。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia. No matter the circumstances, a person must continue to meet every challenge.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아. 사람은 어떤 때라도 계속 도전해야 하는 법이랍니다.<end>"

## 890254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "挑むのをやめる時、それがすなわち、<line-break>生きるのをやめる時なのです。<end>"
EN: "To stop meeting those challenges is to stop living.<end>"
KO: "도전을 멈출 때, 바로 그때가 살아가기를 멈출 때입니다.<end>"

## 890255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスが糧としている<line-break>ザギヴさんの魔力を弱め、<line-break>復活を遅らせています。<line-break>彼女自身の負担もあります。<line-break>とても本来の実力は出せないでしょう。<line-break>頼みますよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We are weakening Zagiv's magic, which Magos feeds upon, and delaying the revival. This also places a burden on Zagiv herself, so she will be unable to fight at anything near her full strength. I am counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "마고스가 양식으로 삼는 자기브 씨의 마력을 약화시켜 부활을 늦추고 있습니다. 그녀 자신에게도 부담이 큽니다. 본래의 실력을 발휘하기는 어렵겠죠. 부탁합니다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>あなたまで、そんな！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Not you too!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 당신까지 그러다니!<end>"

## 890257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあまあ…<line-break><value:$28>を<line-break>信じましょう、ケリュネイア。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>ザギヴさんを呼び出したいときは<line-break>他の人と同じく転送機ですからね。<end>"
EN: "Now, now... Let us put our faith in <value:$28>, Ceryneia. <value:$28>, when you wish to call Zagiv, use the teleporter just as you would for anyone else.<end>"
KO: "자자… <value:$28>을 믿어 봅시다, 케류네이아. <value:$28>, 자기브 씨를 불러내고 싶을 때는 다른 사람들과 마찬가지로 전송기를 쓰면 돼요.<end>"

## 890258

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴさんですが、<line-break>とりあえず眠ってもらいました。<end>"

KO: "자기브 씨는 일단 잠들게 했습니다.<end>"

## 890261
