## 890262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理という言葉は可能性を<line-break>檻に閉じこめてしまいますよ。<line-break>まあ、聞いてください。<end>"
EN: "The word “impossible” locks possibility inside a cage. Please, hear me out.<end>"
KO: "무리라는 말은 가능성을 우리에 가둬 버립니다. 일단 들어 보세요.<end>"

## 890263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスが糧としている<line-break>ザギヴさんの魔力を弱め、<line-break>復活を遅らせています。<line-break>彼女自身の負担もあります。<line-break>とても本来の実力は出せないでしょう。<line-break>頼みますよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We are weakening Zagiv's magic, which Magos feeds upon, and delaying the revival. This also places a burden on Zagiv herself, so she will be unable to fight at anything near her full strength. I am counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "마고스가 양식으로 삼는 자기브 씨의 마력을 약화시켜 부활을 늦추고 있습니다. 그녀 자신에게도 부담이 큽니다. 본래의 실력을 발휘하기는 어렵겠죠. 부탁합니다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは<value:$28>、<line-break>ザギヴさんのこと、頼みますよ。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>ザギヴさんを呼び出したいときは<line-break>他の人と同じく転送機ですからね。<end>"
EN: "Then I leave Zagiv in your care, <value:$28>. <value:$28>, when you wish to call her, use the teleporter just as you would for anyone else.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>, 자기브 씨를 부탁합니다. <value:$28>, 자기브 씨를 부르고 싶을 때도 다른 사람처럼 전송기를 쓰면 됩니다.<end>"

## 890265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、君か。<line-break>こんな時に私を訪ねてくるとは<line-break>君もほとほと運がないな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you. Coming to see me at a time like this―you really have the worst luck.<end>"
KO: "오, 자네인가. 이런 때에 나를 찾아오다니 자네도 참 운이 없군.<end>"

## 890266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐにわかる。<line-break>ケガをしたくなければ<line-break>じっとしていることだ。<end>"
EN: "You'll understand soon enough. If you don't want to get hurt, stay right where you are.<end>"
KO: "곧 알게 될 거다. 다치고 싶지 않으면 가만히 있어.<end>"

## 890267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓の騎士ら魔人や、邪竜、破壊神…<line-break>こういった難物を蘇らせるには、<line-break>莫大な魔道エネルギーが要る。<end>"
EN: "Demons such as the Knights of the Round Table, the Evil Dragons, the God of Destruction... Reviving such formidable beings requires an enormous amount of arcane energy.<end>"
KO: "원탁의 기사 같은 마인이나 사룡, 파괴신… 이런 골치 아픈 존재들을 되살리려면 막대한 마도 에너지가 필요하다.<end>"

## 890268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、人間のソウルを吸い取り、<line-break>そこからそれに足るだけのエネルギーを<line-break>抽出する技というものが存在するのだ。<line-break>その禁忌なる技の名、<line-break>すなわち…ソウルリープ。<end>"
EN: "But there is a technique that drains the Souls of humans and extracts enough energy from them for precisely that purpose. The name of this forbidden art is... Soul Leap.<end>"
KO: "하지만 인간의 소울을 빨아들이고 거기서 필요한 만큼의 에너지를 추출하는 기술이 존재한다. 그 금단의 기술의 이름, 바로… 소울 리프.<end>"

## 890269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっとも…<line-break>この私がいる限り、<line-break>そのような真似はできんがな。<end>"
EN: "Of course... as long as I am here, no one can pull off such a thing.<end>"
KO: "물론… 내가 있는 한 그런 짓은 못 하겠지만.<end>"

## 890270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意見があるのなら、直接言うがいい。<line-break>これを引き起こした張本人が、<line-break>おいでになったようだからな…。<end>"
EN: "If you have something to say, say it directly. It seems the one responsible for all this has arrived...<end>"
KO: "할 말이 있다면 직접 말해라. 이 일을 일으킨 장본인이 오신 모양이니….<end>"

## 890271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "肉体の父、ウリア・ベルライン。<line-break>魂の父、あるいは魔道の塔のウジ虫、<line-break>シャローム。<line-break>そして義理の父、魔王バロル…。<end>"
EN: "My father of the flesh, Uria Berline. My father of the soul―or the maggot of the Arcane Tower―Shalom. And my father by marriage, Balor, Demon King...<end>"
KO: "육체의 아버지, 울리아 벨라인. 영혼의 아버지, 혹은 마도의 탑의 구더기 샬롬. 그리고 의붓아버지, 마왕 발로르….<end>"

## 890272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には父親が多いが、彼もそのひとりだ。<line-break>私の養い親にして、<line-break>私の出生を画策した男…<end>"
EN: "I have many fathers, and he is one of them: the man who raised me and contrived the circumstances of my birth...<end>"
KO: "내겐 아버지가 많지만 그도 그중 한 명이다. 나를 길러 준 부모이자 내 탄생을 꾸민 남자….<end>"

## 890273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "システィーナの伝道師のひとり、<line-break>ゾフォルという老いぼれじゃ。<line-break>昔は妖術宰相とも呼ばれておった。<end>"
EN: "An old fool named Zofor, one of Sistina's missionaries. Once, I was also known as the Sorcerous Chancellor.<end>"
KO: "시스티나의 전도사 중 하나인 조포르라는 늙은이야. 옛날엔 요술 재상이라고도 불렸지.<end>"

## 890274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや…<line-break>そこにおるのは<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主か…<line-break>なるほど、なるほど。<line-break>わしにも未来が読めぬワケじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Well now... Is that the bearer of the Infinite Soul? I see, I see. No wonder even I could not read the future.<end>"
KO: "오호… 거기 있는 건 무한의 소울을 지닌 자인가…. 그렇군, 그렇군. 나조차 미래를 읽을 수 없는 이유가 있었구나.<end>"

## 890275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わざわざ出てきてもらって<line-break>ご苦労だったが、<line-break>さっそく消えてもらおう。<end>"
EN: "I appreciate you coming all this way, but now I'll have you disappear.<end>"
KO: "일부러 나와 주느라 수고했지만, 당장 사라져 줘야겠어.<end>"

## 890276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その自信が命取りじゃ。<line-break>この妖術宰相の力、<line-break>目に焼き付けるがよい！<end>"
EN: "That confidence will be your undoing. Behold the power of the Sorcerous Chancellor―and burn it into your memory!<end>"
KO: "그 자신감이 목숨을 앗아갈 것이다. 이 요술재상의 힘을 눈에 새겨 두거라!<end>"

## 890277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さすがじゃな。<line-break>その身に秘めし闇の力。<line-break>わしの予想を上回っておったわ。<end>"
EN: "Impressive. The dark power hidden within you surpassed even my expectations.<end>"
KO: "…역시 대단하군. 그 몸에 숨겨진 어둠의 힘. 내 예상 이상이었다.<end>"

## 890278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…終わりだ。<end>"
EN: "It's over.<end>"
KO: "…끝이다.<end>"

## 890279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それはどうかな？<end>"
EN: "Is it, now?<end>"
KO: "…글쎄, 어떨까?<end>"

## 890281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今じゃ！　やれ！<end>"
EN: "Now! Do it!<end>"
KO: "…지금이다! 해라!<end>"

## 890282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐあっ！<end>"
EN: "Gah!<end>"
KO: "크악!<end>"

## 890283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。驚いたな。<line-break>次元の裂け目を阻み、さらに<line-break>ソウルリープまで阻むとは…。<line-break>まあ、さすがに無事ってわけには<line-break>いかなかったようだが…。<end>"
EN: "Well, that is a surprise. You stopped the dimensional rift and even blocked Soul Leap... Though it appears you did not come through unscathed.<end>"
KO: "호오. 놀랍군. 차원의 틈을 막고 소울 리프까지 저지하다니…. 뭐, 역시 무사하진 못한 모양이지만….<end>"

## 890284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、貴様は…<end>"
EN: "Y-you...<end>"
KO: "네, 네놈은…<end>"

## 890285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はシスティーナの伝道師、<line-break>黒の祈りのジュサプブロス。<line-break>ゾフォル爺さんのお仲間さ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Jusabros of the Black Prayer, a missionary of Sistina. An associate of old Zofor's.<end>"
KO: "나는 시스티나의 전도사, 검은 기도의 주사프브로스. 조포르 영감의 동료야.<end>"

## 890286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言ったじゃろう？<line-break>その自信が命取りじゃと。<end>"
EN: "Did I not warn you? That confidence would be your undoing.<end>"
KO: "말했지? 그 자신감이 목숨을 앗아갈 거라고.<end>"

## 890287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言ったじゃろう？<line-break>その自信が命取りじゃと。<end>"
EN: "Did I not warn you? That confidence would be your undoing.<end>"
KO: "말했지? 그 자신감이 목숨을 앗아갈 거라고.<end>"

## 890288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今じゃ！　やれ！<end>"
EN: "…Now! Do it!<end>"
KO: "…지금이다! 해라!<end>"

## 890289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐあっ！<end>"
EN: "Gah!<end>"
KO: "크악!<end>"

## 890290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう…まだ耐えられるとはの…。<line-break>だが、帝都の人間のソウルは、<line-break>ウルグ様にささげられた。<end>"
EN: "Well... I'm surprised you can still hold out. But the Souls of the people in the imperial capital have been offered to Lord Ulugh.<end>"
KO: "호오… 아직 견딜 수 있다니…. 하지만 제도 사람들의 소울은 울그 님께 바쳐졌다.<end>"

## 890291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうはいかん。<line-break>人類の革新を…<line-break> <line-break>貴様らごときに阻まれるわけには<line-break>いかんのだ！<end>"
EN: "No... I cannot allow Humanity's Evolution to be thwarted by the likes of you!<end>"
KO: "…그럴 수는 없다. 인류의 혁신을… 너희 따위에게 막히게 둘 수는 없어!<end>"

## 890292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やはり、こうなる、か。<end>"
EN: "So it has come to this after all.<end>"
KO: "…역시 이렇게 되는 건가.<end>"

## 890293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらあらがっても無駄。<line-break>すべては、さだめ…。<end>"
EN: "Struggle all you like. It is futile. All is ordained...<end>"
KO: "아무리 발버둥 쳐도 소용없어. 모든 것은 운명….<end>"

## 890294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらあらがおうとも…<line-break>すべては、さだめ…。<line-break>ふふ…うふふふふふ。<end>"
EN: "However fiercely you struggle... all is ordained... Heh... Ahahahahaha.<end>"
KO: "아무리 저항해도… 모든 것은 숙명…. 후후… 우후후후후.<end>"

## 890295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴがディンガルを支配する…。<line-break>妖術宰相ゾフォルの預言が、<line-break>いよいよ成就の時を迎えるというのか…<end>"
EN: "Zagiv will rule Dyneskal... Can it be that the prophecy of Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, is finally about to be fulfilled?<end>"
KO: "자기브가 딘갈을 지배한다…. 요술 재상 조포르의 예언이 마침내 성취될 때를 맞는 건가….<end>"

## 890296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけたまえ。<line-break>彼女の内には、円卓騎士マゴスが<line-break>棲みついているのだ。<end>"
EN: "Be careful. Magos, a Knight of the Round Table, dwells within her.<end>"
KO: "조심하게. 그녀 안에는 원탁기사 마고스가 깃들어 있다.<end>"

## 890297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ベルゼーヴァ。<line-break>もうあなたを逃さないわ。<line-break>私は皇帝となり、もうなにも逃さない。<end>"
EN: "Beelzeva... I won't let you escape again. I am the emperor now, and I will never let anything escape me again.<end>"
KO: "…벨제바. 이제 당신을 놓치지 않을 거야. 나는 황제가 되어 이제 아무것도 놓치지 않아.<end>"

## 890298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、玄武将軍…。<end>"
EN: "Guh... General of the Black Tortoise...<end>"
KO: "큭, 현무장군….<end>"

## 890299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくら、あなたの力が強くても<line-break>今の私には勝てないわ。<line-break>今の私からは逃げられない…。<end>"
EN: "No matter how great your power is, you cannot defeat me as I am now. And you cannot escape me...<end>"
KO: "아무리 네 힘이 강해도 지금의 나를 이길 수는 없어. 지금의 나한테서는 도망칠 수도 없어….<end>"

## 890301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今よ！<end>"
EN: "…Now!<end>"
KO: "…지금이야!<end>"

## 890302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐあっ！<end>"
EN: "Gah!<end>"
KO: "크악!<end>"

## 890303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まだ耐えられるなんて、さすがね。<line-break>でも、帝都の人間のソウルは、<line-break>ウルグ様にささげられたわ。<line-break>あなたたちも立つことはできない。<line-break>さあ、おとなしくしなさい…<end>"
EN: "I'm impressed you're still holding out. But the Souls of the people in the imperial capital have already been offered to Lord Ulugh. None of you can even stand. Now, be still...<end>"
KO: "…아직 버틸 수 있다니, 역시 대단하네. 하지만 제도의 인간들의 소울은 울그 님께 바쳐졌어. 너희도 일어설 수 없어. 자, 얌전히 있어….<end>"

## 890304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうはいかん。<line-break>人類の革新を…<line-break>阻まれるわけにはいかんのだ！<end>"
EN: "No... I cannot allow Humanity's Evolution to be thwarted!<end>"
KO: "…그럴 수는 없다. 인류의 혁신을… 방해받을 수는 없어!<end>"

## 890305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃がさない…。<line-break>誰も、運命からは逃れられない…！<end>"
EN: "I won't let you escape... No one can escape fate!<end>"
KO: "놓치지 않아…. 누구도 운명에서 도망칠 수 없어…!<end>"

## 890306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらあらがっても無駄。<line-break>すべては、さだめ…。<end>"
EN: "Struggle all you like. It is futile. All is ordained...<end>"
KO: "아무리 발버둥 쳐도 소용없어. 모든 것은 운명….<end>"

## 890307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらあらがおうとも…<line-break>すべては、さだめ…。<line-break>ふふ…うふふふふふ。<end>"
EN: "However fiercely you struggle... all is ordained... Heh... Ahahahahaha.<end>"
KO: "아무리 저항해도… 모든 것은 숙명…. 후후… 우후후후후.<end>"

## 890308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…やはり、ここまでが限界か。<line-break>次のソウルリープからは逃げられん。<line-break>人類の革新なる前に果てるさだめか…！<end>"
EN: "Damn... So this is my limit. I won't survive the next Soul Leap. Am I fated to die before Humanity's Evolution...?<end>"
KO: "큭… 역시 여기까지가 한계인가. 다음 소울 리프에서는 벗어날 수 없어. 인류의 혁신을 이루기 전에 끝날 운명인가…!<end>"

## 890309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな。<line-break>あきらめるには早い。<line-break>まだ、君たちがいる…<end>"
EN: "Yes... It is too soon to give up. You are still here...<end>"
KO: "…그래. 포기하기엔 일러. 아직 너희가 있어….<end>"

## 890310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君たちの力を回復した。<end>"
EN: "I have restored your strength.<end>"
KO: "너희의 힘을 회복시켰다.<end>"

## 890311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も、人類の革新という大望を<line-break>抱きながら、みすみすこんなところで<line-break>死ぬわけにはいかないからな。<end>"
EN: "I, too, harbor the grand ambition of Humanity's Evolution. I cannot simply allow myself to die here.<end>"
KO: "나도 인류의 혁신이라는 큰 뜻을 품고 있는 몸이다. 이런 곳에서 허무하게 죽을 순 없지.<end>"

## 890312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、急ぐのだ。<line-break>次の攻撃が来る前に脱出するのだ。<end>"
EN: "Now, hurry. Escape before the next attack comes.<end>"
KO: "자, 서둘러라. 다음 공격이 오기 전에 탈출해야 한다.<end>"

## 890313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間に合ったか。<end>"
EN: "Made it in time?<end>"
KO: "제때 왔나.<end>"

## 890314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の我らでは、やつらには勝てない。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>…ネメア様亡き今、<line-break>人類の革新は叶わぬこと<line-break>なのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "As we are now, we cannot defeat them. With Lord Nemea gone... perhaps Humanity's Evolution can never be achieved.<end>"
KO: "지금의 우리로는 녀석들에게 이길 수 없다. …네메아 님이 없는 지금, 인류의 혁신은 이루어질 수 없는 일인지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 890315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…その自信はどこから来るのだ？<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>しかし…<line-break>君のおかげで命が助かったのは事実だ。<end>"
EN: "What makes you so confident? Still... the fact remains that I owe you my life.<end>"
KO: "…그 자신감은 어디서 나오는 거지? 하지만… 네 덕분에 목숨을 건진 건 사실이다.<end>"

## 890316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の我らでは、やつらには勝てまい。<line-break>さらなる力を手に入れる必要がある。<end>"
EN: "As we are now, we cannot defeat them. We must obtain still greater power.<end>"
KO: "지금의 우리로는 녀석들에게 이길 수 없다. 더 큰 힘을 손에 넣어야 한다.<end>"

## 890317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…かつて、そのあまりの威力ゆえ、<line-break>封士レオニックの手によって<line-break>遺跡に封印されたという、<line-break>禁呪とも呼ぶべき最強の聖魔法、<line-break>アドヴェント。<end>"
EN: "Advent―the mightiest of holy magics, worthy of being called a Forbidden Spell. It is said that its power was so great, the Sealer Leonic once sealed it away in a ruin.<end>"
KO: "…과거 그 엄청난 위력 때문에 봉사 레오닉의 손으로 유적에 봉인되었다는, 금주라 불러도 될 최강의 성마법 아드벤트.<end>"

## 890318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遺跡はある湖に沈められた。<line-break>強大なる力を秘めた者だけが、<line-break>その遺跡に入ることができると言う。<line-break>もし、君が…<line-break>人類に革新をもたらしうる存在であれば、<line-break>そこに来ることができるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "The ruin was submerged in a lake. It is said that only one who harbors tremendous power can enter it. If you truly are capable of bringing about Humanity's Evolution... you should be able to reach that place.<end>"
KO: "유적은 어느 호수에 가라앉혀졌다. 강대한 힘을 품은 자만 그 유적에 들어갈 수 있다고 한다. 만약 네가… 인류에 혁신을 가져올 존재라면 그곳에 올 수 있을 것이다.<end>"

## 890319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばだ、<value:$28>。<line-break>無限のソウル…無限の可能性を持つ者。<line-break> <line-break>君に人類を導く力があるのなら…<line-break>また会うことになろう。<end>"
EN: "Farewell, <value:$28>―bearer of the Infinite Soul, and of infinite possibility. If you possess the power to lead humanity... then we shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "잘 가라, <value:$28>. 무한의 소울… 무한한 가능성을 지닌 자여. 네게 인류를 이끌 힘이 있다면… 다시 만나게 되겠지.<end>"

## 890320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、来る…！<end>"
EN: "I-it's coming...!<end>"
KO: "오, 온다…!<end>"

## 890321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新を成し遂げんとするこの私、<line-break>そして、それを可能とする鍵である<line-break><value:$28>が、<line-break>分別なき魂のひとつとして<line-break>破壊神の杯に盛られるのか…！<line-break>くっ…こんな、こんなことが…！<end>"
EN: "I would bring about Humanity's Evolution, and <value:$28> is the key to making it possible―yet are we to be served in the God of Destruction's cup as nothing more than two mindless souls...?! No... This cannot be...!<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신을 이룩하려는 이 나와, 그것을 가능케 할 열쇠인 <value:$28>가 분별없는 영혼 중 하나로 파괴신의 잔에 담긴다는 말인가…! 큭… 이런, 이런 일이…!<end>"

## 890322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…なんとふがいなき有り様か。<line-break>我が子よ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... What a pathetic sight, my child.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 이 얼마나 한심한 꼴인가. 내 아이여….<end>"

## 890323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャローム…貴様！<line-break>醜きウジ虫の魂までは、<line-break>ソウルリープも見逃したか！<end>"
EN: "Shalom... you bastard! It seems even Soul Leap overlooked the soul of one repulsive maggot!<end>"
KO: "샬롬… 네놈! 추악한 구더기의 혼까지는 소울 리프도 놓친 건가!<end>"

## 890324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほう、たいした減らず口だ。<line-break>耳元に聞こえる死の息吹、<line-break>その恐怖にどれだけ耐えられるものか。<line-break>人類の革新者たるもの、まずは自身で<line-break>人並みの領域を超越して見せねばならぬ。<line-break>…これは、見ものだな。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Such bold words. Let us see how long that bravado lasts with death breathing in your ear. One who would bring about Humanity's Evolution must first prove capable of transcending the limits of ordinary mortals. This should be quite a spectacle.<end>"
KO: "호오, 제법 입이 살아 있군. 귓가에 들리는 죽음의 숨결, 그 공포를 어디까지 견딜 수 있을까. 인류의 혁신자라면 먼저 스스로 평범한 인간의 영역을 초월해 보여야겠지. …볼 만하겠군.<end>"

## 890325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ！<line-break>貴様の悪趣味ぶりには<line-break>吐き気すら覚える！<line-break>息子の死に際くらい、<line-break>気を利かせられないのか！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! Your depravity makes me sick! Could you not show a little consideration while your own son is dying?<end>"
KO: "큭! 네 악취미에는 구역질까지 난다! 아들이 죽는 순간만큼은 눈치 좀 챌 수 없나!<end>"

## 890326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そう捨て鉢になるな。<line-break>我が子の死を悲しまない親が<line-break>いると思うか？<end>"
EN: "Come now, do not be so desperate. Do you imagine any parent would not mourn the death of a child?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 자포자기하지 마라. 자기 자식의 죽음을 슬퍼하지 않는 부모가 있다고 생각하나?<end>"

## 890327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様が欲しているのは、<line-break>意のままに操れる人形だろう！<line-break> <line-break>恥を知れ！<line-break>貴様は、人類の革新者どころか、<line-break>死肉にたかる金蝿そのものだ！<end>"
EN: "What you want is a puppet you can control at will! Have you no shame? You are no agent of Humanity's Evolution. You are nothing but a blowfly feeding on carrion!<end>"
KO: "네가 원하는 건 마음대로 조종할 수 있는 인형이겠지! 부끄러운 줄 알아라! 너는 인류의 혁신자는커녕 썩은 고기에 달라붙는 금파리 그 자체다!<end>"

## 890329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだと…？<end>"
EN: "W-what did you say...?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고…?<end>"

## 890330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは取り引きだ、我が子よ。<line-break>汝の、親しみやすき肉体をささげ、<line-break>この金蝿の王と契約せよ。<line-break>さすれば、余はこの窮地から、<line-break><value:$28>を救い出そう。<end>"
EN: "I offer you a bargain, my child. Surrender that familiar flesh of yours and enter into a pact with this king of blowflies. In return, I shall rescue <value:$28> from this predicament.<end>"
KO: "이건 거래다, 나의 아이여. 그 친근한 육체를 바치고 이 금파리의 왕과 계약하라. 그러면 짐이 이 궁지에서 <value:$28>를 구해내마.<end>"

## 890331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類革新の方針は違えど、<line-break><value:$28>は、<line-break>余にとってもかけがえのない存在だ。<line-break>こんなところで、<line-break>みすみす失いたくはない。<line-break>悪い話ではあるまい？<end>"
EN: "Though our visions of Humanity's Evolution differ, <value:$28> is irreplaceable to me as well. I have no wish to stand by and lose someone so precious here. It is not such a poor bargain, is it?<end>"
KO: "인류 혁신의 방침은 다르지만, <value:$28>는 짐에게도 무엇과도 바꿀 수 없는 존재다. 이런 곳에서 속절없이 잃고 싶지는 않다. 나쁜 제안은 아니겠지?<end>"

## 890332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかっている。<line-break>だが、完全に食い潰されさえ<line-break>しなければ…<line-break>いつか必ず奴の支配に対して<line-break>私は反旗をひるがえす…<end>"
EN: "I know. But so long as he does not consume me completely... someday, without fail, I will rise against his rule...<end>"
KO: "…알고 있다. 하지만 완전히 먹혀 버리지만 않는다면… 언젠가 반드시 그자의 지배에 반기를 들겠다….<end>"

## 890333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが想定できないほど、<line-break>愚かな男ではない。<end>"
EN: "I am not fool enough to think he has failed to anticipate that.<end>"
KO: "그것조차 예상하지 못할 만큼 어리석은 남자는 아니다.<end>"

## 890334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様が言うとおりにしよう！<line-break>その代わり…<end>"
EN: "I will do as you say! But in return...<end>"
KO: "네놈이 말한 대로 하겠다! 대신…<end>"

## 890335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は必ず助け出そう。<line-break>万が一にもぬかりはない。<end>"
EN: "I shall save <value:$28>. You have my word―nothing will go amiss.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 반드시 구해 내겠다. 만에 하나라도 빈틈은 없어.<end>"

## 890336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が子よ、余は約束を果たしたぞ。<line-break>次は、汝の番だ。<end>"
EN: "My child, I have fulfilled my promise. Now it is your turn.<end>"
KO: "내 아이여, 나는 약속을 지켰다. 다음은 네 차례다.<end>"

## 890337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の性格が、<line-break>狡猾な貴様に似なかったことを<line-break>喜ぶがいい。<line-break>私も…約束を守ろう。<end>"
EN: "Be grateful I did not inherit your cunning nature. I, too... will keep my promise.<end>"
KO: "내 성격이 교활한 네놈을 닮지 않은 걸 기뻐해라. 나도… 약속을 지키겠다.<end>"

## 890338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかろう。<line-break>人類革新の日は近いぞ、わが息子よ…！<end>"
EN: "Very well. The day of Humanity's Evolution draws near, my son...!<end>"
KO: "좋다. 인류 혁신의 날은 머지않았다, 내 아들이여…!<end>"

## 890341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、来る…！<end>"
EN: "I-it's coming...!<end>"
KO: "오, 온다…!<end>"

## 890342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新を成し遂げんとするこの私、<line-break>そして、それを可能とする鍵である<line-break><value:$28>が、<line-break>分別なき魂のひとつとして、<line-break>破壊神の杯に盛られるのか…！<line-break>くっ…こんな、こんなことが…！<end>"
EN: "I would bring about Humanity's Evolution, and <value:$28> is the key to making it possible―yet are we to be served in the God of Destruction's cup as nothing more than two mindless souls...?! No... This cannot be...!<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신을 이루려는 나, 그리고 그것을 가능하게 하는 열쇠인 <value:$28>가 분별없는 영혼 하나로 파괴신의 잔에 담기는 것인가…! 큭… 이런, 이런 일이…!<end>"

## 890343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうはさせぬ。<line-break>我が身果てようとも、人類の革新の芽を<line-break>摘ませるわけにはいかんのだ。<line-break>ゆけ！<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主よ。<line-break>人類の…人類の革新を…<end>"
EN: "N-no, I will not allow it. Even if I perish, I cannot let the seed of Humanity's Evolution be crushed. Go! Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Humanity's... Humanity's Evolution...<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇게 두진 않겠다. 내 몸이 스러지더라도 인류 혁신의 싹을 꺾게 둘 수는 없어. 가라! 무한의 소울을 가진 자여. 인류의… 인류의 혁신을…<end>"

## 890344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…地獄の苦しみだぜ<end>"
EN: "...It's sheer hell.<end>"
KO: "…지옥 같은 고통이야<end>"

## 890345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人相手に<line-break>こいつも、こいつの面倒見てる<line-break>ケリュネイアもよくやってるぜ。<line-break>正直、魔人やってたころは<line-break>人間の生きようって姿に<line-break>俺、気づいてなかった。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia and the one in her care are both doing damn well against a demon. Honestly, back when I was a demon myself, I never saw the way humans strive to live.<end>"
KO: "마인을 상대로 이 녀석도, 이 녀석을 돌보는 케류네이아도 잘하고 있어. 솔직히 마인이었을 때는 인간이 살아가려는 모습 같은 건 내가 몰랐지.<end>"

## 890346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "弱いってことは<line-break>大変なことだけど、だから、<line-break>人は誰かと一緒にいられるんだな。<line-break>そう、弱いってことは<line-break>大事なことなんだ…。<line-break>とても、つらいけれど…。<end>"
EN: "Being weak is hard. But that's why people can be with one another. Yes, weakness matters, painful as it is.<end>"
KO: "약하다는 건 힘든 일이지만, 그렇기 때문에 사람은 누군가와 함께 있을 수 있는 거구나. 그래, 약하다는 건 중요한 일이야…. 아주 괴롭지만….<end>"

## 890347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスは人の心に棲む魔人だ。<line-break>どんなに鋭い剣だろうが、<line-break>どんなに強い魔法だろうが、<line-break>心には触れることすらできない。<line-break>クッ。<line-break>どうすりゃいいんだ？<end>"
EN: "Magos is a demon that dwells in the human heart. No sword, however keen, and no magic, however powerful, can so much as touch the heart. Damn it. What am I supposed to do?<end>"
KO: "마고스는 사람의 마음에 깃드는 마인이다. 아무리 날카로운 검도, 아무리 강한 마법도 마음에는 손조차 댈 수 없어. 큭. 어떻게 해야 하지?<end>"

## 890348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このままじゃ、<line-break>こいつもケリュネイアも…<line-break> <line-break>クッ。魔人の俺には人間の心を<line-break>救うことなんてできやしねぇ。<line-break>人間のお前がなんとかしろ！<end>"
EN: "If this goes on, Ceryneia and the one in her care will... Damn it. I'm a demon. There's no way I can save a human heart. You're human, so do something about it!<end>"
KO: "이대로면 이 녀석도 케류네이아도…. 큭. 마인인 나로선 인간의 마음을 구할 수 없어. 인간인 네가 어떻게든 해!<end>"

## 890349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Ah... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아…, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は、少し、いいみたいなの…。<line-break>でも…。<end>"
EN: "Things seem a little better today... but...<end>"
KO: "오늘은 조금 괜찮은 것 같아…. 그래도….<end>"

## 890351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2また…、<line-break>いつ闇に飲まれるかわからない。<line-break>今度もマゴスに<line-break>支配されずにいられるかわからない…。<line-break>私、こわい…。<end>もう…限界かも知れない。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>それに<line-break>これ以上、みんなに迷惑…<line-break>かけられない…。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2I don't know when the darkness might swallow me again. I don't know if I'll be able to keep Magos from taking control of me next time... I'm scared.<end>I may have reached my limit... And I can't keep burdening everyone like this.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2또… 언제 어둠에 삼켜질지 몰라. 이번에도 마고스에게 지배당하지 않을 수 있을지 모르겠어…. 나, 무서워….<end>이제… 한계일지도 몰라. 게다가 더는 모두에게 폐를… 끼칠 수 없어….<end>"

## 890352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>。<line-break>来てくれたのね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You came.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>. 와 줬구나.<end>"

## 890353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私のことが心配だったの？<end>"
EN: "Were you worried about me?<end>"
KO: "내가 걱정됐던 거야?<end>"

## 890354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…。<line-break><value:$28>が<line-break>そう言ってくれると、心強いわ。<line-break>私、これから…<end>"
EN: "Thank you... Hearing you say that gives me courage, <value:$28>. From now on, I...<end>"
KO: "고마워…. <value:$28>이 그렇게 말해 주니 든든해. 나, 앞으로….<end>"

## 890355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううっ…！<end>"
EN: "Ugh...!<end>"
KO: "으윽…!<end>"

## 890356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ…！<end>"
EN: "Zagiv...!<end>"
KO: "자기브…!<end>"

## 890357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう…ふう…。<line-break>ど、どうして…？<line-break>今日は…大丈夫だと思ったのに…。<line-break>だんだん…<line-break>間隔も短くなってきている。<line-break>もう、限界なのかもしれない…。<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... W-why...? I thought I'd be all right today... but they're coming faster and faster. I may already be at my limit...<end>"
KO: "후우… 후우…. 왜…? 오늘은… 괜찮을 줄 알았는데…. 점점… 간격도 짧아지고 있어. 이제 한계일지도 몰라….<end>"

## 890358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ、弱気にならないで。<line-break>いつだって希望はあるものよ！<end>"
EN: "Zagiv, don't lose heart. There's always hope!<end>"
KO: "자기브, 약해지지 마. 언제나 희망은 있는 법이야!<end>"

## 890359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "希望？　希望なんてない！<line-break>運命には逆らえないの！<line-break>…私にあるのは、絶望だけなのよ！<end>"
EN: "Hope? There is no hope! Fate can't be defied! All I have... is despair!<end>"
KO: "희망? 희망 같은 건 없어! 운명에는 거스를 수 없어! …내게 있는 건 절망뿐이야!<end>"

## 890360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、<value:$28>、<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そろそろ行きましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, shall we be going?<end>"
KO: "그럼, <value:$28>. 슬슬 갈까요.<end>"

## 890361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さん！<line-break>ザギヴを放っておいて<line-break>どこへ行くというの？<end>"
EN: "Father! Where do you think you're going, leaving Zagiv here?<end>"
KO: "아버지! 자기브를 내버려 두고 어디로 가겠다는 거예요?<end>"

## 890362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいところです。<line-break>無論、ザギヴさんを<line-break>放って行きはしません。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>ザギヴさんの護衛をお願いしますよ。<end>"
EN: "Somewhere nice. Of course, I won't be leaving Zagiv behind. <value:$28>, please act as Zagiv's escort.<end>"
KO: "좋습니다. 물론 자기브 씨를 두고 가진 않겠습니다. <value:$28>, 자기브 씨의 호위를 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 890363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな状態で！？<line-break>危険よ！<end>"
EN: "In her condition?! It's too dangerous!<end>"
KO: "이런 상태로!? 위험해!<end>"

## 890364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア。人は誰でも、<line-break>いつかは自分の中の闇と<line-break>向き合わなくてはいけないのですよ。<line-break>そして、誰にとっても、<line-break>それはとても危険なことなんです。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia. Sooner or later, everyone must face the darkness within. It is a very dangerous thing for anyone to do.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아. 사람은 누구나 언젠가 자기 안의 어둠과 마주해야 합니다. 그리고 누구에게나 그건 아주 위험한 일이죠.<end>"

## 890365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア、転送機で<line-break>ワッシャー砦へお願いします。<line-break>船が入り用になるものでね。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia, Washer Fort by teleporter, please. We'll be needing a ship.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아, 전송기로 왓샤 요새까지 부탁합니다. 배가 필요해서 말이지요.<end>"

## 890366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャプテン・ヒルダリア、<line-break>頼もしい舵さばきでした。<line-break>感謝しますよ。<end>"
EN: "Captain Hildaria, that was some fine work at the helm. You have my thanks.<end>"
KO: "캡틴 힐다리아, 믿음직한 조타였습니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 890367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、荒海の海賊カンダカンの<line-break>忘れ形見が、こんなに美しい<line-break>お嬢さんだとは。ふふふ…。<end>"
EN: "Still, to think the child left behind by Kandakan, pirate of the raging seas, would be such a beautiful young lady. Heh heh...<end>"
KO: "그나저나 거친 바다의 해적 칸다칸이 남긴 딸이 이렇게 아름다운 아가씨라니. 후후후….<end>"

## 890368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なかなか、<value:$28>も<line-break>隅に置けませんねえ。<end>"
EN: "You're quite something yourself, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>도 제법이군요.<end>"

## 890369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、失礼しました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, forgive me.<end>"
KO: "앗, 실례했습니다.<end>"

## 890370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "百聞は一見にしかずと言います。<line-break>ついてきてください。<end>"
EN: "They say seeing is believing. Come with me.<end>"
KO: "백문이 불여일견이라고 합니다. 따라오세요.<end>"

## 890371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫と…カワイイ猫としゃべれた…<line-break>ああ、しあわせ…。<end>"
EN: "A cat... I got to talk to a cute cat... Oh, I'm so happy...<end>"
KO: "고양이와… 귀여운 고양이와 이야기했어… 아아, 행복해….<end>"

## 890372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待ちしていましたよ。<line-break>さあ、ここです。<end>"
EN: "I've been waiting for you. Here we are.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었습니다. 자, 여기입니다.<end>"

## 890373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんて…美しい…。<line-break>…こんな所が…あったなんて…。<end>"
EN: "It's so... beautiful. I never knew a place like this existed...<end>"
KO: "어쩜… 아름다워…. 이런 곳이… 있었다니….<end>"

## 890375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここへ来るまでに、<line-break>怪物たちがいたはずです。<line-break><line-break>ひょっとしたら、襲われたんでは<line-break>ないですか？　あなたはそれを<line-break>倒したんじゃないですか？<end>"
EN: "You must have encountered monsters on your way here. Did they attack you, perhaps? And you defeated them?<end>"
KO: "여기 오는 길에 괴물들이 있었을 겁니다. 혹시 습격당한 거 아닙니까? 당신이 그걸 쓰러뜨린 건 아닙니까?<end>"

## 890376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、それでいいのです。<line-break>抵抗しなければ、あなたが<line-break>やられていたでしょうから。<line-break>しかし、見てください。<end>"
EN: "No, you did the right thing. Had you not fought back, you would have been the one struck down. But look.<end>"
KO: "아니요, 그걸로 됐습니다. 저항하지 않았다면 당신이 당했을 테니까요. 하지만 보세요.<end>"

## 890377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの怪物たちは、毎日、この美しい<line-break>景色を見、この景色に抱かれて、<line-break>暮らしているんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Every day, those monsters gaze upon this beautiful landscape and live cradled within it.<end>"
KO: "저 괴물들은 매일 이 아름다운 풍경을 보고, 이 풍경에 안겨 살아가고 있어요.<end>"

## 890378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここには否定も、<line-break>拒絶もありません。<end>"
EN: "There is no denial here. No rejection.<end>"
KO: "여기에는 부정도 거절도 없습니다.<end>"

## 890379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの怪物たちは、<line-break>この世界、この景色の中で、<line-break>生きることを許されているんです。<line-break>すべてがあるべくしてある。<line-break>それが世界の本当の姿なのです。<end>"
EN: "Those monsters are allowed to live in this world, amid this landscape. Everything exists because it has its place. That is the world's true nature.<end>"
KO: "저 괴물들도 이 세계, 이 풍경 속에서 살아갈 허락을 받은 겁니다. 모든 것은 있어야 할 곳에 존재합니다. 그게 세계의 진짜 모습입니다.<end>"

## 890380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、<line-break>そんなことが言えるのです！？<end>"
EN: "How can you say that!?<end>"
KO: "어째서, 그런 말을 할 수 있는 겁니까!?<end>"

## 890381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはこの景色を見て、<line-break>美しいと感じたのではないのですか？ <line-break><line-break>世界はあなたに美しい風景を見せる。<line-break>惜しみなく…。世界はあなたを<line-break>拒んだりしてはいませんよ。<end>"
EN: "Did you not look upon this landscape and find it beautiful? The world shows you beautiful sights without reserve. It has not rejected you.<end>"
KO: "당신은 이 풍경을 보고 아름답다고 느끼지 않았습니까? 세계는 당신에게 아름다운 풍경을 보여 줍니다. 아낌없이…. 세계는 당신을 거부하고 있지 않아요.<end>"

## 890382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、それを美しいと感じたと<line-break>言うことは、あなたもまた世界を<line-break>受け入れたのだとは言えませんか？<end>"
EN: "And if you found it beautiful, could that not mean that you, too, have accepted the world?<end>"
KO: "그리고 그것을 아름답다고 느꼈다는 건 당신 역시 이 세계를 받아들인 것이라고 할 수 있지 않을까요?<end>"

## 890383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。<end>"
EN: "Ah...<end>"
KO: "아…<end>"

## 890384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし…、<line-break>運命には、勝てません。<end>"
EN: "But... fate cannot be overcome.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 운명에는 이길 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 890385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことは、<line-break>運命と闘ってみてから<line-break>言うことですよ。<line-break>私の息子ネメアは、<line-break>運命を変えようと<line-break>懸命にあらがっています。<end>"
EN: "You should say that only after you have tried fighting fate. My son Nemea is struggling with all his might to change it.<end>"
KO: "그런 말은 운명과 싸워 본 뒤에 하세요. 제 아들 네메아는 운명을 바꾸려고 필사적으로 맞서고 있습니다.<end>"

## 890386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決心がついたら来てください。<line-break>私はヒルダリアの所で待ってますから。<end>"
EN: "Come to me once you've made your decision. I'll be waiting at Hildaria's.<end>"
KO: "결심이 서면 와 주세요. 저는 힐다리아에게서 기다리고 있을 테니까.<end>"

## 890387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けて…くれるの…？<end>"
EN: "You'll... help me?<end>"
KO: "도와… 줄 거야…?<end>"

## 890388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>迷惑かけるわね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Thank you. I know this will be a burden.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 고마워. 폐를 끼치네.<end>"

## 890389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決心がつきましたか。<end>"
EN: "Have you made up your mind?<end>"
KO: "결심이 섰습니까?<end>"

## 890390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス様。<line-break>私、運命と…闘ってきます。<end>"
EN: "Lord Orphaus. I'm going to... fight fate.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 님. 저, 운명과… 싸우고 오겠습니다.<end>"

## 890391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>あなたの力を貸してあげてください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Please lend your strength.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신의 힘을 빌려 주세요.<end>"

## 890392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫先生から話は聞いてるよ。<line-break>エンシャントに行くのね。<line-break>送るわ。<end>"
EN: "Professor Cat told me. You're going to Ancient, aren't you? I'll send you there.<end>"
KO: "고양이 선생님에게 들었어. 엔샨트에 가는 거지. 보내 줄게.<end>"

## 890393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って。<end>"
EN: "Wait.<end>"
KO: "기다려.<end>"

## 890394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、あのとき、<line-break>勇気を出してよかったのかしらね？<end>"
EN: "I wonder... was it a good thing that I found the courage back then?<end>"
KO: "…나, 그때 용기를 내길 잘한 걸까?<end>"

## 890395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私を悲しませないでね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Don't make me sad, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나를 슬프게 하지 마, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。あの子と共に、<line-break>この海の向こうを目指しますか。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you're setting out with him, bound for the far side of this sea.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그 아이와 함께 이 바다 너머를 향하시겠습니까.<end>"

## 890397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、あの子が、<line-break>自分の旅に他人を誘うなんて、<line-break>本当に珍しい。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It's truly rare for him to invite anyone along on his own journey.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그 아이가 자기 여행에 남을 초대하다니 정말 드문 일이네.<end>"

## 890398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、ひょっとしたら、　　　<line-break>あの子が<value:$28>の旅に<line-break>　<line-break>ついていくということなのかも<line-break>しれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Or perhaps he's the one joining <value:$28> on the journey.<end>"
KO: "아니, 어쩌면 그 아이가 <value:$28>의 여행에 따라간다는 뜻일지도 모르겠군요.<end>"

## 890399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今だから言いますが、あの子は、<line-break>内心いつも悩んでいました。<line-break> <line-break>その、どこか近寄りがたいような<line-break>雰囲気のせいもありますが…<end>"
EN: "I can tell you this now: deep down, he was always troubled. That air of his―the way he can seem so unapproachable―was partly to blame...<end>"
KO: "지금이라서 말씀드리지만, 그 아이는 속으로 늘 고민하고 있었습니다. 어딘가 가까이하기 어려운 분위기 탓도 있었지만….<end>"

## 890400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまで外の人間が彼に対して   <line-break>要求してきたものといえば、<line-break>その名の重みであるとか、<line-break>卓越した強さであるとか…<line-break>何にせよ、ネメアという人間<line-break>そのものではないことが多かった。<end>"
EN: "Until now, what others wanted from him was usually the weight of his name or his extraordinary strength... Whatever it was, more often than not, it was something other than Nemea himself.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 외부 사람들이 그에게 요구한 건 그 이름의 무게라든가 뛰어난 강함이라든가… 어쨌든 네메아라는 인간 그 자체가 아닌 경우가 많았어.<end>"

## 890401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強くあるということは、<line-break>同時に辛いことでもあり、      <line-break> <line-break>強いまま生き続けるというのは、<line-break>とても難しいことなのです。<end>"
EN: "There is pain in being strong, and it is terribly difficult to go on living without ever ceasing to be strong.<end>"
KO: "강하다는 건 동시에 괴로운 일이기도 하고, 강한 채로 계속 살아가는 건 아주 어려운 일이에요.<end>"

## 890402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、それは、あなたが一番良く<line-break>知っていることですね、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "But no... You know that better than anyone, don't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…아니, 그건 당신이 가장 잘 알고 있는 일이겠죠, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 890403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたと出会えて、あの子は、<line-break>心のくびきからようやく<line-break>解き放たれようとしているのでしょう。<line-break>この一連の戦いで何かを<line-break>得たというのは、<line-break>あの子も一緒だったというわけです。<end>"
EN: "Meeting you may finally be freeing him from the yoke upon his heart. It seems he, too, gained something from this series of battles.<end>"
KO: "당신과 만나면서 그 아이는 마음의 굴레에서 드디어 풀려나려는 것이겠죠. 이 일련의 싸움에서 무언가를 얻은 건 그 아이도 마찬가지였다는 겁니다.<end>"

## 890404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、そろそろだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, it's time.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 슬슬 때가 됐다.<end>"

## 890405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…父よ、何と言おうが、<line-break>あの時死なんとしていた私を救い、<line-break>育ててくれたのは、あなただ。<line-break>そのことには感謝している。<end>"
EN: "Father... Whatever else I may say, you saved me when I was about to die, and you raised me. For that, I am grateful.<end>"
KO: "…아버지여, 무슨 말을 하든 그때 죽으려던 나를 구하고 키워 준 건 당신이다. 그 점은 감사하고 있다.<end>"

## 890406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、この旅に出れば、<line-break>もうあなたには…<end>"
EN: "But once I set out on this journey, you and I will never...<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 여행을 떠나면 이제 당신에게는….<end>"

## 890407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、今度こそ、<line-break>お土産を待っていますよ、ネメア。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. This time, I shall expect a souvenir, Nemea.<end>"
KO: "후후, 이번에는 꼭 선물을 기다리고 있을게요, 네메아.<end>"

## 890408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>フッ、あなたらしい別れの言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "... Heh. A farewell so like you.<end>"
KO: "………………. 훗, 당신다운 작별 인사군.<end>"

## 890409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では行こうか、<value:$28>。<line-break>行ける限り、地の果てまでも。<end>"
EN: "Then let us go, <value:$28>. As far as we can―even to the ends of the earth.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자, <value:$28>. 갈 수 있는 데까지, 세상 끝까지.<end>"

## 890410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ゼネさんが<line-break>ファーロスの御曹司だったとはねぇ。<line-break>スラムの住人一同びっくりだったよ。<end>"
EN: "Still, who'd have thought Zen was the heir to Pharos? Everyone in the Slums was stunned.<end>"
KO: "그런데 제네 씨가 파로스의 도련님이었다니. 슬럼 주민 모두 깜짝 놀랐어.<end>"

## 890411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへ、悪かった。<line-break>だますつもりはなかったんだが、<line-break>ま、許してくれや。<end>"
EN: "Heh, sorry about that. I didn't mean to fool you. Forgive me, all right?<end>"
KO: "헤헤, 미안. 속일 생각은 없었는데, 뭐 용서해 줘.<end>"

## 890412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃ、もちろんですよ。<line-break>ゼネテスさんのおかげで、<line-break>この国は助かったんだから！<end>"
EN: "Of course! Xenetes saved this country!<end>"
KO: "그야 물론이죠. 제네테스 씨 덕분에 이 나라가 살아났으니까요!<end>"

## 890413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の力じゃないさ。<line-break>必死に国を守ろうとした<line-break>みんなのおかげだ。<end>"
EN: "It wasn't my doing. The credit belongs to everyone who fought desperately to protect this country.<end>"
KO: "내 힘이 아니야. 필사적으로 나라를 지키려 한 모두의 덕분이지.<end>"

## 890414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったくだよ。感謝しなきゃね。<line-break>貴族だって、ゼネさんみたいなのがいるんだ。<line-break>この国は変わるかもしれないよ。<end>"
EN: "That's right. We owe them our thanks. If there are nobles like Zen, maybe this country really can change.<end>"
KO: "정말 그래. 감사해야겠네. 귀족 중에도 제네 씨 같은 사람이 있어. 이 나라도 변할지 몰라.<end>"

## 890415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにねー、ゼネテスには、<line-break>さいこーの相棒がいるんだもんね。<end>"
EN: "And Xenetes has the best partner ever!<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이지—, 제네테스에겐 최고의 동료가 있잖아.<end>"

## 890416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<line-break>ハンナはよくわかってるな。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Hannah understands.<end>"
KO: "그래. 한나는 잘 알고 있구나.<end>"

## 890417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "후후후.<end>"

## 890418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、ゼネさん、<line-break>あんたこんなところにいていいのかい？<end>"
EN: "By the way, Zen, should you really be hanging around here?<end>"
KO: "그런데 제네 씨, 이런 데 있어도 괜찮아?<end>"

## 890419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　ああ、まあ、なんだ、<line-break>宮廷は退屈なんでね。<line-break>ちっと相棒と遊びにでも行こうかと。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, well... The court is dull. I thought I'd go have a little fun with my partner.<end>"
KO: "응? 아, 뭐랄까, 궁정은 지루해서 말이야. 잠깐 파트너랑 놀러라도 갈까 해서.<end>"

## 890420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ギルドでいい仕事を　　　　　　　<line-break>見つけたんだわ。<line-break>どうだ、ちょっくら<line-break>やってみないか？<line-break>古代王国の秘宝探しなんだけどよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I found a good job at the Guild. Want to give it a try? We'd be hunting for treasure from an ancient kingdom.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 길드에서 괜찮은 일을 찾았어. 어때, 한번 해 보지 않을래? 고대 왕국의 비보 찾기야.<end>"

## 890421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、決まりだ！<line-break>行こうぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Good, it's settled! Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 결정이다! 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスも<value:$28>も<line-break>結局、冒険好きなのね。<line-break>子供ねー。<end>"
EN: "In the end, both Xenetes and <value:$28> just love adventures. Such children.<end>"
KO: "제네테스도 <value:$28>도 결국 모험을 좋아하는구나. 어린애네~.<end>"

## 890423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、来たか、<value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>今日は、ちょいとお前さんに<line-break>頼みたいことがあってよ。<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>. I've got a little favor to ask you today.<end>"
KO: "오, 왔나, <value:$28>. 오늘은 네게 부탁할 게 좀 있어서 말이야.<end>"

## 890424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…叔母貴が死んじまってから、<line-break>やっぱり何かしら変わっちまったよな。<end>"
EN: "Ever since my aunt died, things really have changed somehow.<end>"
KO: "…숙모가 죽고 나서 역시 뭔가 달라졌지.<end>"

## 890425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変わりたくないって言ったとこで、<line-break>変わらずにゃいられない…。<line-break> <line-break>それだけ叔母貴の存在が<line-break>大きかったってことだものな。<end>"
EN: "You can say you don't want to change, but you can't stay the same... I guess that's how much my aunt meant to me.<end>"
KO: "변하고 싶지 않다고 해도 변하지 않을 수는 없어…. 그만큼 숙모의 존재가 컸다는 뜻이니까.<end>"

## 890426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、改めて一杯、ってのは　　　　<line-break>俺があっちがわへ行くまで<line-break>待っててもらうとして、<line-break>それまで、<line-break>叔母貴のことはできるだけ鮮明に<line-break>記憶に留めておきたいって思ってな。<end>"
EN: "Well, she'll have to wait until I cross over to the other side before we can share another drink. Until then, I want to keep my memories of her as vivid as I can.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 다시 한잔하는 건 내가 저쪽으로 갈 때까지 기다려 달라고 하고. 그때까지는 숙모님에 대한 기억을 가능한 한 선명하게 남겨 두고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 890427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も、ひとつ、<line-break>料理でもやってみようと思うんだが…<line-break>どうかな？<end>"
EN: "So I thought I might try my hand at cooking... What do you say?<end>"
KO: "나도 한번 요리를 해 볼까 하는데… 어때?<end>"

## 890428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだよ、<value:$28>？<line-break>その、けげんそうな顔は？<line-break>俺の腕が疑わしいってのか？<end>"
EN: "W-What is it, <value:$28>? Why are you looking at me like that? You doubt my skill?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야, <value:$28>? 그 미심쩍은 얼굴은? 내 실력이 의심스러운 거냐?<end>"

## 890429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、さすがにはっきり言ってくれるぜ。<line-break>…でも、ま、無理かどうか、<line-break>いっちょ付き合ってみてくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Y-You certainly don't mince words. Still, humor me and let's see whether I can pull it off.<end>"
KO: "하, 확실하게 말해 주는군. …그래도 뭐, 정말 무리인지 한번 같이 확인해 보자고.<end>"

## 890430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外に飛び出さねえだけで、　　　　<line-break>これも大冒険ってことにゃ<line-break>違いないだろ。<end>冒険には信頼できる仲間が必要だ。<line-break>そう思い出させてくれたのは、<line-break><value:$28>、お前さんだ。<end>"
EN: "Just because we're not heading outside doesn't mean this isn't a grand adventure.<end>Every adventure needs a companion you can trust. You're the one who reminded me of that, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "밖으로 뛰쳐나가지 않을 뿐이지, 이것도 대모험인 건 틀림없잖아.<end>모험에는 믿을 수 있는 동료가 필요해. 그걸 다시 떠올리게 해 준 건 <value:$28>, 바로 너야.<end>"

## 890432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか…。<end>"
EN: "So you have come...<end>"
KO: "왔나….<end>"

## 890433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>ぬしの剣は、<line-break>竜すら斬り倒してきた。<line-break>それがこのレーグにも<line-break>通じるかどうか…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Your blade has felled even dragons. Now let us see whether it can prevail against Reig.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그대의 검은 용조차 베어 쓰러뜨려 왔다. 그 검이 이 레그에게도 통할지….<end>"

## 890434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>帝国では竜字将軍と呼ばれるぬしだが…<line-break>さて、一騎当千の武将が、<line-break>現代に出現するものか。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... In the Empire, you are called the Dragon-Sigil General. Now, shall a warrior worth a thousand soldiers truly appear in this age?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 제국에서는 용자장군이라 불리는 그대지만… 과연 일기당천의 무장이 현대에도 나타나는가.<end>"

## 890435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（母さん？<line-break>　こんな時間に、どこへ…？）<end>"
EN: "(Mom? Where are you going at this hour...?)<end>"
KO: "(엄마? 이 시간에 어디로…?)<end>"

## 890436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（やっぱり、母さん、<line-break>　いない…。）<end>"
EN: "(I knew it. Mom's not here...)<end>"
KO: "(역시 엄마는 없어….)<end>"

## 890437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（遺跡のほうへ行く…。<line-break>　…追いかけよう）<end>"
EN: "(Heading for the ruins... I'll follow.)<end>"
KO: "(유적 쪽으로 간다…. …뒤쫓자.)<end>"

## 890438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、大丈夫ね…。<line-break>光は日に日に強くなっているけれど、<line-break>復活する様子はない…。<end>"
EN: "Everything's still all right... The light is growing stronger by the day, but there's no sign of a revival.<end>"
KO: "아직은 괜찮네…. 빛은 날마다 강해지고 있지만 부활할 기미는 없어…<end>"

## 890439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ここに来ては駄目。<line-break>そう言わなかったかしら。<line-break>…！？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you shouldn't have come here. Didn't I tell you that? ...!?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 여기 오면 안 돼. 그렇게 말하지 않았나? …!?<end>"

## 890440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか、竜王の覚醒！？<line-break>また…繰り返されると言うの！？<end>"
EN: "It can't be―the Dragon King's Awakening?! Is it all going to happen again?!<end>"
KO: "설마 용왕의 각성!? 또… 반복된다는 거야!?<end>"

## 890442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前と暮らすようになってから、<line-break>もう１６年になるのね。<line-break>　<line-break>ずっと、こうしていたかったけれど、<line-break>運命はそれを許しては<line-break>くれないようね。<end>"
EN: "We've lived together for sixteen years now. I wish we could have gone on like this, but it seems fate won't allow it.<end>"
KO: "너와 함께 살기 시작한 지 벌써 16년이구나. 계속 이렇게 있고 싶었지만 운명은 허락하지 않는 모양이네.<end>"

## 890443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいで、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Come here, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이리 와, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890444
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この遺跡の真の名前は、<line-break>ヴァシュタールの柩。<line-break>神代から繰り返される戦いの証。<end>"
EN: "This ruin's true name is Vashtar's Coffin, testament to the struggle repeated since the Age of the Gods.<end>"
KO: "이 유적의 진짜 이름은 바슈탈의 관. 신대부터 되풀이된 싸움의 증거다.<end>"

## 890445
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王の覚醒は、戦乱を告げる神の声…。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King's Awakening is the voice of a god, heralding war...<end>"
KO: "용왕의 각성은 전란을 알리는 신의 목소리….<end>"

## 890446
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、戦いの時代が始まるのね…。<end>"
EN: "Another age of war is beginning...<end>"
KO: "또 전쟁의 시대가 시작되는구나….<end>"

## 890447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、たくさんよ…。<line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "I've had enough...<end>"
KO: "이제… 지긋지긋해….<end>"

## 890448
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>どうもありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "응. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 890449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、失礼いたします。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Well then, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 890450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は本当に、疲れたわ…。<line-break>文書の最後に署名を書くだけだった<line-break>けれど、少し曲がってしまったし…。<line-break>もっと、がんばらなくては。<end>"
EN: "I'm truly exhausted today... All I had to do was sign my name at the bottom of a document, but even that came out a little crooked... I must try harder.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 정말 피곤하네…. 문서 끝에 서명만 하면 됐는데 조금 삐뚤어졌고…. 더 열심히 해야겠어.<end>"

## 890451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様ですか？<line-break>どうぞ、お入りになって。<end>"
EN: "Oh, is that you, <value:$28>? Please, come in.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님이십니까? 들어오세요.<end>"

## 890452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、<value:$28>様も<line-break>今日は一日、ご苦労様でした。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そうそう。<value:$28>様を<line-break>お呼びしたのは、これを<line-break>受け取ってもらいたかったからです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You've had a long day too, <value:$28>. Oh, that's right. I called you here, <value:$28>, because I wanted you to have this.<end>"
KO: "우후후, <value:$28> 님도 오늘 하루 수고 많으셨어요. 그리고 <value:$28> 님을 부른 건 이걸 받아 주셨으면 해서예요.<end>"

## 890453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が初めて誰の助けも借りずに<line-break>焼いたクッキーです。<line-break>召しあがってください。<end>"
EN: "This is the first time I've baked cookies without anyone's help. Please, try what I made.<end>"
KO: "제가 처음으로 누구 도움도 받지 않고 구운 쿠키예요. 드셔 주세요.<end>"

## 890454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時は満ちた。<line-break>しかし、なぜ時を告げぬ？<line-break> <line-break>告げるものよ…？<end>"
EN: "The time has come. Yet why do you not proclaim it, O herald of the hour...?<end>"
KO: "때가 찼다. 그런데 왜 때를 알리지 않느냐? 알리는 자여…?<end>"

## 890455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、どうしてかしらね…。<end>"
EN: "Now, why might that be...?<end>"
KO: "글쎄, 왜일까….<end>"

## 890456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml

## 890458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっておろう、アスティアよ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ヴァシュタールを目覚めさせぬのは、<line-break>我らが主への裏切りだぞ。<end>"
EN: "You know full well, Astia. Refusing to awaken Vashtar is a betrayal of our master.<end>"
KO: "알고 있겠지, 아스티아. 바슈타르를 깨우지 않는 건 우리의 주인에 대한 배신이다.<end>"

## 890459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>裏切り者の私を殺しに<line-break>来たんじゃないの？<line-break>円卓騎士、バルザー？<end>"
EN: "Heh... Didn't you come to kill me for my betrayal, Baltzer, Knight of the Round Table?<end>"
KO: "후후…. 배신자인 나를 죽이러 온 거 아니야? 원탁기사 발자르?<end>"

## 890461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしいのね。<line-break>でも、やさしさはすなわち弱さ…<line-break>あなたも歳をとったわね。<end>"
EN: "How gentle of you. But gentleness is weakness... You have grown old.<end>"
KO: "상냥하네. 하지만 상냥함은 곧 약함… 당신도 나이를 먹었구나.<end>"

## 890462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私だけではない。<line-break>お前もだ、アスティア。<end>"
EN: "I am not the only one. So have you, Astia.<end>"
KO: "나만이 아니다. 너도다, 아스티아.<end>"

## 890463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0私があの少年に近づいたとき、<line-break>お前は動く素振りも見せなかった。　<end>私があの少女に近づいたとき、<line-break>お前は動く素振りも見せなかった。　<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0When I approached that boy, you did not so much as move.<end>When I approached that girl, you did not so much as move.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0내가 그 소년에게 다가갔을 때 너는 움직일 기색조차 보이지 않았다.<end>내가 그 소녀에게 다가갔을 때 너는 움직일 기색조차 보이지 않았다.<end>"

## 890464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私があの子を殺し、<line-break>お前をもとの冷酷な円卓騎士に<line-break>戻そうとするとは考えなかったか？<end>"
EN: "Did it never occur to you that I might kill that child and turn you back into the merciless Knight of the Round Table you once were?<end>"
KO: "내가 그 아이를 죽이고 너를 원래의 냉혹한 원탁기사로 되돌리려 할 거라고 생각하지 않았나?<end>"

## 890465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、考えなかったわ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>愛を知ったあなたが、<line-break>失うということの痛みを前に、<line-break>踏みとどまらないわけがないもの。<end>"
EN: "No, it did not. Now that you know love, I knew you would stop rather than face the pain of losing it.<end>"
KO: "그래, 생각하지 않았어. 사랑을 알게 된 네가 잃는 고통 앞에서 멈추지 않을 리 없으니까.<end>"

## 890466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…運命は変えられぬ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お前のしていることは、<line-break>無駄なことだぞ、アスティア。<end>"
EN: "Fate cannot be changed. What you are doing is futile, Astia.<end>"
KO: "…운명은 바꿀 수 없다. 네가 하는 일은 헛된 짓이다, 아스티아.<end>"

## 890467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は警告に来ただけだ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>己が力を過信するな。<line-break>個の力など、運命の前には無力だ。<end>"
EN: "I came only to warn you. Do not place too much faith in your own power. One person's power is nothing before fate.<end>"
KO: "나는 경고하러 왔을 뿐이다. 자신의 힘을 과신하지 마라. 개인의 힘 따위 운명 앞에서는 무력하다.<end>"

## 890468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、それはどうかしら？<end>"
EN: "Are you so certain?<end>"
KO: "글쎄, 그건 어떨까?<end>"

## 890469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァシュタールを目覚めさせることが<line-break>できるのは私だけ。<line-break> <line-break>そして、闇の門を開けられるのは<line-break>ヴァシュタールだけ。<end>"
EN: "I alone can awaken Vashtar. And only Vashtar can open the Dark Gate.<end>"
KO: "바슈타르를 깨울 수 있는 건 나뿐. 그리고 어둠의 문을 열 수 있는 건 바슈타르뿐이다.<end>"

## 890471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ヴァシュタールの柩に<line-break>近づく者がある。<line-break> <line-break>奴の気は千変万化して<line-break>正体をつかませぬ。<line-break>気をつけることだ。<end>"
EN: "Someone is approaching Vashtar's coffin. The intruder's aura is ever-changing, making the identity behind it impossible to discern. Be on your guard.<end>"
KO: "…바슈타르의 관에 다가가는 자가 있다. 녀석의 기운은 천변만화해서 정체를 잡을 수 없어. 조심해라.<end>"

## 890472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…。<line-break>では、隣の間でお待ちください。<line-break>支度をして、すぐに行きます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you... Please wait in the next room. I'll get ready and join you shortly.<end>"
KO: "고마워…. 그럼 옆방에서 기다려 줘. 준비하고 바로 갈게.<end>"

## 890473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また…<line-break>目覚めてしまったのか…？<end>"
EN: "Have I awakened again...?<end>"
KO: "또… 깨어나 버린 건가…?<end>"

## 890474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよ、破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士<line-break>ヴァシュタール。<end>"
EN: "Morning, Vashtar, Knight of the Round Table in service to Ulugh, God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 바슈타르.<end>"

## 890475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ…私を呼び覚ました。<end>"
EN: "Why have you awakened me?<end>"
KO: "왜… 나를 깨운 거지.<end>"

## 890476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神降臨の通路、闇の門を<line-break>開けるのが君の役目でしょ。<line-break>他に芸があるの？<end>"
EN: "Your job is to open the Dark Gate, the path by which the God of Destruction descends. What else are you good for?<end>"
KO: "파괴신 강림의 통로인 어둠의 문을 여는 게 네 역할이잖아. 다른 재주라도 있어?<end>"

## 890477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…虚無の子か。<line-break>何を考えている？<line-break>　<line-break>神が去ったこの世界で、<line-break>ウルグ様が復活することが<line-break>どういうことかわかっているのか？<end>"
EN: "The Child of the Void... What are you planning? Do you understand what Lord Ulugh's return would mean in this godless world?<end>"
KO: "…허무의 아이인가. 무슨 생각이지? 신이 떠난 이 세계에서 울그 님이 부활한다는 게 무슨 뜻인지 알고 있나?<end>"

## 890478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おもしろいことさ。<end>"
EN: "Something interesting.<end>"
KO: "재미있는 일이야.<end>"

## 890479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、よかろう。時は満ちている。<line-break>闇の門を開けるのは、<line-break>開け放つものである私の役目だ。<line-break>だが…まだ、力が足りぬ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Very well. The time has come. I am the one charged with throwing open the Dark Gate. But I still lack the power.<end>"
KO: "후후, 좋다. 때가 무르익었다. 어둠의 문을 여는 건, 문을 열어젖히는 자인 내 역할이다. 하지만… 아직 힘이 부족하다.<end>"

## 890480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーに言ってんの！<line-break>この町を作ったのは、なんのため？<line-break> <line-break>たくさん、人間を飼っておいて<line-break>目覚めたときに、ソウルを刈り取って<line-break>力を吸収するためでしょ？<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? Why do you think this town was built? You kept all these humans penned up so you could harvest their Souls when you awoke and absorb their power, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "무슨 소리야! 이 마을을 만든 이유가 뭐였는데? 인간을 잔뜩 길러 뒀다가 깨어났을 때 소울을 거둬 힘을 흡수하려고 한 거잖아?<end>"

## 890481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、さくさくソウルを<line-break>吸い取っちゃってよ。<line-break>ヴァシュタール。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Vashtar. Hurry up and drain those Souls.<end>"
KO: "자, 얼른 소울을 쭉쭉 빨아들여. 바슈타르.<end>"

## 890482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや、もうジャマがはいるなんて。<line-break>先が思いやられるよ。<end>"
EN: "My, someone's interfering already. This doesn't bode well.<end>"
KO: "이런, 벌써 방해가 들어오다니. 앞날이 걱정되는군.<end>"

## 890483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は！<line-break>虚無の子？<line-break>なんてこと……！<end>"
EN: "You! The Child of the Void? No...!<end>"
KO: "너는! 허무의 아이? 이럴 수가……!<end>"

## 890484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士<line-break>告げる者アスティアよ。<line-break>お前の背信のもくろみは失敗だ。<end>"
EN: "Astia the Herald, Knight of the Round Table in service to Ulugh, God of Destruction. Your treacherous scheme has failed.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사, 고하는 자 아스티아여. 네 배신 계획은 실패다.<end>"

## 890485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…。<end>"
EN: "No...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가….<end>"

## 890486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうあがこうと<line-break>運命には逆らえない。<end>"
EN: "Struggle all you like. You cannot defy fate.<end>"
KO: "아무리 발버둥 쳐도 운명에는 거스를 수 없어.<end>"

## 890487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ。アスティア。<line-break>与えられた仕事があるんだから<line-break>きちんとやらないとね。<line-break>おや？　誰か来るみたいだね。<line-break>ばれたら大変だ。<line-break>じゃあね、アスティア。<end>"
EN: "That's right, Astia. You were given a job, so you ought to do it properly. Oh? Looks like someone's coming. It would be bad if anyone found out. See you, Astia.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아스티아. 맡은 일이 있으니 제대로 해야지. 어라? 누가 오는 것 같네. 들키면 큰일이야. 그럼 안녕, 아스티아.<end>"

## 890488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、逃げなさい！<line-break>この町を出るの！<line-break>私もすぐにあとを追うわ！<end>"

## 890489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐにあとを追う、だと？<line-break>フッ、笑止な。<line-break>実力を知れ…、背信者アスティアよ。<end>"
EN: "Right behind, you say? Hah. Absurd. Know your limits, Astia the Betrayer.<end>"
KO: "곧바로 뒤쫓겠다고? 흥, 우습군. 네 실력을 알아라… 배신자 아스티아여.<end>"

## 890490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い！　逃げて！<end>"
EN: "Please! Run!<end>"
KO: "부탁이야! 도망쳐!<end>"

## 890491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、無駄だ。<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主でも、<line-break>私に勝つことはできん。<line-break>その偉大なソウルを我に奉げるがよい。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Futile. Even one who possesses an Infinite Soul cannot defeat me. Offer that magnificent Soul unto me.<end>"
KO: "흥, 소용없다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자라도 나를 이길 수는 없다. 그 위대한 소울을 나에게 바쳐라.<end>"

## 890492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今まで黙っていてごめんなさい、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry I kept this from you all this time, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "지금까지 말하지 않아서 미안해, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 890493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私は、破壊神に仕える魔人。<line-break>円卓騎士、告げる者アスティア。<line-break> <line-break>何千年という時を、私は、<line-break>戦いながら生きてきた…。<end>"
EN: "I am a demon in service to the God of Destruction: Astia the Herald, Knight of the Round Table. I have lived for thousands of years, fighting all the while...<end>"
KO: "…나는 파괴신을 섬기는 마인. 원탁기사, 고하는 자 아스티아. 수천 년이라는 세월을 나는 싸우며 살아왔다….<end>"

## 890494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、お前と過ごした１６年…<line-break>私は初めて安らぎを知った…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>強く…生きて…。<end>"
EN: "But in the sixteen years I spent with you, I knew peace for the first time. <value:$28>, be strong... and live...<end>"
KO: "하지만 너와 보낸 16년… 나는 처음으로 평온을 알았다…. <value:$28>, 강하게… 살아….<end>"

## 890495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分の身を捨ててまで逃がすか…<line-break>アスティアよ、母とは強いものだな。<end>"
EN: "You would sacrifice yourself to let another escape? Astia, a mother's strength is formidable indeed.<end>"
KO: "자신을 희생해서까지 도망치게 하나… 아스티아여, 어머니란 강한 존재구나.<end>"

## 890496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや～、はやいもんだよねぇ。<line-break>もう、バイアシオン大陸なんて<line-break>ちっちゃくて見えないわ。<line-break>あんな、ちっぽけな陸の上で<line-break>あたしたちは戦ってたんだよね。<line-break>ここからこっちは自分の国だって。<end>"
EN: "Well, that was quick, wasn't it? The Vyashion Continent's already too small to see. And to think we fought on that tiny scrap of land, saying, “This side is our country.”<end>"
KO: "이야~ 빠르네. 벌써 바이아시온 대륙은 작아서 안 보일 정도야. 저 조그만 땅 위에서 우리는 싸우고 있었던 거지. 여기서부터는 자기 나라라고 말하면서.<end>"

## 890497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<line-break>しかし、伯爵様、よろしいのですか？<line-break>お兄君のエリエナイ公は…<line-break>い、いえ、出過ぎだ発言でした。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see Her Majesty the Queen. Very well. You may pass. But, my lord, are you certain? Your elder brother, Duke Elienai... N-no, forgive me. I spoke out of turn.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 들어가십시오. 하지만 백작님, 괜찮으십니까? 형님이신 에리에나이 공은…. 아, 아닙니다. 제가 주제넘은 말을 했습니다.<end>"

## 890498
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see Her Majesty the Queen. Very well. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 890499
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様に会うのか。<line-break>よかろう、話は承っている。<line-break>通るとよい。<end>"
EN: "You seek an audience with Her Majesty? Very well. I have been informed. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가는가. 좋다, 이야기는 들었다. 지나가라.<end>"

## 890500
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>無理だな。王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<line-break>なに、総司令閣下の書状が…。<line-break>あ、それなら、承っております。<line-break>どうぞ、ご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "You want to see Her Majesty? Not a chance. The Queen is busy. Now off with you! What? A letter from the Commander-in-Chief...? Ah, yes, I was told about it. Please allow me to show you in.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나고 싶다고? 안 돼. 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가! 뭐, 총사령관 각하의 서신이…? 아, 그거라면 전달받았습니다. 이쪽으로 안내하겠습니다.<end>"

## 890501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様はお忙しく、<line-break>どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<line-break>は？　ファーロス総司令閣下の書状…。<line-break>あ！　そういえば…。失礼しました！<line-break>それではご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but Her Majesty is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave. What? A letter from Commander-in-Chief Pharos...? Ah! Of course. Forgive me! I will escort you at once.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구와도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오…. 네? 파로스 총사령관 각하의 서신…. 아! 그러고 보니…. 실례했습니다! 그럼 안내해 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 890502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>無理だな。王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<end>"
EN: "You want to see Her Majesty? Not a chance. The Queen is busy. Now off with you!<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나고 싶다고? 무리다. 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가!<end>"

## 890503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様はお忙しく、<line-break>どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but Her Majesty is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구와도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 890504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た、フリントの息子<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>今日は何用か？<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>, son of Flint. What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, 플린트의 아들 <value:$28>이여. 오늘은 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 890505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにごとだ。<line-break>今は、王宮は大変なのだ。<line-break>用なら、あとにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "What is it? The palace has enough to deal with right now. If you have business here, it will have to wait.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이냐. 지금 왕궁은 매우 바쁘다. 볼일이라면 나중에 와라.<end>"

## 890506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ファーロス総司令が貴族を召集しています。<line-break>どうぞ、謁見の間へ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Commander-in-Chief Pharos is assembling the nobles. Please proceed to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 파로스 총사령관께서 귀족들을 소집하고 계십니다. 알현실로 가 주세요.<end>"

## 890507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "謁見の間へ行く<end>"
EN: "Go to the audience chamber<end>"
KO: "알현실로 간다<end>"

## 890508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "謁見の間へ行かない<end>"
EN: "Don't go to the audience chamber<end>"
KO: "알현실에는 가지 않는다<end>"

## 890509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、どうぞ。<line-break>お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Very well. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 890510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<end>"
EN: "...I see.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까.<end>"

## 890511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帰れ帰れ。<line-break>ここは、薄汚い冒険者の<line-break>うろついていい場所じゃないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Go on, get out of here. This is no place for filthy adventurers to wander about.<end>"
KO: "돌아가, 돌아가. 여긴 꾀죄죄한 모험자가 돌아다녀도 되는 곳이 아니야.<end>"

## 890512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "中へ？　ならんならん。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>陛下は、貴族方とも<line-break>お会いになっていないのだ。<line-break>お前ごときを通すわけにはいかん。<end>"
EN: "Inside? Absolutely not. The sovereign will not even receive the nobles. Someone like you certainly won't be allowed through.<end>"
KO: "안으로? 안 돼, 안 돼. 폐하께서는 귀족들과도 만나지 않고 계신다. 너 같은 자를 들여보낼 수는 없어.<end>"

## 890513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>恐れながら、お通しできません。<line-break>陛下のご気分が優れないとかで…。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I regret that I cannot let you pass. I am told the sovereign is indisposed.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 송구하지만 들여보내 드릴 수 없습니다. 폐하의 상태가 좋지 않으시다 해서….<end>"

## 890514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>雨が降ろうが槍が降ろうが、<line-break>ここに城がある限り、<line-break>我々はここを守り続ける所存です。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Come rain or spears, so long as this castle stands, we shall stand guard over it.<end>"
KO: "이런, <value:$28>님. 비가 오든 창이 쏟아지든 이 성이 있는 한 저희는 계속 이곳을 지킬 생각입니다.<end>"

## 890515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王宮の怪異を調べに？<line-break>しかし…<line-break>陛下からは誰も通すなと…。<end>"
EN: "You mean to investigate the strange occurrences in the palace? But... the sovereign has ordered that no one be allowed through.<end>"
KO: "왕궁의 괴이를 조사하러? 하지만… 폐하께선 아무도 들이지 말라고…<end>"

## 890516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "説得を続ける<end>"
EN: "Keep trying to persuade<end>"
KO: "설득을 계속한다<end>"

## 890517
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あきらめる<end>"
EN: "Give up<end>"
KO: "포기한다<end>"

## 890518
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、陛下の命令です。<line-break>逆らうわけには…。<end>"
EN: "But it is the sovereign's order. I cannot disobey...<end>"
KO: "하지만 폐하의 명령입니다. 거역할 수는….<end>"

## 890519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、出た！<line-break>ば、化け物、が！<end>"
EN: "I-it's here! A m-monster!<end>"
KO: "나, 나왔다! 괴, 괴물이!<end>"

## 890520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなバカな！？<line-break>あれは単なる噂ではないのか！<end>"
EN: "Th-that can't be! I thought it was only a rumor!<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 리가!? 그건 그냥 소문 아니었어!?<end>"

## 890521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、嘘じゃない。<line-break>な、仲間が中で！<end>"
EN: "I-it's not a lie. M-my comrades are in there!<end>"
KO: "거, 거짓말 아니야. 도, 동료가 안에 있어!<end>"

## 890522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わかりました。<line-break>私たちは負傷者の搬出にあたります。<end>"
EN: "U-understood. We will see to the evacuation of the wounded.<end>"
KO: "아, 알겠습니다. 저희는 부상자 반출을 맡겠습니다.<end>"

## 890523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申しわけございません。<line-break>ですが、陛下のご命令ですので。<end>"
EN: "My deepest apologies, but these are the sovereign's orders.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 하지만 폐하의 명령이라서요.<end>"

## 890524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、王宮は大騒ぎなのだ。<line-break>さあ、行った行った！<end>"
EN: "The royal palace is in an uproar right now. Go on, get moving!<end>"
KO: "지금 왕궁은 큰 소동이다. 자, 어서 가!<end>"

## 890525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>今日はどうなされたのです？<end>"

KO: "<value:$28>님이시군요. 오늘은 무슨 일로 오셨습니까?<end>"
