## 890540
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの魔の手が迫ってきて…。<line-break>いったい、これから<line-break>どうなるのでしょう、伯爵様。<end>"
EN: "Dyneskal's clutches are closing in on us... What will become of us now, my lord Count?<end>"
KO: "딘갈의 마수가 다가오고 있습니다…. 대체 앞으로 어떻게 되는 걸까요, 백작님.<end>"

## 890541
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 890542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様の所に<line-break>いらっしゃるのですね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You're here to see Tiana, then. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님께 가시는군요. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 890543
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様の所に<line-break>いらっしゃるのですね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You're here to see Atleia, then. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님께 가시는군요. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 890544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様はお忙しく、<line-break>どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but Her Majesty is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구와도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 890545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がエリスだ。<line-break>なんの用か？<end>"
EN: "I am Eris. What do you want?<end>"
KO: "내가 에리스다. 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 890546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダム解放軍への援助要請か。<line-break>あいにくと事態はさほど悠長ではない。<line-break>あまり興味はないな。<end>"
EN: "You've come to ask for aid for the Liberdam Liberation Army? I'm afraid matters are far too urgent for that. It holds little interest for me.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤 해방군에 대한 지원 요청인가. 유감이지만 상황은 그렇게 한가하지 않아. 별로 관심 없군.<end>"

## 890547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それよりも、ものは相談だが、<line-break>次の戦に参戦してはくれぬか？<line-break>カルラ進軍のこと、聞いていよう？<line-break>そなたが、こちらの言うことを<line-break>聞いてくれるなら、こちらも<line-break>援助の件、引き受けよう。いかがか？<end>"
EN: "Instead, I have a proposition for you. Will you join us in the next battle? You have heard of Karula's Advance, I presume. If you agree to our request, we will agree to provide the aid you seek. What say you?<end>"
KO: "그보다 상담할 게 있는데 다음 전투에 참가해 주지 않겠나? 카를라 진군 이야기는 들었겠지? 네가 우리 말을 들어준다면 우리도 지원 건을 받아들이겠다. 어떤가?<end>"

## 890548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 890549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 890550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、引き受けてくれるか。　<line-break>ありがたい。<line-break>今回の戦、かなり分が悪くてな。<line-break>その上、レムオン坊やが、<line-break>貴族をまとめて、今回の戦には<line-break>参加せぬと申しておる。<end>"
EN: "So you will accept. You have my thanks. The odds are decidedly against us in this battle. To make matters worse, young Lemghon has rallied the nobles and declared that they will take no part in it.<end>"
KO: "그래, 맡아 주겠나. 고맙군. 이번 전투는 꽤 불리해서 말이야. 게다가 레무온 도련님이 귀족들을 모아 이번 전쟁엔 참가하지 않겠다고 하고 있어.<end>"

## 890551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆえに、ファーロス家の軍だけで<line-break>立ち向かう。しかし、将星も乏しい。<line-break>そなたのような人材を欲していた。<line-break>頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>我が甥、ゼネテスの力になってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Thus, the forces of House Pharos must face the enemy alone. Yet we have precious few capable commanders. We need someone of your caliber. I am counting on you, <value:$28>. Lend your strength to my nephew Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "그러므로 파로스 가문의 군대만으로 맞선다. 하지만 장수도 부족하다. 너 같은 인재가 필요했다. 부탁한다, <value:$28>. 내 조카 제네테스에게 힘이 되어 다오.<end>"

## 890552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、残念だな。<line-break>それならば、こちらも援助できぬ。<line-break>下がるがよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. A pity. In that case, we cannot grant your request for aid. You may withdraw.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 유감이군. 그렇다면 이쪽도 지원할 수 없다. 물러가라.<end>"

## 890553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、あの神速の戦術家カルラが<line-break>勇猛なディンガル兵をわんさか連れて<line-break>こっちに向かってるそうだ。<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんとの戦いで<line-break>こっちは兵力をあらかた失ってる上に<line-break>レムオンのダンナがな…。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Word is Karula, that lightning-fast tactician, is heading this way with a whole horde of fierce Dyneskal soldiers. We lost nearly all our troops fighting old Angeerdan, and now Lord Lemghon has gone and...<end>"
KO: "이봐, 그 신속의 전략가 카를라가 용맹한 딘갈 병사들을 잔뜩 끌고 이쪽으로 온다는군. 앙길단 영감과의 싸움으로 우린 병력을 거의 다 잃은 데다 레무온 나리까지….<end>"

## 890554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族を集めて、兵を出さねえってさ。<line-break>んで、俺はファーロス家の兵だけで<line-break>カルラを迎え撃つことにした。<line-break>状況は最悪だ…つっても、<line-break>もっと悪くなるかもしれねえから<line-break>最悪じゃねえか。<end>"
EN: "He rounded up the nobles and said they wouldn't send any troops. So I've decided to meet Karula with the soldiers of House Pharos alone. Things couldn't be worse... except they could still get worse, so I suppose they could.<end>"
KO: "귀족들을 모아도 병력을 안 낸대. 그래서 나는 파로스 가문의 병사들만으로 카를라를 맞기로 했어. 상황은 최악이야… 라고 해도 더 나빠질 수 있으니 최악은 아닌가.<end>"

## 890555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、お腹ぺこぺこ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>この家からいい匂いがするよぅ。<line-break>おじゃましてみようかなぁ。<end>"
EN: "I'm starving. There's a delicious smell coming from this house. Maybe I'll invite myself in.<end>"
KO: "나 배고파 죽겠어. 이 집에서 좋은 냄새가 나. 한번 들어가 볼까.<end>"

## 890556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。　　<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<line-break>しかし、伯爵様、よろしいのですか？<line-break>お兄君のエリエナイ公は…<line-break>い、いえ、出過ぎだ発言でした。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see Her Majesty the Queen. Very well. You may pass. But, my lord Count, are you certain? Your elder brother, Duke Elienai... N-no, forgive me. I spoke out of turn.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 들어가십시오. 하지만 백작님, 괜찮으십니까? 형님이신 에리에나이 공은…. 아, 아닙니다. 제가 주제넘은 말을 했습니다.<end>"

## 890557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see Her Majesty the Queen. Very well. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 890558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様に会うのか。<line-break>よかろう、話は承っている。<line-break>通るとよい。<end>"
EN: "You seek an audience with Her Majesty? Very well. I have been informed. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가는가. 좋다, 이야기는 들었다. 지나가라.<end>"

## 890559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>らちもない。王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<line-break>なに、総司令閣下の書状が…。<line-break>あ、それなら、承っております。<line-break>どうぞ、ご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "You want to see Her Majesty? Nonsense. Her Majesty is busy. Off you go! What? A letter from His Excellency the Commander-in-Chief...? Ah, yes. I was told about that. Please allow me to escort you.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나고 싶다고? 말도 안 된다. 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가! 뭐, 총사령관 각하의 서신이…. 아, 그거라면 전달받았습니다. 이쪽으로 안내하겠습니다.<end>"

## 890560
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様には<line-break>お忙しく、どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<line-break>は？　ファーロス総司令閣下の書状…。<line-break>あ！　そういえば…。失礼しました！<line-break>それではご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but Her Majesty is busy and cannot receive anyone. I must ask you to leave. What? A letter from His Excellency, Commander-in-Chief Pharos...? Ah! Now that you mention it... Forgive me! I will escort you at once.<end>"
KO: "죄송하지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구도 만나실 수 없습니다. 돌아가 주십시오…. 네? 파로스 총사령관 각하의 서신…. 아! 그러고 보니…. 실례했습니다! 안내해 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 890561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>らちもない。王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<end>"
EN: "You want to see Her Majesty? Nonsense. Her Majesty is busy. Off you go!<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 뵙고 싶다고? 가당찮군. 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가 돌아가!<end>"

## 890562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テストようなのじゃよ。<line-break>今は時代０なんじゃ。<line-break>ひょっほっほっ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "테스트용이란다. 지금은 시대 0이야. 흐흐흐.<end>"

## 890563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１から５までの間で、<line-break>行きたい時代を選ぶんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Choose the era you want to visit, from 1 to 5.<end>"
KO: "1부터 5 사이에서 가고 싶은 시대를 고르거라.<end>"

## 890564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにごとだ。<line-break>今は、王宮は大変なのだ。<line-break>用なら、あとにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "What is it? The palace has enough to deal with right now. If you have business here, it'll have to wait.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이냐. 지금 왕궁은 매우 바쁘다. 볼일이라면 나중에 와라.<end>"

## 890570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王宮に何か用かね？<line-break>王宮に用事のない者がうろつくのは<line-break>感心しかねるな。<end>"
EN: "Do you have business at Rostorl Palace? I can't say I approve of people loitering around the palace when they have no business here.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕궁에 무슨 볼일이라도 있나? 볼일 없는 사람이 왕궁을 어슬렁거리는 건 좋지 않군.<end>"

## 890573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国王様まで崩御されてしまうとは…。<line-break>こうなってしまうと、<line-break>門衛という役目もつらいものです。<end>"
EN: "To think that even His Majesty has passed away... Now that things have come to this, it's hard being a gate guard.<end>"
KO: "국왕님까지 붕어하시다니…. 이렇게 되고 나니 문지기라는 역할도 괴롭군요.<end>"

## 890574

## 890579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな馬鹿な！？<line-break>あれは単なる噂ではないのか！<end>"
EN: "Th-that's impossible! Wasn't that just a rumor?<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 리가!? 그건 그냥 소문 아니었나!<end>"

## 890580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、嘘じゃない。<line-break>な、仲間が中で！<end>"
EN: "I-it's not a lie. M-my comrades are in there!<end>"
KO: "거, 거짓말 아니야. 도, 동료가 안에 있어!<end>"

## 890581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わかりました。<line-break>私達は負傷者の救出にあたります。<end>"
EN: "U-understood. We'll see to rescuing the wounded.<end>"
KO: "아, 알겠습니다. 저희는 부상자 구조에 나서겠습니다.<end>"

## 890582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申し訳ありませんが、<line-break>規則は規則ですので。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but rules are rules.<end>"
KO: "죄송하지만 규칙은 규칙이라서요.<end>"

## 890583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、王宮は大騒ぎなのだ。<line-break>さあ、行った行った！<end>"

KO: "지금 왕궁은 큰 소동이다. 자, 어서 가!<end>"

## 890599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの魔の手が迫ってきて…<line-break>いったい、これから<line-break>どうなるのでしょう、伯爵様。<end>"
EN: "With Dyneskal's clutches closing in... what will happen now, Count?<end>"
KO: "딩갈의 마수가 다가오고 있습니다…. 대체 앞으로 어떻게 되는 걸까요, 백작님.<end>"

## 890600
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 890601
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様の所に<line-break>いらっしゃるのですね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You're here to see Tiana, then. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님께 가시는군요. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 890602
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様の所に<line-break>いらっしゃるのですね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You're here to see Atleia, then. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님께 가시는군요. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 890603
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様には<line-break>お忙しく、どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but Her Majesty the Queen is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 890604
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がエリスだ。<line-break>なんの用か？<end>"
EN: "I am Eris. What do you want?<end>"
KO: "내가 에리스다. 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 890605
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダム解放軍への援助要請か。<line-break>あいにくと事態はさほど悠長ではない。<line-break>あまり興味はないな。<end>"
EN: "A request for aid for the Liberdam Liberation Army? Unfortunately, events leave no time for such things. I have little interest in it.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤 해방군에 대한 지원 요청인가. 유감이지만 상황은 그렇게 한가하지 않아. 별로 관심 없군.<end>"

## 890606
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ。<value:$28>様で<line-break>ございますね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください！<end>"
EN: "Yes. You must be <value:$28>. Please, go on through!<end>"
KO: "예. <value:$28> 님이시군요. 부디 지나가십시오!<end>"

## 890607
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>カルラ様がお呼びです。<line-break>謁見の間にお急ぎください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Karula has sent for you. Please hurry to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님! 카를라 님이 부르십니다. 알현실로 서둘러 주세요.<end>"

## 890608
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ。<value:$28>様で<line-break>ございますね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください！<end>"
EN: "Ah, you must be <value:$28>. Please, go on through!<end>"
KO: "예. <value:$28> 님이시군요. 부디 지나가십시오!<end>"

## 890609
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっ、貴様は、<value:$28>！<line-break>帝国を裏切っておきながら！<line-break>警備隊！　こいつを捕らえよ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you! <value:$28>! You have some nerve showing yourself after betraying the Empire! Guards! Seize this traitor!<end>"
KO: "네, 네놈은 <value:$28>! 제국을 배신하고도! 경비대! 이자를 붙잡아라!<end>"

## 890610
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっ、貴様は解放軍の<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>警備隊を呼べっ！　早く！<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28> of the Liberation Army! Call the guards! Hurry!<end>"

## 890634
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890635
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<line-break>猫屋敷ってとこで呼び出すってんだな。<line-break>なら、待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Right. So you'll call for me at a place called Cat Manor. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 불러내겠다는 거군. 그럼 기다리고 있지.<end>"

## 890636
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン、やめて！<line-break><line-break><line-break>あなたまで…あなたまで<line-break>死んでしまったら、わたし…！<end>"
EN: "Coffin, stop! If you die too... if I lose you too, I...!<end>"
KO: "카핀, 그만해! 너까지… 너까지 죽어 버리면, 나는…!<end>"

## 890637
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、お聞き。<line-break><line-break><line-break>レイヴンはあんたを守るために、<line-break>その命を差し出したんだ。<end>"
EN: "Listen to me, Noel. Raven gave his life to protect you.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 잘 들어. 레이븐은 너를 지키기 위해 목숨을 내놓은 거야.<end>"

## 890638
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの魂にそれだけの価値を見、<line-break>あんたに願いを託した…<end>"
EN: "He believed your soul was worth that sacrifice, and entrusted his hopes to you...<end>"
KO: "네 영혼에 그만한 가치를 보고, 네게 소원을 맡겼어….<end>"

## 890639
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのレイヴンの願いを<line-break>無駄にしろっていうのかい！？<end>"
EN: "And now you're going to let Raven's hopes go to waste?!<end>"
KO: "그 레이븐의 소원을 헛되게 하라는 거야!?<end>"

## 890640
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カ、カフィン！<end>"
EN: "C-Coffin!<end>"
KO: "카, 카핀!<end>"

## 890641
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦う前から負けたような声を<line-break>出すんじゃないよ、ノエル！<line-break><line-break>レイヴンとの冒険で、<line-break>何を学んだんだい！<end>"
EN: "Don't sound beaten before the fight has even begun, Noel! What did your adventures with Raven teach you?!<end>"
KO: "싸우기도 전에 진 것 같은 소리 하지 마, 노엘! 레이븐과의 모험에서 뭘 배웠어!<end>"

## 890643
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンは、こいつに<line-break>挑まないわけにはいかなかった。<line-break><line-break>こいつに喰われたシルヴァの人々に、<line-break>償いをするためにね！<end>"
EN: "Raven had no choice but to face this thing. He had to make amends to the people of Silva it devoured!<end>"
KO: "레이븐은 이 녀석에게 도전하지 않을 수 없었어. 이 녀석에게 먹힌 실바 사람들에게 속죄하기 위해서 말이야!<end>"

## 890644
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…一度動き出した運命の歯車を、<line-break>とめることはできない。<line-break><line-break>とめようと刃向かう者は…<line-break>その外へと弾き…飛ばされる…。<line-break>けど、そう定められていたとしても…。<end>"
EN: "Once the wheel of fate is set in motion, it cannot be stopped. Those who defy it and try to stop it... are cast... outside it. But even if that is what fate has decreed...<end>"
KO: "…한번 움직이기 시작한 운명의 톱니바퀴는 멈출 수 없어. 멈추려 거스르는 자는… 그 밖으로 튕겨… 날아가 버리지…. 하지만 그렇게 정해져 있었다 해도….<end>"

## 890645
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あくまで、どうするか選択する権利は、<line-break>あたしたち人にあるのよ！<end>"
EN: "No matter what, the right to choose what we do belongs to us humans!<end>"
KO: "어디까지나 어떻게 할지 선택할 권리는 우리 인간에게 있어!<end>"

## 890646
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…カ、カフィン…！<end>"
EN: "Oh... C-Coffin...!<end>"
KO: "아아… 카, 카핀…!<end>"

## 890647
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐっ…ぐううっ！<end>"
EN: "Ghh... grrrgh!<end>"
KO: "크윽… 으으윽!<end>"

## 890648
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "があああああっ！<end>"
EN: "Gaaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "가아아아아악!<end>"

## 890649
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…カフィンが変わっていく！<line-break>私の知らないカフィンに…。<end>"

KO: "아아… 카핀이 변해 가! 내가 모르는 카핀으로….<end>"

## 890650
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウオオオオッ！<end>"
EN: "Uooooooh!<end>"
KO: "우오오오옷!<end>"

## 890651
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ……！<end>"

KO: "윽……!<end>"

## 890652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャアアアア！<end>"
EN: "Aaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "꺄아아아아!<end>"

## 890653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カ、カフィン！！<end>"

KO: "카, 카핀!!<end>"

## 890654
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、無事かっ？<end>"
EN: "Noel, are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "노엘, 무사해!?<end>"

## 890655
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナーシェス、<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>カフィンが…！<end>"
EN: "Narscess! <value:$28>! Coffin...!<end>"
KO: "나셰스, <value:$28> 씨! 카핀이…!<end>"

## 890656
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遅かったか…。<end>"
EN: "Too late...?<end>"
KO: "늦었나….<end>"

## 890657
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この残り香…カフィンは<line-break>新月の香を使ってダルケニスの<line-break>力を解放したのだろう。<line-break>しかし、それでも…<line-break>ソウルイーターに<line-break>とどめは刺せなかった…。<end>"
EN: "This lingering scent... Coffin must have used the Scent of the New Moon to unleash the power of Darkenith. And even then... she could not finish off the Soul Eater...<end>"
KO: "이 잔향… 카핀은 신월의 향을 사용해 다르케니스의 힘을 해방한 거겠지. 하지만 그래도… 소울 이터에게 결정타를 주지는 못했어…<end>"

## 890658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、我々に退く道は<line-break>残されてはいないということか…。<end>"
EN: "So there is no path of retreat left to us...<end>"
KO: "이제 우리에게 물러설 길은 남아 있지 않다는 건가….<end>"

## 890659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ノエルは私が守る。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ソウルイーターは手負いだ、<line-break>今なら倒せるだろう。<line-break>頼んだぞ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I will protect Noel. The Soul Eater is wounded. You should be able to defeat it now. I'm counting on you!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 노엘은 내가 지킨다. 소울 이터는 부상당했다. 지금이라면 쓰러뜨릴 수 있을 거야. 부탁한다!<end>"

## 890660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>さん。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>ソウルイーターを倒してくれて。<line-break>…でも、ふたりが、<line-break>カフィンも、レイヴンも<line-break>目を開けてくれないんです…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Thank you for defeating the Soul Eater. But neither of them―Coffin or Raven―will open their eyes...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 씨. 감사합니다. 소울 이터를 쓰러뜨려 줘서. …하지만 두 사람, 카핀도 레이븐도 눈을 떠 주지 않아요….<end>"

## 890661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やっと、<line-break>ソウルイーターを倒したのに…。<line-break>こんなはずじゃ、なかったのに…。<line-break>わたし、何をやってるんだろう…<line-break>結局また大切な人たちを失って…。<end>"
EN: "We finally defeated the Soul Eater... This wasn't how it was supposed to end. What am I even doing? In the end, I've lost the people I love all over again...<end>"
KO: "…겨우 소울 이터를 쓰러뜨렸는데…. 이럴 리가 없었는데…. 나, 뭘 하고 있는 거지… 결국 또 소중한 사람들을 잃고….<end>"

## 890662
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890666
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890668
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シルヴァは以前より竜王様への<line-break>信仰が厚かった地。お前も幼少より、<line-break>竜王様に祈りを捧げてきたのだろう？<end>"

KO: "실바는 예전부터 용왕님에 대한 신앙이 깊은 땅이다. 너도 어릴 때부터 용왕님께 기도를 올려왔겠지?<end>"

## 890669
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "네? 아, 네. 늘 기도에는 참가했지만요…<end>"

## 890670
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのお前の願いなら、<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ<line-break>お前の願いであるのなら、<line-break>竜王様がお聞き届けに<line-break>なるやもしれぬ。<line-break>さぁ、願うがいい。<end>"

## 890671
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890676
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890677
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　だが、そのときは　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　汝は我の授けし使命を<line-break>　　　　　　　果たさねばならぬ。<line-break>　　　　　　　それでも、よいか？<end>"

## 890678

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890691

## 890713
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "友達だからだよ、ヴァン。<line-break>僕はある人に言われたんだ。<line-break> <line-break>復讐を果たしたときにどうするのか。<line-break>復讐したあとにどうするのか。<line-break>って…。<end>"

## 890714
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもね。いくら考えても何もないんだ。<line-break>何も浮かばなかったんだ。<line-break> <line-break>僕がガルドランを殺しても…。<line-break>ヴァンの怪我が良くなるワケじゃない。<line-break>僕が強くなるワケでもない。<end>"
EN: "But no matter how much I thought about it, there was nothing. I couldn't think of a single thing. Even if I killed Girldlan, it wouldn't heal your wounds. It wouldn't make me any stronger.<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야. 아무리 생각해도 아무것도 없었어. 아무것도 떠오르지 않았어. 내가 갈드란을 죽여도… 반의 상처가 나아지는 것도 아니고, 내가 강해지는 것도 아니야.<end>"

## 890715
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も…。<line-break>そう、何もないんだ。<line-break> <line-break>ただ、一つの命が奪われて<line-break>それを悲しむ人達がいて。<line-break>それに憤りを覚える人がいて。<end>"
EN: "Nothing... No, there'd be nothing. One life would be taken. People would grieve that loss, and someone would be outraged by it.<end>"
KO: "아무것도…. 그래, 아무것도 없어. 그저 하나의 생명이 빼앗기고, 그걸 슬퍼하는 사람들이 있고. 거기에 분노하는 사람이 있고.<end>"

## 890716
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一つの未来が、<line-break>たくさんの可能性が<line-break>失われてしまうんだ。永遠に。<line-break>そして、それが繰り返される。<line-break>ねぇ、ヴァン。<line-break>それが正しいことなのかな。<end>"
EN: "One future, with all its possibilities, would be lost forever. And then it would happen all over again. Tell me, Van... can that really be right?<end>"
KO: "하나의 미래와 수많은 가능성이 사라져 버리는 거야. 영원히. 그리고 그게 반복돼. 있지, 반. 그게 옳은 걸까.<end>"

## 890717
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕には…<line-break>僕には分からないんだ。<end>"
EN: "I... I don't know.<end>"
KO: "나는… 나는 모르겠어.<end>"

## 890718
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それは…<end>"
EN: "Th-that's...<end>"
KO: "그, 그건…<end>"

## 890719
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チッ！　仕方ねぇなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Tch. Fine, then.<end>"
KO: "쳇! 어쩔 수 없군.<end>"

## 890720
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランをこのヴァンが、<line-break>テラネのヴァンがヴァーンと倒した。<line-break>これで勘弁してやるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan was vanquished by Van―Van of Terane! That'll have to be enough.<end>"
KO: "갈드란을 이 반이, 테라네의 반이 빵 하고 쓰러뜨렸다. 이걸로 봐준다.<end>"

## 890721
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン！<end>"
EN: "Van!<end>"
KO: "반!<end>"

## 890722
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これは…友情の光！<line-break>友情の光が俺様を癒す。<end>"
EN: "Th-this is... the light of friendship! The light of friendship is healing me!<end>"
KO: "이, 이건… 우정의 빛! 우정의 빛이 이 몸을 치유한다!<end>"

## 890723
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様、復活！<end>"
EN: "I have returned!<end>"
KO: "이 몸, 부활!<end>"

## 890724
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けっ…もう、暴れんじゃねぇぞ。<end>"
EN: "Tch... Don't go running wild again.<end>"
KO: "쳇… 이제 난동 부리지 마.<end>"

## 890725
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "友情の光を放つ者達が悪のはずはない。<line-break>俺様は間違っていたようだ。<line-break>友情の力は俺様に力を与える！<line-break>正しい魂よ。<line-break>これからも俺様を応援してくれ！<line-break>さらばだ！<end>"
EN: "Those who shine with the light of friendship cannot be evil. It seems I was mistaken. The power of friendship fills me with strength! O righteous souls, continue to cheer me on! Farewell!<end>"
KO: "우정의 빛을 내는 자들이 악일 리 없다. 이 몸이 틀렸던 것 같군. 우정의 힘은 이 몸에게 힘을 준다! 올바른 영혼이여. 앞으로도 이 몸을 응원해 줘라! 잘 있어라!<end>"

## 890726
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おいっ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>…行っちまった。<line-break>あいつ、反省してるのか？<line-break>"
EN: "H-hey! He's gone... Did he actually learn anything?"
KO: "어, 어이! …가 버렸네. 저 녀석, 반성은 하는 건가?"

## 890727
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、終わったからいいか。<end>"
EN: "Well, it's over. That's what matters.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 끝났으니 됐나.<end>"

## 890728
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺も仲間に加えてくれ。<line-break>俺、お前らと一緒に旅がしたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "By the way, <value:$28>... let me join you. I want to travel with you.<end>"
KO: "그런데 <value:$28>…. 나도 동료로 받아 줘. 나, 너희와 함께 여행하고 싶어.<end>"

## 890729
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、ナッジ。<line-break>俺の怪我もよくなったし、<line-break>今度こそ俺と旅に出ないか？<end>"
EN: "By the way, Nadge, my injury's better now. Will you come travel with me this time?<end>"
KO: "그런데 나지. 내 상처도 다 나았고 이번엔 정말 나와 여행을 떠나지 않을래?<end>"

## 890730
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はいま、<line-break>この<value:$28>と<line-break>旅をしてるんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm traveling with <value:$28> now.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금 이 <value:$28>와 여행하고 있어.<end>"

## 890731
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<line-break><value:$28>か。<line-break><line-break>じゃ、よろしくな、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh. <value:$28>, huh? Well, good to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그런가. <value:$28>인가. 그럼 잘 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 890732
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>！<line-break>ヴァンには宿屋が…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! But Van has the inn...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>! 반에게는 여관이….<end>"

## 890733
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主たちも立つことはできまい？<line-break>もはや、破壊神の復活を<line-break>妨げることはできん。<line-break>お主たちもソウルを<line-break>ウルグ様にささげるがよい。<end>"
EN: "You cannot even stand, can you? It is too late to prevent the resurrection of the God of Destruction. Offer your Souls to Lord Ulugh as well.<end>"
KO: "너희도 일어설 수 없겠지? 이제 파괴신의 부활을 막을 수 없다. 너희도 소울을 울그 님께 바쳐라.<end>"

## 890734
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひさしいのう、ベルゼーヴァ。<line-break>結界をすでに張っておったとは。<line-break> <line-break>それを見抜けぬとは、<line-break>わしも、もうろくしたものじゃ。<end>"
EN: "It has been a long time, Beelzeva. To think you had already raised a barrier... I must be growing senile not to have seen through it.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이구나, 벨제바. 이미 결계를 쳐 두었다니. 그걸 간파하지 못하다니 나도 늙었구나.<end>"

## 890735
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　聞こえるか、<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "Can you hear me, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "들리느냐, <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 890736
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　探してもムダだ。余は汝の心に<line-break>　　　　直接、呼びかけておる。<line-break>　　　他の誰にも聞こえはせぬ。<line-break>                                <line-break>　　　  よいか、<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　汝はベルゼーヴァをつれて<line-break>　　　　脱出してはならん。<end>"
EN: "There is no use looking for me. I am speaking directly into your mind. No one else can hear me. Listen well, <value:$28>. You must not escape with Beelzeva.<end>"
KO: "찾아봐야 소용없다. 짐은 네 마음에 직접 말을 걸고 있다. 다른 누구에게도 들리지 않는다. 잘 들어라, <value:$28>. 너는 벨제바를 데리고 탈출해서는 안 된다.<end>"

## 890737
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　                           <line-break>　　　 　　フフフ。<line-break>　　　　なぜか、だと？<line-break>　                           <line-break>　                           <line-break>　　 ベルゼーヴァと取り引きをし、<line-break>　 余がその肉体を手に入れるためだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. You ask why? So that I may strike a bargain with Beelzeva and take possession of his body.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 왜냐고? 벨제바와 거래를 하고, 짐이 그 육체를 손에 넣기 위해서다.<end>"

## 890738
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　 汝が余と共に<line-break>　　人類革新の道を歩みたいのならば、<line-break>　　　 　余の指示通りにするのだ。<line-break>　                           <line-break>　<line-break>　　　　決してベルゼーヴァを<line-break>　　脱出させてはならぬ。わかったな。<end>"
EN: "If you wish to walk beside me on the path to Humanity's Evolution, then follow my instructions. You must never allow Beelzeva to escape. Is that understood?<end>"
KO: "그대가 나와 함께 인류 혁신의 길을 걷고 싶다면 내 지시대로 하라. 절대로 벨제바를 탈출시켜서는 안 된다. 알겠나.<end>"

## 890739
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ…。<line-break>多少、時間はかかったが、<line-break>すべては余の計画通りになった。<line-break>いよいよだぞ。<line-break>我らが人類の革新に<line-break>本格的に乗り出すのは…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... It took some time, but everything has unfolded according to my plan. At last, the moment has come for us to begin Humanity's Evolution in earnest...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 조금 시간은 걸렸지만 모든 것이 내 계획대로 되었다. 이제 시작이다. 우리 인류의 혁신에 본격적으로 나서는 것이….<end>"

## 890740
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、とりあえず余はディンガル軍に<line-break>戻り、宰相ベルゼーヴァとして<line-break>采配を振るとするか。<line-break>まずは全軍を魔道兵に仕立て、<line-break>戦力増強を図らねばならんからな。<line-break>フフフ…。<end>"
EN: "For now, I shall return to the Dyneskal Army and take command as Chancellor Beelzeva. My first task will be to turn the entire army into sorcerous troops and strengthen our forces. Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "자, 우선 짐은 딘갈군으로 돌아가 재상 벨제바로서 지휘를 맡도록 하지. 먼저 전군을 마도병으로 만들어 전력을 강화해야 하니까. 후후후….<end>"

## 900001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者登録かい？<line-break>そこに名前を書いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Here to register as an adventurer? Write your name there.<end>"
KO: "모험자 등록인가? 거기에 이름을 써 줘.<end>"

## 900002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻術計振れておらず<line-break>犯罪登録当てはまらず。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>おめでとう。<line-break>あんたも冒険者として<line-break>登録されたよ。<end>"
EN: "No reading on the illusion meter, and no match in the criminal registry. Congratulations. You're now registered as an adventurer too.<end>"
KO: "환술계 수치도 없고 범죄 등록에도 해당하지 않는군. 축하해. 너도 모험자로 등록됐어.<end>"

## 900003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名をあげていってくれよ。<line-break>がんばりな！<end>"
EN: "Now get out there, take on plenty of work, and make a name for yourself. Good luck!<end>"
KO: "이제부터 팍팍 일해서 이름을 날려 줘. 힘내!<end>"

## 900004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういえば、こんな噂知ってるかい？ 　<line-break>エンシャントの南東の賢者の森深くに 　<line-break>猫屋敷ってのがあるらしいんだがね。 　<line-break>これが不思議なことに、そこに行っても<line-break>誰も屋敷なんて見たことがねえんだ。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者だけ入れるんだとさ。<end>"
EN: "By the way, have you heard this rumor? There's supposed to be a manor called Cat Manor deep in the Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. But here's the strange part: no one who's gone there has ever seen a manor. The story goes that only those chosen by fate can enter.<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 이런 소문 아나? 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 고양이 저택이라는 곳이 있다는데 말이야. 이상하게도 거기 가 봐도 아무도 저택을 본 적이 없대. 운명에 선택받은 자만 들어갈 수 있다나 봐.<end>"

## 900005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不思議な話だろう？<line-break>誰も見たこともない屋敷の噂だなんて。<line-break>ま、運試しに行ってみたらどうだい。<end>"
EN: "Strange, isn't it? A rumor about a manor no one's ever seen. Well, why not go and try your luck?<end>"
KO: "이상한 이야기지? 아무도 본 적 없는 저택에 대한 소문이라니. 뭐, 운을 시험한다 생각하고 가 보는 게 어때.<end>"

## 900006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじょおさぁんん！！<line-break>君は幸せ者だ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今、君は俺様とつきあうチャンスを<line-break>手に入れたぞ。さあ、俺様の愛の胸に<line-break>飛び込んでくるがいい！<end>"
EN: "Young laaady! What a lucky girl you are! You've just been given the chance to go out with me. Now come, fling yourself into my loving arms!<end>"
KO: "아가씨이이!! 넌 행복한 사람이야. 지금 넌 이 몸과 사귈 기회를 손에 넣었다. 자, 이 몸의 사랑 가득한 품으로 뛰어들어 와!<end>"

## 900007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結構です。<end>"
EN: "No, thank you.<end>"
KO: "괜찮습니다.<end>"

## 900008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッハァー！<line-break>俺様の名を聞いても<line-break>そういう風に冷たくあしらえるかな。<end>"
EN: "Ha-ha! Let's see if you can still brush me off so coldly once you've heard my name.<end>"
KO: "핫하아! 이 몸의 이름을 듣고도 그렇게 차갑게 대할 수 있을까.<end>"

## 900009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様は未来の勇者、ガルドラン様だ。<line-break>さぁ、デートしたくなってきただろう？<end>"
EN: "I am the future Hero, the great Girldlan! You're starting to want that date now, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "이 몸은 미래의 용자 갈드란 님이다. 자, 데이트하고 싶어졌지?<end>"

## 900010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄が一緒ですから。<end>"
EN: "My brother is with me.<end>"
KO: "오빠가 함께 있으니까요.<end>"

## 900011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなアニキは捨て置け！<line-break>未来の勇者が許す！　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>君の瞳に俺様への恋の炎が燃えている。<line-break>未来の勇者にはガマンは無用さ！<line-break>俺様と甘いロマンの海に漕ぎ出すんだ！<end>"
EN: "Forget that brother of yours! The future Hero gives you leave! I see the flames of your love for me burning in your eyes. And the future Hero has no need for restraint! Come, set sail with me upon a sweet sea of romance!<end>"
KO: "그런 오빠는 내버려 둬! 미래의 용사가 허락한다! 네 눈동자에 나님을 향한 사랑의 불꽃이 타오르고 있어. 미래의 용사에게 참는 건 필요 없어! 나님과 달콤한 로망의 바다로 나아가자!<end>"

## 900012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄が来る前に<line-break>腕を放した方がいいですよ。<end>"
EN: "You should let go of my arm before my brother gets here.<end>"
KO: "오빠가 오기 전에 팔을 놓는 게 좋을 겁니다.<end>"

## 900013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は俺様を愛している。<line-break>いや、愛さなくてはいけないのだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>俺様の腕から熱い鼓動を感じ取れえ！<line-break>そして、君の愛の脈拍を<line-break>俺様の愛の脈拍と共鳴させるんだあ！<end>"
EN: "You love me. No, you must love me! Feel the fevered pulse in my arm! Then let the pulse of your love beat in harmony with mine!<end>"
KO: "넌 이 몸을 사랑하고 있다. 아니, 사랑해야만 한다. 이 몸의 팔에서 뜨거운 고동을 느껴라! 그리고 네 사랑의 맥박을 이 몸의 사랑의 맥박과 공명시키는 거다!<end>"

## 900014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹の手を放せ。<end>"
EN: "Let go of my sister's hand.<end>"
KO: "내 여동생 손 놔.<end>"

## 900015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？<line-break>俺様が未来の勇者ガルドランだと知って<line-break>そういうことを言ってるのか？<end>"
EN: "What? You know that I am Girldlan, the future Hero, and you still speak to me like that?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고? 이 몸이 미래의 용사 갈드란인 걸 알고도 그런 말을 하는 거냐?<end>"

## 900016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前が未来の勇者だというなら、<line-break>この世界の未来は見たくないな。<end>"
EN: "If you're the future Hero, I'd rather not see the future of this world.<end>"
KO: "…네가 미래의 용자라면 이 세계의 미래는 보고 싶지 않군.<end>"

## 900017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　未来の勇者をバカにしたな！<line-break>正義の鉄槌を食らいたいようだな！　<line-break>　<line-break>よかろう！　未来の勇者である俺様が<line-break>チンケな悪役のセリフはやっちまえと<line-break>覚えてろで十分なことを教えてやる！<end>"
EN: "Gah! You dare mock the future Hero! It seems you're eager to taste the hammer of justice! Very well! I, the future Hero, shall teach you that a third-rate villain needs only two lines: “Get him!” and “You'll pay for this!”<end>"
KO: "큭! 미래의 용사를 바보 취급했겠다! 정의의 철퇴를 맞고 싶은 모양이군! 좋아! 미래의 용사인 나님이 하찮은 악당의 대사는 '두고 보자'와 '기억해 둬라'면 충분하다는 걸 가르쳐 주마!<end>"

## 900018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹を侮辱するからだ。<end>"
EN: "That's for insulting my sister.<end>"
KO: "여동생을 모욕했기 때문이다.<end>"

## 900019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。兄さん。<line-break>その技…、使いたくないんでしょう？<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, Brother. You didn't want to use that technique, did you?<end>"
KO: "미안해요, 오빠. 그 기술… 쓰고 싶지 않은 거죠?<end>"

## 900020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前たちのためなら、いいさ。<line-break>それより大丈夫か？<end>"
EN: "For all of you, I don't mind. More importantly, are you all right?<end>"
KO: "너희를 위해서라면 괜찮아. 그것보다 괜찮냐?<end>"

## 900021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec090-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 910001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec091-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちんけな紙芝居だったよなぁ。<end>"
EN: "That was one pathetic little picture-card show, huh?<end>"
KO: "시시한 종이연극이었지.<end>"

## 910002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec091-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今時、人情もの、<line-break>しかも兄妹ものなんてダサダサだよな。<end>"
EN: "A sentimental story about a brother and sister, in this day and age? It doesn't get any lamer.<end>"
KO: "요즘 세상에 인정물, 그것도 남매 이야기는 너무 촌스럽잖아.<end>"

## 910003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec091-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供だと思ってバカにしてんじゃん？<end>"
EN: "What, being a kid means it's okay to treat someone like an idiot?<end>"
KO: "애라고 무시하는 거지?<end>"

## 910004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec091-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょーがねーよなー。<line-break>ま、あの程度だよね。<end>"
EN: "What can you do? It was about as good as you'd expect.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없지 뭐. 뭐, 그 정도였네.<end>"

## 910005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec091-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不覚だぜ…。<line-break>この俺が…涙なんて…くっ。<end>"
EN: "Damn, I let it get to me... Me, shedding tears... Ngh.<end>"
KO: "실수했군…. 이 내가… 눈물이라니… 큭.<end>"

## 920000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デルガド　Ｖ１　開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "델가드 V1 시작<end>"

## 920001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "刀工デルガドをお探しかい？<line-break>あそこで飲んだくれてるのがご当人さ。<line-break>なんの用か知らんが、剣は打てないよ。<end>"
EN: "Looking for Dergado the swordsmith? That's him over there, drinking himself senseless. Don't know what you want with him, but he can't forge swords.<end>"
KO: "도공 델가도를 찾나? 저기서 술에 절어 있는 사람이 본인이야. 무슨 볼일인진 몰라도 검은 못 만들어.<end>"

## 920002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、あのザマじゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Not anymore. Not in the state he's in.<end>"
KO: "이제 저 꼴이니 말이지.<end>"

## 920003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほれ、あそこ見てみな。　　　　　　　<line-break>あれがデルガドだ。<line-break>　<line-break>昔は当代一の刀工とたたえられた男が、<line-break>何があったのかは知らないが、<line-break>今はただのへそまがりな酔っぱらいさ。<end>"
EN: "See that man over there? That's Dergado. He was once hailed as the finest swordsmith of his day. I don't know what happened to him, but now he's just a cantankerous drunk.<end>"
KO: "자, 저기 봐. 저자가 델가도야. 옛날엔 당대 최고의 도공이라 칭송받던 남자인데 무슨 일이 있었는지는 몰라도 지금은 그냥 심술궂은 술주정뱅이지.<end>"

## 920004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっかいな客で迷惑してるんだ。<line-break>ま、気をつけて、あいつと<line-break>面倒は起こさないようにしてくれ。　　<line-break>特に刀とか鍛冶とかの話はご法度だぜ。<line-break>前にその手の話をした時は、店の中を<line-break>めちゃくちゃにされたからな。<end>"
EN: "He's a troublesome customer and nothing but a nuisance. So watch yourself, and try not to get into any trouble with him. Above all, don't mention swords or smithing. The last time that subject came up, he wrecked the place.<end>"
KO: "골치 아픈 손님이라 곤란해. 아무튼 조심해서 그 녀석과 문제는 일으키지 마. 특히 검이나 대장장이 이야기는 금물이야. 전에 그런 이야기를 꺼냈다가 가게 안을 엉망으로 만들어 놨거든.<end>"

## 920005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい。<line-break>今日は珍しくデルガド爺さんが<line-break>来てないから、ゆっくりできるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. Old Dergado isn't here today for once, so you can take it easy.<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 오늘은 드물게 델가도 영감이 안 와서 느긋하게 있을 수 있어.<end>"

## 920006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デルガド師匠に用かい？<line-break>たぶん、酒場で飲んだくれてんだろ？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>自分の名を銘に入れた剣デルガドが<line-break>ネメアに折られちまってから<line-break>剣が打てねえって、あの調子だよ。<end>"
EN: "Looking for Master Dergado? He's probably at the tavern drinking himself senseless. Ever since Nemea broke Dergado―the sword bearing his own name―he's been like this, saying he can't forge another sword.<end>"
KO: "델가도 스승님한테 볼일 있어? 아마 술집에서 퍼마시고 있겠지. 자기 이름을 새긴 검 델가도가 네메아에게 부러진 뒤로 검을 못 만들겠다며 저 모양이야.<end>"

## 920007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当代随一の刀工なんて持ち上げられて、　　　<line-break>それが、いまや、ただの酔っぱらいさ。<line-break>天才は、逆境に弱いからねえ。<line-break>酒場にいなきゃ、どっか、ふらついてるのさ。<line-break>帰ってくるまで、結構かかるから<line-break>出直して来た方がいいよ。<end>"
EN: "He was once hailed as the finest swordsmith of the age, and now he's nothing but a drunk. Geniuses don't handle adversity well. If he isn't at the tavern, he's probably wandering around somewhere. It'll be a while before he gets back, so you'd better come back later.<end>"
KO: "당대 최고의 도공이라고 추켜세워졌는데 지금은 그냥 술주정뱅이야. 천재는 역경에 약한 법이니까. 술집에 없으면 어딘가 돌아다니는 중일 거야. 돌아오기까지 꽤 걸리니 나중에 다시 오는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 920008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、剣を打ってくれっつっても、<line-break>あれじゃ、無理だろうけどねえ。<line-break>え？　師匠に用があったんじゃないの？　　　<end>"
EN: "Though even if you asked him to forge a sword, I doubt he could in that state. Huh? You didn't come to see Master Dergado?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 검을 만들어 달라고 해도 저 상태론 무리겠지만. 응? 스승님께 볼일이 있었던 거 아니야?<end>"

## 920009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、わしがデルガドじゃ。<line-break>若いの、デルガドを知っとるか？<end>"
EN: "Hmm. I'm Dergado. Tell me, youngster―have you heard of Dergado?<end>"
KO: "흠, 내가 델가도다. 젊은이, 델가도를 아나?<end>"

## 920010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当代一とたたえられた刀工<end>"
EN: "A swordsmith hailed as the finest of the age<end>"
KO: "당대 최고라 칭송받던 도공<end>"

## 920011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただの酔っぱらい<end>"
EN: "Just a drunk<end>"
KO: "그냥 술주정뱅이<end>"

## 920012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの略奪の時、老人子供を虐殺した剣<end>"
EN: "The sword that slaughtered the elderly and children during the sack of Rocen<end>"
KO: "로센 약탈 때 노인과 아이를 학살한 검<end>"

## 920013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッ！　当代一の刀工じゃと？<line-break>当代一の刀工はこんな風に話すのか？<line-break>こんな風に酒浸りの毎日を送るのか？<line-break>では、わしはいったい誰なんじゃ？<end>"
EN: "Hah! The finest swordsmith of this age, you say? Would the finest swordsmith of this age talk like this? Would he spend every day drowning himself in drink? Then who in blazes am I?<end>"
KO: "흥! 당대 최고의 도공이라고? 최고의 도공이 이렇게 말하나? 매일 술에 절어 사나? 그럼 나는 대체 누구지?<end>"

## 920014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デルガドの影だよ。<end>"

## 920015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ、リルビーめ！<line-break>お前なんぞには話しかけとらん。<line-break>リルビーらしく歌でも歌っとれ！<end>"
EN: "Shut up, you Lilubee! I wasn't talking to the likes of you. Go sing something, like a Lilubee should!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐라, 릴비 녀석! 너 같은 놈한테 말 건 게 아니다. 릴비답게 노래나 부르고 있어!<end>"

## 920016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リルビーじゃないよ。<line-break>レルラ＝ロントンって名前が　　　<line-break>あるんだから。　<line-break>自分は名前だけで決めてかかられて<line-break>怒ってるくせに、僕のことは<line-break>種族だけで決めてかかるの？<end>"
EN: "I'm not “Lilubee.” I have a name: Relra-Lonton. You get angry when people judge you by nothing but your name, yet you judge me by nothing but my race?<end>"
KO: "릴비가 아니야. 레를라=론톤이라는 이름이 있다고. 자기는 이름만 보고 단정 지으면 화내면서, 나는 종족만 보고 단정 짓는 거야?<end>"

## 920017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふん。<line-break>こしゃくなチビめ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Impudent little runt.<end>"
KO: "…흥. 건방진 꼬맹이 같으니.<end>"

## 920018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チビはお互い様だよ。<end>"
EN: "You're just as short as I am.<end>"
KO: "꼬맹이인 건 피차 마찬가지야.<end>"

## 920019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "후후후….<end>"

## 920020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふ…わはははは…！　<line-break>こしゃくな奴め！<line-break>お前らの勝ちじゃな。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Bwahahaha! You impudent little wretch! You win.<end>"
KO: "후… 와하하하…! 건방진 녀석들! 너희 승리다.<end>"

## 920021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで若いの。<line-break>その風体、冒険者か？<line-break>わしも、もとが付くが冒険者じゃ。<line-break>ちょうどいい。<line-break>わしもまた旅に出たかったのじゃ。<line-break>一緒に連れていってくれんか？<end>"
EN: "By the way, youngster. Judging by the look of you, are you an adventurer? I'm one myself―or was, at any rate. This is perfect. I've been wanting to take to the road again. How about letting me come with you?<end>"
KO: "그런데 젊은이. 그 차림, 모험자인가? 나도 옛날에는 모험자였지. 잘됐군. 나도 다시 여행을 떠나고 싶었어. 함께 데려가 주겠나?<end>"

## 920022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間にする<end>"
EN: "Accept as a companion<end>"
KO: "동료로 삼는다<end>"

## 920023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Don't accept as a companion<end>"

## 920024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。ではこれから<line-break>よろしく頼むぞ、若いの。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>おっと、名前を聞いておらなんだな。<line-break>名はなんという、若いの？<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I look forward to traveling with you, youngster. Ah, but I haven't asked your name. What is it?<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그럼 앞으로 잘 부탁한다, 젊은이. 아차, 이름을 묻지 않았군. 이름이 무엇이냐, 젊은이?<end>"

## 920025

## 920026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그럼 모험을 떠날 때 고양이 저택에서 부를게.<end>"

## 920027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくわからんが、待っておるぞ。<line-break>では、用意をしておこうかの。<end>"

KO: "잘은 모르겠지만 기다리고 있으마. 그럼 준비를 해 둘까.<end>"

## 920028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐにデルガドをパーティーに入れますか？<end>"

KO: "지금 바로 델가도를 파티에 넣겠습니까?<end>"

## 920029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 920031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もぉ、イヤー！<line-break><value:$28>ー！<end>"

KO: "정말, 싫어! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 920032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泥臭い！　ドワーフ臭いわ！<line-break>こぉーんな所、息が詰まってよー！<line-break>早く出るのよ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "It reeks of mud! It reeks of Dwarves! I can't breathe in a place like this! Hurry up and get us out of here, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "흙냄새 나! 드워프 냄새도! 이런 데 있으면 숨 막혀! 빨리 나가자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 920033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやっ！　またもドワーフぅ！？<line-break>し～か～も、酒臭いわー！？<end>"
EN: "No! Another Dwarf?! And this one reeks of drink?!<end>"
KO: "싫어! 또 드워프야!? 게다가 술 냄새까지 나잖아!?<end>"

## 920034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁんですってぇ？　このワタクシに<line-break>そこの下等な泥臭いドワーフと<line-break>一緒に旅をしろっていうのー！？<end>"
EN: "What did you say? You expect me, of all people, to travel with that inferior, mud-reeking Dwarf?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요오? 이 몸더러 저기 있는 천하고 흙내 나는 드워프와 함께 여행하라고요오!?<end>"

## 920035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ～か～ら、早死にだけが取り柄の<line-break>ド下等生物どもは、困るのよー！<line-break>そんな非常識を平気で言ってくるから！<end>"
EN: "This is precisely why I can't abide you utterly inferior creatures whose only virtue is dying young! You say the most outrageous things without batting an eye!<end>"
KO: "그~러~니~까, 일찍 죽는 것밖에 장점 없는 최하등생물들은 곤란하다니까! 그런 비상식적인 말을 아무렇지도 않게 하니까!<end>"

## 920036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が、早死にだけが取り柄じゃ！<line-break>わしはもう１６０年も生きとるわい！<end>"
EN: "What's this about my only virtue being an early death? I've lived for a hundred and sixty years already!<end>"
KO: "뭐가 일찍 죽는 것만 장점이라는 거냐! 나는 벌써 160년이나 살았다고!<end>"

## 920037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お・あ・い・に・く、<line-break>アタクシはその１０倍は生きてるの！<line-break>　<line-break>しかも、人間やドワーフみたいな<line-break>下等生物のように、あくせくせずに<line-break>優雅に、高貴に、生きてきたの！<end>"
EN: "Too bad for you, but I've lived ten times that long! And unlike inferior creatures such as humans and Dwarves, I've lived without all that frantic scurrying―gracefully and nobly!<end>"
KO: "유·감·이·지·만, 난 그 열 배는 살았어! 게다가 인간이나 드워프 같은 하등생물처럼 허둥대지 않고 우아하고 고귀하게 살아왔지!<end>"

## 920038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまりは、なんにもせずに<line-break>のらりくらりと生きとったわけじゃろ？<end>"
EN: "So you've spent all that time idling about and doing nothing, then?<end>"
KO: "그러니까 아무것도 하지 않고 빈둥거리며 살았다는 거지?<end>"

## 920039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や～ね～、自分の尺度でしか<line-break>ものを見られないなんて。　　　　　<line-break>所詮、下等生物なんてその程度かしら。<end>"
EN: "How tiresome. You can't see anything except by your own standards. I suppose that's all inferior creatures are capable of.<end>"
KO: "싫다~ 자기 기준으로밖에 사물을 못 보다니. 결국 하등생물이란 그 정도인가 봐.<end>"

## 920040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、その泥くっさいドワーフと<line-break>一緒に旅しないといけないくらいなら<line-break>舌かんで死んだ方がましだわ！<end>"
EN: "In any case, if I have to travel with that mud-reeking Dwarf, I'd sooner bite off my tongue and die!<end>"
KO: "아무튼 그 흙냄새 나는 드워프랑 같이 여행해야 한다면 혀 깨물고 죽는 게 낫겠어!<end>"

## 920041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "是非とも死んでほしいものじゃが…、<line-break>わしもその娘との旅はガマンできん！<line-break>その娘がおらんときに呼んでくれ。<end>"
EN: "Much as I'd like to see you dead... I couldn't bear traveling with that girl either! Call for me when she isn't around.<end>"
KO: "꼭 죽어 줬으면 싶긴 하다만…, 나도 그 아가씨와 함께 여행하는 건 못 참겠다! 그 아가씨가 없을 때 불러 주게.<end>"

## 920042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、ま、とにかくさ。<line-break>デルガドはあとで、猫屋敷で呼ぶよ。<line-break>それまで、待機しててね。<end>"
EN: "Now, now. We'll summon Dergado from Cat Manor later. Until then, wait here, okay?<end>"
KO: "자자, 아무튼 말이야. 델가도는 나중에 고양이 저택에서 부를게. 그때까지 기다리고 있어.<end>"

## 920043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんかよくわからんが<line-break>待っておればよいのじゃな。　<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、その娘とこれ以上、<line-break>一緒にいるのはごめんじゃ。<line-break>失礼させてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "I don't really understand, but I just need to wait, yes? In any case, I refuse to spend another moment with that girl. I'll take my leave.<end>"
KO: "뭔지는 잘 모르겠지만 기다리면 되는 거군. 어쨌든 그 여자와 더 같이 있는 건 사양이야. 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 920044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無礼で無神経なドワーフねぇ！<line-break>自分が何言ってるかわかってないのね。<line-break>　<line-break>ほら！　ここで用事があるなら<line-break>とっととすませて出発するわよ！<line-break>もう、イヤ！　失礼するわ！<end>"
EN: "What a rude, insensitive Dwarf! You don't even understand what you're saying. Now, if you have business here, finish it quickly so we can leave! I've had enough! I'm leaving!<end>"
KO: "무례하고 눈치 없는 드워프네! 자기가 무슨 말을 하는지도 모르나 봐. 자! 여기서 볼일 있으면 얼른 끝내고 출발할 거야! 정말 싫어! 실례할게!<end>"

## 920045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか、先が思いやられるね。<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "This doesn't bode well, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "앞날이 걱정되네. <value:$28>….<end>"

## 920046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、出口で合流じゃな？<line-break>では、わしも準備してくるわい。<line-break>またあとで会おうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Right, we'll meet at the exit, then. I'll go make my preparations. See you shortly.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 출구에서 합류하는 거지? 그럼 나도 준비하고 오마. 나중에 다시 보자.<end>"

## 920048

## 920049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 920050

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hmph! You've soured my drink!<end>"

## 920051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふわっははは！　そうじゃ。<line-break>ずけずけとものを言うわい。<line-break>気に入ったぞ、若いの。<line-break>ところで、その風体、<line-break>もしかして冒険者か、若いの？<end>"

KO: "후하하하! 그래. 거침없이 말하는군. 마음에 들었다, 젊은이. 그런데 그 차림새, 혹시 모험자인가?<end>"

## 920052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！<line-break>わしも、もとが付くが冒険者じゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>ちょうど、旅に出たいと<line-break>思うておったところじゃ。<line-break>一緒に連れていってくれんかの。<end>"

KO: "그렇군! 나도 전직이긴 하지만 모험자다. 마침 여행을 떠나고 싶던 참이야. 함께 데려가 주지 않겠나.<end>"

## 920053

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやっ！　またもドワーフぅ！？<line-break>し～か～も、酒臭いわー！？<end>"
EN: "No! Another Dwarf?! And this one reeks of drink?!<end>"
KO: "싫어! 또 드워프야!? 게다가 술 냄새까지 나잖아!?<end>"

## 920060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁんですってぇ？　このアタクシに<line-break>そこの下等な泥臭いドワーフと<line-break>一緒に旅をしろっていうのー！？<end>"
EN: "What did you say? You expect me, of all people, to travel with that inferior, mud-reeking Dwarf?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고오? 이 몸더러 저기 있는 천하고 흙내 나는 드워프와 함께 여행하라고오!?<end>"

## 920061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ～か～ら、早死にだけが取り柄の<line-break>ド下等生物どもは、困るのよー！<line-break>そんな非常識を平気で言ってくるから！<end>"
EN: "This is precisely why I can't abide you utterly inferior creatures whose only virtue is dying young! You say the most outrageous things without batting an eye!<end>"
KO: "그~러~니~까, 일찍 죽는 것밖에 장점 없는 최하등생물들은 곤란하다니까! 그런 비상식적인 말을 아무렇지도 않게 하니까!<end>"

## 920062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が、早死にだけが取り柄じゃ！<line-break>わしはもう１６０年も生きとるわい！<end>"
EN: "What's this about my only virtue being an early death? I've lived for a hundred and sixty years already!<end>"
KO: "뭐가 일찍 죽는 것만 장점이라는 거냐! 나는 벌써 160년이나 살았다고!<end>"

## 920063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お・あ・い・に・く、<line-break>アタクシはその１０倍は生きてるの！<line-break>　<line-break>しかも、人間やドワーフみたいな<line-break>下等生物のように、あくせくせずに<line-break>優雅に、高貴に、生きてきたの！<end>"
EN: "Too bad for you, but I've lived ten times that long! And unlike inferior creatures such as humans and Dwarves, I've lived without all that frantic scurrying―gracefully and nobly!<end>"
KO: "유·감·이·지·만, 난 그 열 배는 살았어! 게다가 인간이나 드워프 같은 하등생물처럼 허둥대지 않고 우아하고 고귀하게 살아왔지!<end>"

## 920064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまりは、なんにもせずに<line-break>のらりくらりと生きとったわけじゃろ？<end>"
EN: "So you've spent all that time idling about and doing nothing, then?<end>"
KO: "그러니까 아무것도 하지 않고 빈둥거리며 살았다는 거지?<end>"

## 920065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や～ね～、自分の尺度でしか<line-break>ものを見られないなんて。<line-break>所詮、下等生物なんてその程度かしら。<end>"
EN: "How tiresome. You can't see anything except by your own standards. I suppose that's all inferior creatures are capable of.<end>"
KO: "싫다~ 자기 기준으로밖에 사물을 못 보다니. 결국 하등생물이란 그 정도인가 봐.<end>"

## 920066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、その泥くっさいドワーフと<line-break>一緒に旅しないといけないくらいなら<line-break>舌かんで死んだ方がましだわ！<end>"
EN: "In any case, if I have to travel with that mud-reeking Dwarf, I'd sooner bite off my tongue and die!<end>"
KO: "아무튼 그 흙냄새 나는 드워프랑 같이 여행해야 한다면 혀 깨물고 죽는 게 낫겠어!<end>"

## 920067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "是非とも死んでほしいものじゃが…、<line-break>わしもその娘との旅はガマンできん！<line-break>その娘がおらんときに呼んでくれ。<end>"
EN: "Much as I'd like to see that girl dead... I couldn't bear traveling with her either! Call for me when she isn't around.<end>"
KO: "꼭 죽어 줬으면 싶긴 하다만…, 나도 그 아가씨와 함께 여행하는 건 못 참겠다! 그 아가씨가 없을 때 불러 주게.<end>"

## 920068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "若いの、お前が悪いとは言うとらん。<line-break>ただその娘が気に入らんだけじゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>猫屋敷で呼び出すというのなら<line-break>なんかよくわからんが<line-break>わしは待っておるわい。<end>"
EN: "Youngster, I'm not saying this is your fault. I just don't like that girl. If you're going to summon me from Cat Manor, I don't really understand it, but I'll wait.<end>"
KO: "젊은이, 네가 나쁘다는 건 아니다. 그저 그 처자가 마음에 안 들 뿐이야. 고양이 저택에서 불러낸다는 거면 잘 모르겠지만 기다리고 있으마.<end>"

## 920069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、その娘とこれ以上、　　　<line-break>一緒にいるのはごめんじゃ。<line-break>失礼させてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "In any case, I refuse to spend another moment with that girl. I'll take my leave.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 그 여자애와 더 이상 같이 있는 건 사양이야. 이만 실례하겠네.<end>"

## 920070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無礼で無神経なドワーフねぇ！<line-break>自分が何言ってるかわかってないのね。<line-break>　<line-break>ほら！　ここで用事があるなら<line-break>とっとと済ませて出発するわよ！<line-break>もう、イヤ！　失礼するわ！<end>"
EN: "What a rude, insensitive Dwarf! You don't even understand what you're saying. Now, if you have business here, finish it quickly so we can leave! I've had enough! I'm leaving!<end>"
KO: "무례하고 눈치 없는 드워프네! 자기가 무슨 말을 하는지도 모르나 봐. 자! 여기서 볼일 있으면 얼른 끝내고 출발할 거야! 정말 싫어! 실례할게!<end>"

## 920071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなら、出口で合流すれば　　<line-break>いいんじゃな？<line-break>　<line-break>わしも準備してくるわい。<line-break>またあとで会おうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Then we'll meet at the exit, yes? I'll go make my preparations. See you shortly.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 출구에서 합류하면 되겠구나. 나도 준비하고 오마. 나중에 다시 만나자.<end>"

## 920072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に冒険するときは<line-break>猫屋敷から呼び出す…のか？<line-break>　<line-break>なんだかよくわからんが、<line-break>待っておればよいのだな。<line-break>では、用意をしとくかの。<end>"
EN: "You'll summon me from Cat Manor when it's time to adventure together...? I don't really understand, but I just need to wait, yes? Then I suppose I'd best get ready.<end>"
KO: "함께 모험할 때는 고양이 저택에서 불러낸다…는 건가? 잘 모르겠지만 기다리면 되는 거군. 그럼 준비해 두지.<end>"

## 920073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…、なら、いいわい…。<end>"
EN: "I see... Then that's fine...<end>"
KO: "그런가… 그럼 됐다….<end>"

## 920074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気分が悪いわ！<line-break>…出て行け！<end>"
EN: "I feel sick! Get out!<end>"
KO: "…기분 나빠! …나가!<end>"

## 920075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そっちが出て行かぬのなら。<line-break>わしが出て行くわい！<end>"
EN: "If you won't leave... then I will!<end>"
KO: "…그쪽이 안 나간다면 내가 나가겠네!<end>"

## 920076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 920077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣を打つ…とは<line-break>どういうことなんじゃろうな？<line-break>　<line-break>ハハ、すまん。<line-break>いきなりこんなことを言うても<line-break>お主にはピンとこんか。<end>"
EN: "What does it mean to forge a sword, I wonder? Ha! Forgive me. I suppose that came out of nowhere. You probably don't know what I'm getting at.<end>"
KO: "검을 벼린다는 건… 대체 무슨 뜻일까. 하하, 미안하네. 갑자기 이런 말을 해도 자네에겐 와닿지 않겠지.<end>"

## 920078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしにとって、剣を打つということは<line-break>仕事であり、生活であり、<line-break>さらに言えば信仰でもあったのじゃ。<line-break>強い、天地を切り崩すほどの剣…、<line-break>わしが今まで打ってきた剣は、いわば<line-break>そこへたどり着くまでの過程じゃ。<end>"
EN: "For me, forging swords was my work, my livelihood, and more than that, my faith. A sword so mighty it could cleave heaven and earth... Every sword I've forged until now was but another step toward that goal.<end>"
KO: "내게 검을 벼리는 일은 일이자 생활이었고, 더 말하면 신앙이기도 했지. 강하고 천지를 가를 정도의 검…. 내가 지금까지 만든 검들은 말하자면 그곳에 도달하기 위한 과정이었던 게야.<end>"

## 920079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、わしの剣たちが　　　　　　　<line-break>人々の血を吸うのを見るうちに<line-break>わしにはわからなくなってしもうた。<line-break>剣はしょせん、人殺しの道具なのか？<line-break>強き力は、人を傷つけねばならぬのか？<line-break>わしは今も迷い、剣を打てずにいる…。<end>"
EN: "But as I watched my swords drink people's blood, I lost sight of what it all meant. Are swords, in the end, nothing more than tools for killing? Must great power be used to hurt others? I am still torn, unable to forge another sword...<end>"
KO: "하지만 내가 만든 검들이 사람들의 피를 마시는 걸 보다 보니 나도 모르게 혼란스러워졌네. 검은 결국 사람을 죽이는 도구에 불과한가? 강한 힘은 반드시 사람을 상처 입혀야 하는가? 나는 지금도 고민하며 검을 만들지 못하고 있네….<end>"

## 920080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまらん話をしたのう。<line-break>じじいの愚痴じゃ。忘れてくれ。<line-break>さあ、明日も早い。もう寝るとしよう。<end>"
EN: "That was a tedious tale. Nothing but an old man's grumbling. Forget it. Come, tomorrow will be another early morning. Let's get some sleep.<end>"
KO: "시시한 이야기를 했구나. 늙은이의 푸념이다. 잊어라. 자, 내일도 일찍 일어나야 한다. 이제 자자.<end>"

## 920081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "炎竜山か…。<line-break>すまぬが、わしは登りとうない。<line-break>　<line-break>この山で鍛冶をしてるはずの<line-break>古い仲間に、今のわしを<line-break>見られるのはつらい……。<end>"
EN: "Flame Dragon Mountain... Forgive me, but I don't want to climb it. An old comrade of mine should be smithing here, and I couldn't bear to be seen as I am now...<end>"
KO: "염룡산인가…. 미안하지만 난 오르고 싶지 않네. 이 산에서 대장장이 일을 하고 있을 옛 동료에게 지금의 나를 보이는 건 괴로워….<end>"

## 920083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>……不思議なもんじゃ。お主と旅に出る<line-break>前から、ずっと同じ酒を飲んでるはず<line-break>なのに、味が全然違うんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Strange, isn't it? I've been drinking the same stuff since before we set out together, yet now it tastes completely different.<end>"
KO: "오오, <value:$28>인가. ……신기하구먼. 자네와 여행을 떠나기 전부터 줄곧 같은 술을 마셨을 텐데 맛이 전혀 다르단 말이지.<end>"

## 920084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんというか、わし自身の気力が<line-break>味わいとなっているというか……。<line-break>こんなうまい酒は何年ぶりじゃろう…。<line-break>…なあ<value:$28>、わしは<line-break>やはり、最高の剣を打ってみたい。<line-break>最高の剣の、その意味を知るためにも。<end>"
EN: "Somehow, it's as if my own vigor has become part of the flavor... How many years has it been since I've had a drink this good? You know, <value:$28>, I still want to forge the finest sword there is. Partly so I can learn what that truly means.<end>"
KO: "뭐랄까, 내 기력이 맛으로 배어든다고 해야 하나……. 이렇게 맛있는 술은 몇 년 만인지…. …<value:$28>, 나는 역시 최고의 검을 벼려 보고 싶네. 최고의 검이 무엇을 뜻하는지 알기 위해서라도.<end>"

## 920085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、炎竜山に向かいたいのじゃ。<line-break>あの山頂は天然の炉になっとるでの。<line-break>最高の剣を打つにふさわしい。<line-break>それに、あの山には、わしの<line-break>古い鍛冶仲間、カッツがおる。<line-break>奴もきっと力を貸してくれるだろう。<end>"
EN: "That's why I want to go to Flame Dragon Mountain. The summit is a natural furnace, just the place to forge the finest sword. Besides, an old smithing associate of mine, Katz, is up there. I'm sure he'll lend a hand.<end>"
KO: "그래서 염룡산으로 가고 싶네. 그 산 정상은 천연 용광로가 되어 있거든. 최고의 검을 벼리기에 딱 좋은 곳이지. 게다가 그 산에는 내 오랜 대장장이 동료 카츠가 있어. 그 녀석도 분명 힘을 빌려줄 게다.<end>"

## 920086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、炎竜山へ出発じゃ。<line-break>町を出る時に落ちあおうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Then it's settled. We leave for Flame Dragon Mountain. We'll meet when we leave town.<end>"
KO: "그럼 염룡산으로 출발하자. 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자꾸나.<end>"

## 920087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>カッツと、ロッチナの家か…？<line-break>　<line-break>だいぶほこりをかぶっとるが…。<line-break>おおかた山頂にでもいるのじゃろう。<line-break>よし、行くかの。<end>"
EN: "Is this... Katz and Rotchina's house? It's thick with dust. I expect they're up at the summit. Well, shall we go?<end>"
KO: "이건… 카츠와 로치나의 집인가…? 먼지가 꽤 쌓였군…. 아마 산꼭대기에라도 있겠지. 좋아, 가 보자.<end>"

## 920088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カッツ…。<line-break>ロッチナ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Katz... Rotchina...<end>"
KO: "캇츠…. 롯치나….<end>"

## 920089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまなんだ。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>しかし、我が子を失う親というのは<line-break>こんな気分なのじゃろうな…。<line-break>……行くかの。<line-break>生きているわしらには、まだこれから<line-break>なすべきことがあるしの。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. But I suppose this is how it feels for a parent to lose a child... Let's go. We who are still alive have things left to do.<end>"
KO: "미안하구나. 하지만 자식을 잃는 부모의 마음이란 이런 것이겠지…. ……가자꾸나. 살아 있는 우리에겐 아직 앞으로 해야 할 일이 있으니.<end>"

## 920090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　なんじゃ。<line-break>墓のようじゃが…。<line-break>ここにささっているのは…剣か？<end>"
EN: "Hm? What's this? It looks like a grave... Is that a sword planted there?<end>"
KO: "응? 뭐지. 무덤 같은데…. 여기에 꽂혀 있는 건… 검인가?<end>"

## 920091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これは…。　　　　　<line-break>この剣は…。<line-break>　<line-break>なんと…いうことじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "Th-this is... This sword... How can this be...?<end>"
KO: "이, 이건…. 이 검은…. 이럴 수가….<end>"

## 920092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしには昔、娘がおってな。<line-break>戦火で親を失った人間の少女じゃ。　<line-break>名をロッチナという。<line-break>ドワーフ王国で育て、ドワーフの男も<line-break>顔負けの鍛冶の腕も身につけた。<line-break>わしの、宝じゃった。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, I had a daughter. A human girl who had lost her parents in the war. Her name was Rotchina. I raised her in the Dwarf Kingdom, where she became a smith skilled enough to put any dwarf man to shame. She was my treasure.<end>"
KO: "나에게는 옛날 딸이 하나 있었네. 전쟁으로 부모를 잃은 인간 여자아이였지. 이름은 로치나. 드워프 왕국에서 키웠고 드워프 남자 못지않은 대장장이 솜씨도 익혔다. 내 보물이었어.<end>"

## 920093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やがて、カッツと出会い、<line-break>ふたりで最強の剣を打つと言って<line-break>この地に移り住んだのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "In time, she met Katz. They said they would forge the mightiest sword together, and moved here.<end>"
KO: "이윽고 캇츠를 만나 둘이서 최강의 검을 만들겠다며 이곳으로 이주해 왔지.<end>"

## 920094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その時、わしが祝いに打ったのが<line-break>この剣じゃ。しかし、この剣が<line-break>墓に供えられているということは…。<line-break>ふたりは…。<line-break>あの、希望に満ちていたふたりは…。<end>"
EN: "I forged this sword as a gift to mark the occasion. But if it has been laid here at a grave... Then the two of them... Those two, so full of hope...<end>"
KO: "그때 내가 축하로 만든 것이 이 검이다. 하지만 이 검이 무덤에 바쳐져 있다는 건… 두 사람은… 그 희망에 가득 차 있던 두 사람은…<end>"

## 920095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…！<line-break>これは…。<end>"
EN: "What...! This is...<end>"
KO: "…! 이건….<end>"

## 920096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "折れた宝剣を入手した。<end>"
EN: "Obtained the Broken Precious Sword.<end>"
KO: "부러진 보검을 입수했다.<end>"

## 920097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、折れてはいるが、<line-break>間違いなくカッツの剣じゃ。<line-break>しかも、かなりのできのな。<line-break>おそらくは、この山の山頂で<line-break>打った剣じゃろう。しかし、<line-break>何ゆえに折れたのか…。<end>"
EN: "Broken though it is, that is unmistakably Katz's sword. And it is a fine piece of work. It was probably forged at the summit of this mountain. But why did it break...?<end>"
KO: "부러져 있기는 하지만 틀림없는 카츠의 검이네. 게다가 상당히 잘 만든 물건이군. 아마 이 산 정상에서 만든 검이겠지. 하지만 어째서 부러진 건지….<end>"

## 920098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、すまんが<line-break>今日は山を下りんか？<line-break>今のわしでは、最高の剣なぞ打てまい。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>, but could we head back down the mountain for today? I'm in no state to forge the finest sword.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안하지만 오늘은 산을 내려가 주겠나? 지금의 나로선 최고의 검을 만들 수 없을 것 같구나.<end>"

## 920099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまん。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "미안하군.<end>"

## 920101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わぁ、珍しい剣だね。<line-break>折れてるけど…ね、見せてよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, what an unusual sword. It's broken, but... let me see it.<end>"
KO: "와, 희귀한 검이네. 부러져 있긴 하지만… 있지, 보여 줘.<end>"

## 920102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近の作だけど、この作り方は、<line-break>古代の方式にのっとっているね。<line-break>…緋炎の宝剣を作ろうとしたのかな？<line-break>炎竜山の炎で鍛えた剣は<line-break>緋色に燃え、<line-break>神をも斬り裂く宝剣となる…。<end>"
EN: "It was made recently, but it follows an ancient method. I wonder if someone was trying to forge the Crimson Flame Sword. A sword forged in the fires of Flame Dragon Mountain shall burn crimson, becoming a treasured blade that can cleave even a god...<end>"
KO: "최근에 만든 물건이지만 제작법은 고대 방식에 따르고 있군. …비염의 보검을 만들려 했던 건가? 염룡산의 불로 벼린 검은 진홍빛으로 타오르며 신조차 베어 가르는 보검이 된다….<end>"

## 920103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが緋炎の宝剣の伝承さ。<line-break>神代、破壊神ウルグと<line-break>戦神ソリアスの戦いのときに、<line-break>ソリアスを助けようと、人間が<line-break>作って渡そうとした宝剣なんだって。<line-break>ありがと。<end>"
EN: "That's the legend of the Crimson Flame Sword. During the battle between Ulugh, God of Destruction, and the War God Solias in the Age of the Gods, humans are said to have tried to forge the sword and deliver it to Solias, hoping to aid the War God. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "이게 비염의 보검 전승이야. 신대에 파괴신 울그와 전신 솔리아스가 싸울 때 솔리아스를 돕기 위해 인간이 만들어 건네려 했던 보검이래. 고마워.<end>"

## 920104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "珍しいものが見られたよ。<line-break>じゃ、今日はこれで！<end>"
EN: "That was quite a rare sight. Well, that's all for today!<end>"
KO: "진귀한 걸 봤네. 그럼 오늘은 이만!<end>"

## 920105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉ、珍しい剣をもっておるな。<line-break>見せてみよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? That is an unusual sword you have there. Let me see it.<end>"
KO: "호오, 보기 드문 검을 가지고 있군. 보여 봐라.<end>"

## 920106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近の作じゃが、古代の製法じゃな。<line-break>…もしや…緋炎の宝剣か？<line-break>　<line-break>炎竜山の炎で鍛えた剣は<line-break>緋色に燃え、<line-break>神をも斬り裂く宝剣となる…。<end>"
EN: "Recent work, but the forging method is ancient. Could this be... the Crimson Flame Sword? A sword forged in the fires of Flame Dragon Mountain shall burn crimson, becoming a treasured blade that can cleave even a god...<end>"
KO: "최근 작품이지만 고대 방식이군. …설마… 비염의 보검인가? 염룡산의 불꽃으로 단련한 검은 진홍빛으로 타올라 신조차 베는 보검이 된다….<end>"

## 920107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが緋炎の宝剣の伝承じゃ。<line-break>神代、破壊神ウルグと<line-break>戦神ソリアスの戦いのときに、<line-break>ソリアスを助けようと、人間が<line-break>作って渡そうとした宝剣なのじゃ。<line-break>…無論、渡せはせなんだがな。<end>"
EN: "That is the legend of the Crimson Flame Sword. During the battle between Ulugh, God of Destruction, and the War God Solias in the Age of the Gods, humans tried to forge the sword and deliver it to Solias, hoping to aid the War God. Of course, it was never presented.<end>"
KO: "이것이 비염의 보검 전승이다. 신대에 파괴신 울그와 전신 솔리아스가 싸울 때 인간이 솔리아스를 돕기 위해 만들어 전하려 했던 보검이지. …물론 전해 주지는 못했지만.<end>"

## 920108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグ様を恐れ、消したいという<line-break>人間の願いが生み出した伝承かと<line-break>思うておったが、案外古い伝承でな。<line-break>信憑性も実は高い。<line-break>じゃが、これを打つには心技体が<line-break>そろわねばならぬ。<end>"
EN: "I had thought the legend was born of humanity's fear of Lord Ulugh and its wish to destroy the god. But it turns out to be surprisingly old, and in fact quite credible. Still, to forge this blade, body, skill, and heart must all be present.<end>"
KO: "울그 님을 두려워해 없애고 싶다는 인간의 바람이 낳은 전승인 줄 알았는데 의외로 오래된 전승이더군. 사실 신빙성도 높아. 하지만 이걸 만들려면 심기체가 모두 갖춰져야 한다.<end>"

## 920109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最高の炎の精霊力を宿した身体、　　<line-break>器用さとしての技術力…そして、<line-break>最後に、心というのがくせ者でな。<line-break>神代の人間はこの宝剣を打とうとして<line-break>誰ひとり、その心がなくて<line-break>打てなんだという。<end>"
EN: "A body imbued with fire-spirit power of the highest order, technical skill expressed through dexterity... and finally, the difficult part: the heart. It is said that none of the humans who tried to forge this treasured sword in the Age of the Gods possessed that heart, and so none succeeded.<end>"
KO: "최고의 불꽃 정령력을 품은 육체, 손재주라는 기술력… 그리고 마지막으로 마음이라는 게 골칫거리다. 신대의 인간들은 이 보검을 만들려 했지만 누구 하나 그 마음이 없어 만들지 못했다고 한다.<end>"

## 920110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを打った者も、失敗しておったな。<line-break>ま、人間の打ったものが、神を斬れる<line-break>わけがないということじゃな。<end>"
EN: "The one who forged this failed as well. Well, I suppose that means no blade forged by human hands can cut a god.<end>"
KO: "이걸 벼린 자도 실패했군. 뭐, 인간이 만든 것으로 신을 벨 수 있을 리 없다는 뜻이겠지.<end>"

## 920111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、珍しい剣ね。<line-break>折れてるみたいだけど。<line-break>見ていい？<end>"
EN: "My, what an unusual sword. It seems to be broken, though. May I see it?<end>"
KO: "어머, 희한한 검이네. 부러진 것 같지만. 봐도 돼?<end>"

## 920112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近の作だけど、　　　　　　<line-break>古い製法を使ってるわね。<line-break>…もしかして、緋炎の宝剣？<line-break>炎竜山の炎で鍛えた剣は<line-break>緋色に燃え、<line-break>神をも斬り裂く宝剣となる…。<end>"
EN: "It was made recently, but it uses an old forging method. Could it be the Crimson Flame Sword? A sword forged in the fires of Flame Dragon Mountain shall burn crimson, becoming a treasured blade that can cleave even a god...<end>"
KO: "최근에 만든 거지만 옛 제작법을 썼네. …혹시 비염의 보검? 염룡산의 불꽃으로 벼린 검은 붉게 타오르며 신마저 베어 가르는 보검이 된다…<end>"

## 920113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが緋炎の宝剣の伝承。　　　<line-break>神代、破壊神ウルグと<line-break>戦神ソリアスの戦いのときに、<line-break>ソリアスを助けようと、人間が<line-break>作って渡そうとした宝剣なのよ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That is the legend of the Crimson Flame Sword. During the battle between Ulugh, God of Destruction, and the War God Solias in the Age of the Gods, humans tried to forge the sword and deliver it to Solias, hoping to aid the War God.<end>"
KO: "이게 비염의 보검에 관한 전승이야. 신대에 파괴신 울그와 전신 솔리아스가 싸울 때, 솔리아스를 돕기 위해 인간이 만들어 건네려 했던 보검이지.<end>"

## 920114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>珍しいものが見られたわ。<line-break>それじゃ、失礼するわね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I got to see something quite rare. Now, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 신기한 걸 봤네. 그럼 실례할게.<end>"

## 920115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>さすが、ドワーフ王国の酒場だね。<line-break>もんのすごーくお酒がおいしいよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Just what you'd expect from a tavern in the Dwarf Kingdom. The booze is sooo good!<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 역시 드워프 왕국 술집이네. 술이 정말 엄청 맛있어.<end>"

## 920116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec092-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたたたたた…。<line-break>まったく、ひどい目にあったよ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>名工デルガドがいるってんで<line-break>刀を打ってくれって頼みに行ったら　<line-break>いきなりゴツン！　だよ。あー痛て。<end>"
EN: "Ow, ow, ow, ow... Honestly, that was awful. I heard the master smith Dergado was here, so I went to ask him to forge me a sword. Then, out of nowhere, wham! Ow, that hurts.<end>"
KO: "아야야야야…. 정말 끔찍한 꼴을 당했어. 명공 델가도가 있다길래 칼을 만들어 달라고 부탁하러 갔더니 갑자기 쿵! 이라니까. 아파라.<end>"
