## 930001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もぅ、ほんっっっっっっっっとに　　　<line-break>あったま、きちゃうわ！<line-break>　<line-break>何が世界は驚きに満ちているよ！<line-break>ぜんっぜん、なんっにもないじゃない、<line-break>そんなの！<end>"
EN: "Oh, this makes me so, so, so mad! “The world is full of surprises,” my foot! There aren't any! Not one!<end>"
KO: "정말, 진짜 너무 열받아! 세상이 놀라움으로 가득하다니 뭐야! 전혀, 아무것도 없잖아, 그런 거!<end>"

## 930002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>のウソツキー！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you liar!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 거짓말쟁이~!<end>"

## 930003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふんっっっっっっだ！　それじゃ、<line-break>あんたが心を動かされたってのは何？<line-break>いったい、何だって言うのよーーーー！<end>"
EN: "Hmph! Then what moved you? What was it? Tell meeeeee!<end>"
KO: "흥이다아아아! 그럼 네 마음을 움직였다는 게 뭐야? 대체 뭐냐고오오오!<end>"

## 930004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふんっ、ふんっ、ふっっっんだ！<line-break>初めてギルドの依頼を達成したときの<line-break>感動ですって？<end>"
EN: "Hmph, hmph, hmmmph! You mean the thrill of completing your first Guild job?<end>"
KO: "흥, 흥, 흐으응! 처음 길드 의뢰를 달성했을 때의 감동이라고?<end>"

## 930005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダサイ、ダサイ、ダサすぎよーー！　<line-break>本当にセンスのかけらもないわね！<line-break>　<line-break>アタクシのように気高く崇高な存在は<line-break>本来ならそんな泥くさいことになんて<line-break>一生無縁なのよ。わかっているの？<end>"
EN: "Lame, lame, utterly lame! You really haven't got a shred of taste! A noble, exalted being such as myself should go an entire lifetime without ever having anything to do with such grubby business. Is that clear?<end>"
KO: "촌스러워, 촌스러워, 너무 촌스럽잖아~! 정말 센스라고는 한 조각도 없네! 나처럼 고귀하고 숭고한 존재는 원래 그런 흙냄새 나는 일과는 평생 인연이 없는 거야. 알고 있어?<end>"

## 930006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、<value:$28>！<line-break>それが欠けていたんじゃ！<end>"
EN: "That's it, <value:$28>! That's what I was missing!<end>"
KO: "그래, <value:$28>! 그게 빠져 있었던 거야!<end>"

## 930007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃ、ちょ、ちょっと、どきなさいよ！<line-break>この酒まみれ泥ドワーフ！<line-break>アタクシに酒臭い身体、近づけないで！<end>"
EN: "Eek! W-wait, move! You filthy, booze-soaked dwarf! Keep that booze-reeking body away from me!<end>"
KO: "꺄, 자, 잠깐, 비켜! 이 술에 절은 흙투성이 드워프! 술 냄새 나는 몸을 나한테 가까이 대지 마!<end>"

## 930008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最強の剣を作りたいと願ったころは、<line-break>わしにも感動があふれとった。<line-break>剣を打つたびに心が踊った。<line-break>それが、いつの間にか、最強の剣を<line-break>作らねばならぬという思いが、<line-break>動かしようのない重石になっておった。<end>"
EN: "When I first dreamed of forging the strongest sword, I was brimming with passion. My heart leapt every time I forged a sword. But somewhere along the way, the conviction that I had to forge the strongest sword became a weight I could no longer shift.<end>"
KO: "최강의 검을 만들고 싶다고 바라던 시절엔 나도 감동으로 가득했지. 검을 벼릴 때마다 마음이 뛰었어. 그런데 어느새 최강의 검을 만들어야 한다는 생각이 꼼짝 못 하게 하는 무거운 짐이 되어 버렸네.<end>"

## 930009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは刀工だから剣を打っとった<line-break>のではない。剣を作りたかったから<line-break>刀工になったんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I didn't forge swords because I was a swordsmith. I became a swordsmith because I wanted to forge them.<end>"
KO: "나는 도공이라서 검을 만든 게 아니다. 검을 만들고 싶어서 도공이 된 거야.<end>"

## 930010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おぉ、そうじゃ、そうだったんじゃ！<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh! Aye, that's it! That's how it was!<end>"
KO: "오오, 그래, 그랬었지!<end>"

## 930011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をわけわかんないこと言ってるの、<line-break>このヒゲダルマ！<line-break>早く、どきなさいよー！<end>"
EN: "What are you babbling about, you great ball of beard? Hurry up and move!<end>"
KO: "뭔 소린지 하나도 모르겠어, 이 수염 뚱보! 빨리 비켜!<end>"

## 930012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうしちゃあ、おれん。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "I can't stay here like this. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "이러고 있을 때가 아니다. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 930013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこへ？　決まっとる。炎竜山じゃ。<line-break>わしの刀工の血が騒いどる。<line-break>今動かずにはおれんのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Where? Why, Flame Dragon Mountain, of course! My swordsmith's blood is stirring. I must act now!<end>"
KO: "어디로? 정해져 있지. 염룡산이다. 내 대장장이 피가 끓고 있어. 지금 움직이지 않고는 못 배기겠어!<end>"

## 930014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、私を置いていこうなんて<line-break>５００年早くてよー！<end>"
EN: "Wait! You're five hundred years too early to think you can leave me behind!<end>"
KO: "나를 두고 가다니 500년은 일러!<end>"

## 930015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>カッツたちの小屋へ<line-break>寄っていいかの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, can we stop by Katz and the others' hut?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 캇츠네 오두막에 들러도 되겠나?<end>"

## 930016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カッツもロッチナも<line-break>もう剣を打つことはできん。<end>"
EN: "Neither Katz nor Rotchina can forge a sword anymore.<end>"
KO: "카츠도 로치나도 이제 검을 만들 수 없다.<end>"

## 930017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せめて、ふたりの道具で剣を<line-break>完成させてやりたいんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "The least I can do is finish the sword with their tools.<end>"
KO: "적어도 두 사람의 도구로 검을 완성해 주고 싶구먼.<end>"

## 930018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまんの。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "미안하구나.<end>"

## 930019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カッツの奴め、相変わらず　　　　　<line-break>道具の手入れがいきとどいとる。<line-break>　<line-break>これなら、<value:$28>でも<line-break>十分に手伝ってもらえそうじゃな。<end>"
EN: "That Katz... He always did keep his tools in fine condition. With these, even you should be able to lend a hand, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "캇츠 녀석, 여전히 도구 관리를 잘 해 놨군. 이 정도면 <value:$28>도 충분히 도와줄 수 있겠어.<end>"

## 930020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配はいらん。お主には<line-break>ちょっと手伝ってもらうだけじゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>それじゃ、行くとしようかの。<line-break>この山の聖なる頂へ。<end>"
EN: "There's no need to worry. I only need a little help from you. Well then, let us be off―to the sacred summit of this mountain.<end>"
KO: "걱정할 것 없다. 자네는 조금만 도와주면 돼. 그럼 가 볼까. 이 산의 성스러운 정상으로.<end>"

## 930021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カッツ…。　　　　　　　<line-break>ロッチナ…。<line-break>　<line-break>このふたりのためにも<line-break>宝剣を完成させねばならん。<line-break>さあ、行こうかの。<end>"
EN: "Katz... Rotchina... For their sake, I must finish the treasured sword. Come, let us go.<end>"
KO: "카츠…. 로치나…. 이 둘을 위해서라도 보검을 완성해야 한다. 자, 가 볼까.<end>"

## 930022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、炎の泉が<line-break>炎竜の炎で満ちておる。<end>"
EN: "Good. The spring of flame is filled with the fire dragon's flames.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 불꽃의 샘이 염룡의 불꽃으로 가득 찼다.<end>"

## 930023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっそく準備じゃ。<line-break><value:$28>、そこにある<line-break>光る炎竜岩を持ってきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Right, let's get ready. <value:$28>, bring me that glowing Flame Dragon Rock over there.<end>"
KO: "당장 준비하자. <value:$28>, 거기 있는 빛나는 염룡암을 가져와 줘.<end>"

## 930024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこへ行く気じゃ？　<line-break>少しくらい暑くともガマンせい。<line-break>お主なら耐えられるはずじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Where do you think you're going? Put up with a little heat. You should be able to handle it.<end>"
KO: "어디 가려는 게냐? 좀 덥더라도 참거라. 너라면 견딜 수 있을 게다.<end>"

## 930025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>早く炎竜岩を持ってくるんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, hurry up and bring me the Flame Dragon Rock.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 빨리 염룡암을 가져오너라.<end>"

## 930026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、始めるかの。<end>"
EN: "Good. Let's begin.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 시작해 볼까.<end>"

## 930027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、最後の仕上げじゃ。<line-break><value:$28>、お前さんが<line-break>剣に命を吹き込むんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Good. Now for the finishing touch. <value:$28>, you must breathe life into the sword.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 마지막 마무리다. <value:$28>, 네가 검에 생명을 불어넣는 거야.<end>"

## 930028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配せんでいい。<line-break>お主の精霊力を<line-break>剣に吹きこむつもりでやってみい。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. Just try to pour your spirit power into the sword.<end>"
KO: "걱정할 것 없다. 네 정령력을검에 불어넣는다는 마음으로 해 보거라.<end>"

## 930029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よし！<line-break>完成じゃ。<end>"
EN: "...Good! It's finished.<end>"
KO: "…좋아! 완성이다.<end>"

## 930030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしの長年の夢はかなった。<line-break>カッツとロッチナの夢も…。<line-break>すべて、お主のおかげじゃ。<line-break>ありがとう、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "My lifelong dream has come true. Katz and Rotchina's dream as well... and it is all thanks to you. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "내 오랜 꿈은 이루어졌다. 캇츠와 롯치나의 꿈도…. 모두 자네 덕분이야. 고맙네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 930031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、戻ろうかの。<line-break>なぜだか無性に、<line-break>酒が飲みとうなったわ。<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we head back? For some reason, I find myself desperately craving a drink.<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌아갈까. 왠지 몹시 술이 마시고 싶어졌구나.<end>"

## 930032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カッツ、ロッチナ、　　　　　　　<line-break>わしはやったぞ。<line-break>お主らの宝剣と共に…。<line-break>きっとお主らも、あの世から<line-break>手助けしてくれていたのじゃろう。<line-break>ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Katz, Rotchina... I did it. With your treasured sword... You must have been helping me from the other side. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "카츠, 로치나, 나 해냈다. 너희 보검과 함께…. 분명 너희도 저세상에서 도와줬겠지. 고맙다.<end>"

## 930034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの旅は終わった。<line-break>これからのわしは<line-break>お主と旅を共にする戦士じゃ。<line-break>では、行こうかの、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well, my journey is over. From now on, I am a warrior who travels at your side. Shall we be off, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "자, 내 여행은 끝났다. 이제부터 나는 자네와 여행을 함께하는 전사다. 그럼 가 볼까, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 930035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。<line-break>それではそれを握りながら<line-break>この炎の泉に向かって祈るんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Aye. Now hold on to it, face this spring of flame, and pray.<end>"
KO: "음. 그럼 그걸 쥔 채로이 불꽃의 샘을 향해 기도하거라.<end>"

## 930036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "炎の神剣を打つにはな、<line-break>祈り手と打ち手が必要なんじゃ。<line-break>そのどちらが欠けても神剣は打てぬ。<line-break>カッツとロッチナもそうじゃ。<line-break>きっとロッチナがここで祈り、<line-break>カッツが打ったのじゃろう。<end>"
EN: "To forge the divine sword of flame, you need someone to pray and someone to do the forging. Without both, the divine sword cannot be forged. It was the same for Katz and Rotchina. Rotchina must have prayed here while Katz did the forging.<end>"
KO: "불의 신검을 만들려면 기도하는 자와 두드리는 자가 필요하다. 둘 중 하나라도 빠지면 신검은 만들 수 없어. 카츠와 로치나도 그랬겠지. 분명 로치나가 여기서 기도하고 카츠가 두드렸을 게다.<end>"

## 930037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ、ふもとで打つのかはわからぬ。<line-break>恐らくは精霊力の流れか何かの関係で<line-break>それがもっともよいのじゃろうな。<line-break>さあ、わしはふもとの小屋へ戻る。<line-break>お主はここで祈りをささげてくれ。<line-break>激しい炎を宿した剣を思い描くんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I don't know why it has to be forged at the foot of the mountain. Perhaps the flow of spirit power, or something like that, makes it the best place. Now, I'll return to the hut down below. Stay here and pray. Picture a sword with fierce flames burning within it.<end>"
KO: "왜 산기슭에서 만드는지는 모르겠군. 아마 정령력의 흐름 같은 것과 관련해 그곳이 가장 좋은 거겠지. 자, 나는 산기슭 오두막으로 돌아가겠다. 너는 여기서 기도를 올려라. 맹렬한 불꽃을 품은 검을 떠올리는 거야.<end>"

## 930039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの旅は終わった。<line-break>これからのわしは<line-break>お主と旅を共にする戦士じゃ。<line-break>では、行こうかの、<value:$28>。<line-break>まずは…酒場じゃ。<line-break>なぜだか無性に酒が飲みとうなったわ。<end>"
EN: "Well, my journey is over. From now on, I am a warrior who travels at your side. Shall we be off, <value:$28>? First... to the tavern. For some reason, I find myself desperately craving a drink.<end>"
KO: "자, 내 여행은 끝났다. 이제부터 나는 너와 함께 여행하는 전사다. 그럼 가 볼까, <value:$28>. 우선은… 술집이다. 왠지 몹시 술이 마시고 싶어졌어.<end>"

## 930040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 930041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec093-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カッツの奴め、相変わらず　　　　　<line-break>道具の手入れがいきとどいとる。<line-break>　<line-break>これなら、<value:$28>とわしでも<line-break>十分に炎の神剣を打つことが<line-break>できるじゃろうな。<end>"
EN: "That Katz... He always did keep his tools in fine condition. With these, you and I should have no trouble forging the divine sword of flame, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "캇츠 녀석, 여전히 도구 손질이 잘돼 있군. 이 정도면 <value:$28>와 나라도 충분히 화염의 신검을 벼릴 수 있겠어.<end>"

<end><if><value:$01><equal>20行くか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11じゃあ、行きましょうか。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21私を待たせるのは<line-break><value:$28>くらいだな。<line-break>さあ、行くぞ<end><if><value:$01><equal>23行こう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27じゃあ、行こうか。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26うむ、よく呼び出してくれた。<line-break>では、さっそく行こう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24お待ちしておりました。<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>では、参りましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25呼んでくれるの待ってたの。<line-break>じゃあ、さっそく行きましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192連れていってくれるのですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193呼んでくださって<line-break>ありがとうございます！<line-break>また…一緒にいられるんですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194君は光。<line-break>僕は光を守る盾。<line-break>さあ、行こう、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191私の力が必要なのね。<line-break>じゃあ、行きましょ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190待たせたな。<line-break>さあ、行くぞ。<end>お待たせー！！<line-break>また<value:$28>と冒険だね。<line-break>よーし、しゅっぱーつ！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Whew... This is making me a little nervous. Come on, <value:$28>. Let's do what must be done!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Ah, good of you to summon me. I was just beginning to grow bored. Let us be off at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4I got summoned! <value:$28>, you can be so fickle! Oh well. Time to go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Hey, <value:$28>! I'm ready to leave whenever you are! Ready, steady, Van! Heh heh.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Heh-heh! You finally called me. I've been waiting, <value:$28>. Come on, let's give it our all!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7So we're going. My strength is yours. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>8Oh? You need my strength? Very well. I'll do everything in my power to help.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Hey there, <value:$28>! You wanted to see me in action, didn't you? Just wait till I show you how cool I can be!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Hey, <value:$28>. Adventuring with you really does sound like the most fun. Well, shall we go?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You need my strength? All right. Let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, it's my turn, right?! All right, I'll give it everything I've got!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14So you've finally called me. Honestly, I was growing weary of waiting. Well then, shall we go?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hi, <value:$28>. I'll do my best.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17You want to borrow my power? Oh, very well. Just don't slow me down.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19I'm back, <value:$28>! Lulluantha's going to do her best. Let's both give it our all, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Shall we go, then?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Only you could keep me waiting, <value:$28>. Come, let us go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Well, shall we go?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Ah, good of you to summon me. Let us be off at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>. Shall we be on our way?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25I've been waiting for you to call me. Let's get going right away.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192You're taking me with you, then.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Thank you for calling me! We can... be together again.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194You are the light. I am the shield that guards it. Come, <value:$28>. Let us go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191So you need my strength. All right, let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190I've kept you waiting. Come, let's go.<end>Sorry to keep you waiting!! Another adventure with <value:$28>! All right, let's gooo!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2…후우, 왠지 긴장되네. 자, 가자 <value:$28>. 지금 해야 할 일을 하러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3그래, 잘 불렀다. 마침 지루하던 참이야. 바로 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4불려 나왔네. <value:$28> 님은 변덕쟁이일지도! 뭐, 좋아. 출발하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5요, <value:$28>! 난 언제든 출발할 수 있어! 준비 반탄, 이랄까. 푸푸.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6헤헤. 드디어 불러 줬네. 기다렸어, <value:$28>. 자, 힘차게 가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7가는구나. 내 힘을 네게 맡길게. 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>8제 힘이 필요하십니까. 알겠습니다. 힘껏 돕겠습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9야호, <value:$28>! 내 활약이 보고 싶어진 거지? 멋진 모습 보여 줄게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12요, <value:$28>. 역시 너와 모험하는 게 제일 재미있겠어. 그럼 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13내 힘이 필요한가. 좋아. 간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 내 차례지!? 좋아, 힘낼게!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이제야 불렀구나. 기다리다 지쳤다. 자, 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15안녕, <value:$28>. 나 힘낼게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17아타쿠시 힘을 빌리고 싶어? 어쩔 수 없네. 발목이나 잡지 마.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19다녀왔어, <value:$28>. 루루안타 힘낼 테니까 <value:$28>도 같이 힘내자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20갈까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11그럼 가 볼까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21나를 기다리게 하는 건 <value:$28> 정도군. 자, 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그럼 가 볼까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26그래, 잘 불렀다. 바로 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24기다리고 있었습니다. <value:$28> 님. 그럼 가시죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25불러 주길 기다렸어. 그럼 바로 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192데려가 주시는군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193불러 주셔서 감사합니다! 또… 함께 있을 수 있네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194너는 빛. 나는 빛을 지키는 방패. 자, 가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191내 힘이 필요한 거구나. 그럼 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190기다렸지. 자, 간다.<end>기다렸지!! 또 <value:$28>와 모험이네. 좋아, 출발!<end>"

## 940002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec094-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2じゃ、また会いましょ。<line-break>がんばってね、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3うむ。ではわしは<line-break>酒場にでも行くことにしよう。<line-break>しばしさらばだ、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4いらなくなったら放り出すなんて<line-break>この機械って、けっこうひどいかも！<line-break><value:$28>様…さようなら！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5じゃあな、<value:$28>。<line-break>またいつでも呼んでくれよ。<line-break>俺たちは仲間なんだからな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>…<line-break>また、一緒に冒険しようね。<line-break>絶対、絶対だからね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>7じゃあ、私は私で冒険を続けるわ。<line-break>何かあったらすぐに呼ぶのよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>8では、私はみなさんの成功を<line-break>祈っていることにしましょう。<line-break>何か困ったら、また呼んでください。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>9う～ん、ここでいっぺんお別れですか。<line-break>ま、それはそれでしょうがないやね。<line-break>ほんじゃ、またね、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12ま、お前さんのことだ。<line-break>これも何か考えがあってのことだろう。<line-break>じゃあな、また呼んでくれ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13さらばだ。<line-break>お前だけでどこまでできるか<line-break>試してみるのもいいだろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10じゃあな、姉ちゃん。<line-break>あんまり無理するなよ。<line-break>危なくなったら、いつでも呼ぶんだぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14さて、これからどうしたものか。<line-break>工房に戻るのもなんだしのう……。<line-break>うむ、大陸中の酒を飲んでまわるか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15さよなら、<value:$28>。<line-break>僕も、強くなるためにガンバるよ。<line-break>じゃ、また呼んでね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17あら？　アタクシ抜きで大丈夫なわけ？<line-break>アタクシの存在の偉大さに気付いたら<line-break>いつでも呼んでくれてよくってよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>19お別れって、さびしいな。<line-break>でも、絶対また呼んでくれるよね？<line-break>だから、またね、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20しばしさらばだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11また会いましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21いつでも呼ぶがよい。<line-break>待っているぞ、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23さらばだ、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>27何かあったら、すぐ呼んでくれ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26しばしさらばだ、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24いつでも呼んでください。<line-break>それでは失礼します。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25…また呼んでね。<line-break>絶対だよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>192貴方と離れるのですね。<line-break>…了解、しました。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193お別れですね…。<line-break>また必ず呼んでくださいね！<end><if><value:$01><equal>194また、ひとりになるのか…。<line-break>これが、一番いやな瞬間だな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191またいつでも呼んでね。<line-break>じゃあね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190今度会うまでに力をつけておく。<line-break>また呼んでくれ。<end>ひどいなあ。<line-break>君の歌はまだ完成していないのに。<line-break>仕方ない。今度また一緒に旅させてね。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Well, until we meet again. Good luck, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Very well. I think I'll pay a visit to the tavern. Farewell for now, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4This machine just tosses you aside when it doesn't need you anymore. That's kind of awful! Farewell, <value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>5See you, <value:$28>. Call me again whenever you like. We're comrades, after all!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>... Let's go adventuring together again. You have to promise. Promise!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7I'll carry on with my own adventures, then. Call me at once if anything happens.<end><if><value:$01><equal>8Then I shall pray for everyone's success. If you run into any trouble, please call upon me again.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Hmm, so this is where we part ways for a while. Well, I suppose that's how it goes. See you around, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Well, knowing you, there must be some reason for this. See you. Call me again sometime.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Farewell. It may be worthwhile to see how far you can get on your own.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10See you, Sis. Don't push yourself too hard. If things get dangerous, call me anytime.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Now, what shall I do? I don't much fancy returning to the workshop... Ah, I know. I'll drink my way around the whole continent.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Goodbye, <value:$28>. I'll work hard so I can get stronger too. Call me again, okay?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh? Are you sure you can manage without me? Once you realize just how magnificent my presence is, you may call upon me whenever you please.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19It's sad to say goodbye. But you'll definitely call me again, won't you? So... see you, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Farewell for now.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Until we meet again.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21You may call upon me whenever you wish. I shall be waiting, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Farewell, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27If anything happens, call me at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Farewell for now, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Please call upon me whenever you need me. Now, if you'll excuse me.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Call me again... You have to promise!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192So we're parting, then. ...Understood.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193So this is goodbye... Please, be sure to call me again!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Alone again... This is the moment I hate most...<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Call me again anytime. Bye for now.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190I'll be stronger by the next time we meet. Call me again.<end>That's mean. Your song isn't even finished yet. Oh well. Let me travel with you again sometime, all right?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2그럼 또 만나자. 힘내, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3그래. 그럼 난 술집에라도 가야겠군. 잠시 작별이다, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4필요 없어지면 내다 버리다니 이 기계 꽤 너무할지도! <value:$28> 님… 안녕히!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5잘 있어, <value:$28>. 언제든 다시 불러. 우린 동료니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>… 또 같이 모험하자. 꼭, 꼭이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7그럼 난 나대로 모험을 계속할게. 무슨 일 있으면 바로 불러.<end><if><value:$01><equal>8그럼 여러분의 성공을 빌고 있겠습니다. 곤란한 일이 생기면 다시 불러 주세요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9음, 여기서 한번 헤어지는 건가. 뭐, 어쩔 수 없지. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12뭐, 너니까 이것도 뭔가 생각이 있어서겠지. 그럼 또 불러 줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13잘 있어라. 너 혼자 어디까지 할 수 있는지 시험해 보는 것도 좋겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10잘 있어, 누나. 너무 무리하지 마. 위험해지면 언제든 불러.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14자, 이제 어떻게 할까. 공방으로 돌아가기도 그렇고…. 그래, 대륙의 술을 전부 마시러 다녀 볼까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15잘 있어, <value:$28>. 나도 강해지도록 노력할게. 그럼 또 불러 줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17어머? 아타쿠시 없이 괜찮겠어? 아타쿠시의 위대함을 깨달으면 언제든 불러도 좋아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19헤어지는 건 쓸쓸하네. 그래도 꼭 다시 불러 줄 거지? 그러니까 또 보자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20잠시 작별이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11또 만나자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21언제든 불러라. 기다리고 있겠다, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23잘 있어라, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27무슨 일 있으면 바로 불러 줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26잠시 작별이다, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24언제든 불러 주세요. 그럼 실례하겠습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25…또 불러 줘. 꼭이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192당신과 떨어지는군요. …알겠습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193헤어지는군요…. 꼭 다시 불러 주세요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194또 혼자가 되는 건가…. 이 순간이 제일 싫군….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191언제든 다시 불러 줘. 그럼 안녕.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190다음에 만날 때까지 힘을 길러 두겠다. 다시 불러 줘.<end>너무하네. 네 노래는 아직 완성되지 않았는데. 어쩔 수 없지. 다음에 또 같이 여행하게 해 줘.<end>"

## 940003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec094-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、それから<line-break>手に入れたソウルはちゃんと宿すこと。<line-break>これを忘れちゃ、意味がねえからな。<line-break>最後に、こいつも<line-break>言っておかなきゃならんな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and make sure you actually take any Souls you acquire into yourself. Forget that, and acquiring them was pointless. There's one last thing I need to tell you.<end>"
KO: "그리고 손에 넣은 소울은 제대로 깃들게 해 둬. 이걸 잊으면 의미가 없으니까. 마지막으로 이것도 말해 둬야겠군.<end>"

## 940012

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>。<line-break>準備はいい？<line-break>最後まで一緒にがんばりましょ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3では、<value:$28>。<line-break>いざ、参ろうぞ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4この戦いに勝ったら<line-break>私は、世界を守った勇者様！<line-break>みんながちやほやしてくれるのね！<line-break>うふふ。<line-break>バラ色の未来のためだもん！<line-break>灰色の戦いにだって行っちゃうわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5だろ！？　そうだろ？<line-break>ここはやっぱ俺しかいないよな！<line-break>よーし、行くぜ！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6ボク、とってもうれしいよ。<line-break>今、キミと一緒に戦いに行くことが、<line-break>ここにいることが…。<line-break>さぁ、行こう！<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>7…最後まで、<line-break>決着をつけるわ。<line-break>それが私の仲間への手向け。<line-break>…ゼリグ、ドルドラム。<line-break>私たちを見守ってて…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>8さあ、行きましょう。<line-break>未来への扉を開き、<line-break>新しい地平へと進むために。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9やっほー！　<value:$28>！<line-break>こういう見せ場はカルラちゃんが<line-break>ビシッとシメてあげるかんねー！<end><if><value:$01><equal>11今度は私があなたの力になる番ね。<line-break>行きましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>この先に何が待っていても、<line-break>あなたさえいれば<line-break>私には何も恐れるものはないわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12よ、<value:$28>。<line-break>バクチも勝負もビビった方が負け。<line-break>ま、気楽にいこうぜ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13俺を呼びだしたのは<line-break>いい選択だ。<line-break>あとの奴らは能なしばかりだからな。<line-break>…行くぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん…。<line-break>俺、姉ちゃんと一緒なら、<line-break>どこまでだって行ってやるぜ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14こんなどえらい戦いで<line-break>わしの力を振るえるとはのう…。<line-break>燃えるわい！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>これが最後の戦いになるのかな…？<line-break>　<line-break>いや、これで最後にしよう！<line-break>僕たちでこの戦いを終わらせるんだ！<line-break>頑張ろう、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17高貴なエルフであるこのアタクシを<line-break>呼び出さないとカタが付かないなんて<line-break>なっていなくてよぉー！！<line-break>とにかく、アタクシが来たからには、<line-break>こんなくだらないこと、<line-break>さっさと片づけるのよぉー！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19…ルルアンタたちが負けちゃったら<line-break>みんなのほほえみが<line-break>なくなっちゃうんだよね。<line-break>でも、でもぉ。<line-break><value:$28>は負けないよね。<line-break>ぜったい、がんばろうね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…行くぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>22やっぱり、この戦いにも、<line-break>きみの歌を作る<line-break>ぼくがいなくっちゃ！<line-break>じゃあ、<line-break><value:$28>の歌のヤマ場へ<line-break>しゅっぱーつ！！<end>よし、一緒に戦おう。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>. 준비됐어? 마지막까지 같이 힘내자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3그럼 <value:$28>. 자, 가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4이 싸움에서 이기면 나는 세계를 지킨 용사님! 모두가 떠받들어 주겠지! 후후. 장밋빛 미래를 위해서라면 잿빛 싸움에도 가겠어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5그렇지!? 역시 여기선 내가 필요하지! 좋아, 간다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6나 정말 기뻐. 지금 너와 함께 싸우러 가는 게, 여기에 있는 게…. 자, 가자! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7…마지막까지 결판을 내겠어. 그게 동료들에게 바치는 선물이야. …젤리그, 돌드람. 지켜봐 줘….<end><if><value:$01><equal>8자, 가죠. 미래의 문을 열고 새로운 지평으로 나아가기 위해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9야호, <value:$28>! 이런 볼거리는 카를라가 멋지게 마무리해 줄게!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11이번엔 내가 네 힘이 될 차례야. 가자. 앞에 무엇이 기다려도 네가 있으면 두렵지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12요, <value:$28>. 도박도 승부도 겁먹은 쪽이 져. 편하게 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13나를 부른 건 좋은 선택이다. 다른 녀석들은 무능하니까. …간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나…. 난 누나와 함께라면 어디까지든 갈게!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이 엄청난 싸움에서 내 힘을 쓸 수 있다니…. 불타는군!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, <value:$28>. 이게 마지막 싸움일까…? 아니, 이걸 마지막으로 만들자! 우리가 이 싸움을 끝내는 거야! 힘내자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17고귀한 엘프인 아타쿠시를 부르지 않으면 끝나지도 않는다니 한심해! 어쨌든 아타쿠시가 왔으니 이런 시시한 일은 빨리 끝내는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19…루루안타들이 지면 모두의 미소가 사라지는 거지. 하지만 <value:$28>는 안 지지? 반드시 힘내자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22역시 이 싸움에도 네 노래를 만드는 내가 있어야지! 그럼 <value:$28>의 노래 절정으로 출발!!<end>좋아, 같이 싸우자.<end>"

## 940013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec094-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2そうね。<line-break>じゃあ、あとは頼んだわよ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>3<value:$28>。<line-break>けっして油断はするな。<line-break>それが長生きのコツじゃ。<line-break>わしの力が必要なら<line-break>いつでも手を貸すぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4私がいないときに<line-break>世界が救われてもぉ、<line-break>私は世界を救った勇者のひとりよね。<line-break>うふふ。<line-break>働かないですむなんて<line-break>とーってもおトクかも！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5俺をおいてくのか？　チクショー！<line-break>でも、お前ならきっとラクショー！<line-break>ププ、こんな時でも俺ってさえてるぜ！<line-break><value:$28>、頑張ってこいよ！<line-break>俺はとっておきのダジャレ用意して<line-break>待ってるからな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6ガンバってね、<value:$28>。<line-break>ボク、キミを信じてる。<line-break>ボク、キミを待ってる。<end><if><value:$01><equal>7<value:$28>。必ず最後まで決着をつけて…。<line-break>ゼリグやドルドラムのためにも…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>8そうですか。<line-break>では、ネメア…息子のこと<line-break>よろしくお願いしますね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9うーん。<line-break>こういう見せ場はカルラちゃんが<line-break>いた方がいいと思うんだけどなー。<line-break>強いし、明るいし、頭いいし、<line-break>オマケにメチャかわいいし！<line-break>呼び出し、待ってるかんねー！<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…そう。<line-break>私の魔の力が必要になったら<line-break>また、呼び出してね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12<value:$28>。<line-break>お前ならやれるさ。<line-break>とにかく、ムリはすんなよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…俺なしで<line-break>やれるのか？<line-break><line-break>…まあいい。<line-break>お手並み拝見といこう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん。<line-break>ムチャはすんなよ。<line-break>俺の力がいるときはすぐ呼べよ。<line-break>俺、姉ちゃんの勝利を<line-break>信じて待ってるよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14わしゃ、まだまだ若いモンには<line-break>負けんとらんわい！<line-break>　<line-break>わしの力がいるときは<line-break>すぐに呼びにくるのじゃ！<line-break>よいな、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>回復はこまめにね。<line-break>どんな敵が待ってるかわからないもの。<line-break>僕、<value:$28>が<line-break>また、呼び出してくれるの<line-break>待ってるよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17高貴で優雅なこのアタクシなしで<line-break>やっていけると思ってるなんて<line-break>思い違いもはなはだしくてよぉー！！<line-break>そんなの１０００年早くてよぉー！<line-break>とにかく、またアタクシを、<line-break>さっさと呼び出すのよぉー！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19うん、ルルアンタ、<line-break>ちょっとつかれちゃった。<line-break>ちょっとだけ、さよならだね。<line-break>でも、でもぉ。<line-break>まだまだガンバれるからぁ。<line-break>たすけてほしいときはよびだしてね！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20………………。<end><if><value:$01><equal>22うーん。、<line-break>ぼくがついていかないで<line-break>誰がきみの歌を作るんだよぉ！<line-break>また、呼び出してね！<line-break>この戦い、<line-break>ぜーったい、見ておきたいんだー。<end>また呼んでね。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2그래. 그럼 나머지는 부탁할게, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3<value:$28>. 절대 방심하지 마라. 그게 오래 사는 비결이다. 내 힘이 필요하면 언제든 도와주마.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4내가 없을 때 세계가 구원돼도 난 세계를 구한 용사 중 하나지? 후후. 일 안 해도 된다니 엄청 이득일지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5나를 두고 가냐? 젠장! 그래도 너라면 낙승이겠지! 푸푸, 이럴 때도 난 감이 좋아! <value:$28>, 힘내고 와! 난 최고의 아재개그 준비해서 기다릴게!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6힘내, <value:$28>. 난 널 믿어. 널 기다릴게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7<value:$28>. 반드시 끝까지 결판을 내…. 젤리그와 돌드람을 위해서도….<end><if><value:$01><equal>8그렇군요. 그럼 네메아… 제 아들을 잘 부탁합니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9음. 이런 볼거리는 카를라가 있어야 할 것 같은데. 강하고 밝고 머리도 좋고 엄청 귀엽잖아! 호출 기다릴게!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…그래. 내 마의 힘이 필요하면 다시 불러 줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12<value:$28>. 너라면 할 수 있어. 아무튼 무리는 하지 마.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…나 없이 할 수 있나? …뭐 좋아. 실력 구경이나 하지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나. 무리하지 마. 내 힘이 필요하면 바로 불러. 누나가 이길 거라 믿고 기다릴게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14나도 아직 젊은 것들에겐 안 진다! 내 힘이 필요하면 바로 부르러 와라! 알겠지, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, <value:$28>. 회복은 자주 해. 어떤 적이 기다릴지 모르니까. 난 <value:$28>가 다시 불러 주길 기다릴게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17고귀하고 우아한 이 아타쿠시 없이 해낼 수 있다고 생각하다니 큰 착각이야! 천 년은 일러! 아무튼 빨리 다시 아타쿠시를 부르는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19응, 루루안타 좀 지쳤어. 잠깐만 안녕이네. 그래도 아직 힘낼 수 있으니까 도움이 필요하면 불러 줘!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>22음, 내가 안 따라가면 누가 네 노래를 만들겠어! 다시 불러 줘! 이 싸움은 꼭 보고 싶어!<end>또 불러 줘.<end>"

## 940014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec094-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者は、町の中ではひとりで行動し、<line-break>町の外に出て冒険するときだけ<line-break>パーティーと一緒になるのが基本だ。<line-break>だから、町の中を歩いてるときは、<line-break>何かあっても仲間が助けに来る<line-break>とは限らない。油断しないようにな。<end>"
EN: "As a rule, adventurers go about town alone and only join their party when venturing outside town. So if something happens while you're in town, don't assume your companions will come to your aid. Keep your guard up.<end>"
KO: "모험자는 도시 안에서는 혼자 행동하고, 도시 밖으로 나가 모험할 때만 파티와 함께하는 게 기본이다. 그러니 도시 안을 걷고 있을 때는 무슨 일이 생겨도 동료가 도와주러 온다는 보장은 없어. 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 940015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec094-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、町の中にある塔や廃城なんかを　<line-break>調べるときなんかは、一緒に行動する<line-break>こともあるだろうがな。<end>"
EN: "Still, there may be times when you travel with companions while investigating places in town, such as the towers or the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 마을 안에 있는 탑이나 폐성 같은 곳을 조사할 때는 함께 행동할 수도 있겠지만 말이지.<end>"

## 940016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec094-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、町の中にある塔や廃城なんかを　<line-break>調べるときなんかは、一緒に行動する<line-break>こともあるだろうがな。<line-break>説明はこんなところだな。<line-break>これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名をあげていってくれよ。<end>"
EN: "That said, you may sometimes travel together when investigating a tower or a Ruined Castle in town. That about covers it. Now get out there, work hard, and make a name for yourself.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 마을 안의 탑이나 폐성을 조사할 때는 같이 행동할 일도 있겠지. 설명은 이 정도다. 이제부터 열심히 일해서 이름을 날려 줘.<end>"

## 950001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たち冒険者のことをバカにする気か？<line-break>この小娘！<end>"
EN: "Are you mocking us adventurers, you little brat?<end>"
KO: "우리 모험자들을 바보 취급하는 거냐? 이 꼬맹이가!<end>"

## 950002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別にバカになんかしてなくてよー？　<line-break>ただ、事実を言ってあげただけ。<line-break>　<line-break>こぉ～んなに、つまらない世界を<line-break>真顔でうろついて冒険ですって？<line-break>ホント笑わせるわ！　くだらないわ！<end>"
EN: "I'm not mocking you. I merely told you the truth. You wander around this dreadfully dull world with straight faces and call it adventure? What a joke! How utterly ridiculous!<end>"
KO: "딱히 바보 취급한 건 아닌데~? 그냥 사실을 말해 준 것뿐이야. 이렇게나 재미없는 세상을 진지한 얼굴로 돌아다니며 모험이라니? 정말 웃겨! 시시해!<end>"

## 950003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、この…！　冒険のすばらしさを<line-break>バカにしやがって…！<end>"
EN: "Y-you... How dare you mock the wonders of adventure!<end>"
KO: "이, 이 자식…! 모험의 훌륭함을 비웃다니…!<end>"

## 950004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険をバカになんてしてないわ。<line-break>あなたがたの程度の低さを指摘したの。<end>"
EN: "I wasn't mocking adventure. I was pointing out how inferior you are.<end>"
KO: "모험을 바보 취급한 게 아니야. 너희 수준이 낮다고 지적한 거지.<end>"

## 950005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっ、こっ、このエルフが！<line-break>ギタギタにしてやる！！<end>"
EN: "Y-you... You damned Elf! I'll tear you to pieces!<end>"
KO: "이, 이, 이 엘프가! 박살을 내 주마!!<end>"

## 950006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、アタクシは高貴なエルフなのよ。<line-break>あなたたち下等生物とは違うのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>すぐ暴力に訴えようとするのね？<line-break>これだから下等生物ってイヤなのよ。<line-break>つまらないわ。ホントつまらない！<end>"
EN: "That's right. I am a noble Elf, quite unlike you inferior creatures. Do you always resort to violence so quickly? That's precisely what I can't stand about inferior creatures. How dull. Utterly dull!<end>"
KO: "그래, 나는 고귀한 엘프야. 너희 하등생물과는 달라. 금방 폭력에 기대려 하네? 이래서 하등생물은 싫어. 시시해. 정말 시시해!<end>"

## 950007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのエルフを助ける<end>"
EN: "Help that elf<end>"
KO: "저 엘프를 돕는다<end>"

## 950008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放っておく<end>"
EN: "Leave the elf alone<end>"
KO: "내버려 둔다<end>"

## 950009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっ、こいつは、俺たちのことを<line-break>バカにしてるんだぞ！<line-break>　<line-break>こんな奴を助ける気か！<line-break>貴様も痛い目にあわねば<line-break>わからんようだな！<end>"
EN: "S-she's making fools of us! You're going to help someone like that? Looks like you won't understand until you've had a taste of pain yourself!<end>"
KO: "이, 이 녀석은 우리를 바보 취급하고 있다고! 이런 녀석을 도울 셈이냐! 너도 호되게 당해 봐야 알겠군!<end>"

## 950010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　この…！<line-break>覚えていろよ！<end>"
EN: "Damn you... Just you remember this!<end>"
KO: "큭! 이 자식…! 두고 봐라!<end>"

## 950011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、そこのエルフ、<line-break>お前も今度会ったらこうしてやる！<line-break>覚えていろよ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, Elf! Next time we meet, you'll get the same! Remember that!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 거기 엘프! 너도 다음에 만나면 이렇게 해 주마! 기억해 둬!<end>"

## 950012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、程度が低いわね、下等生物って！<line-break>こうやってなんでも暴力で<line-break>決めようとするのよねー！<line-break>ま、そこが下等生物が下等生物である<line-break>理由なんでしょうけど、エルフである<line-break>高貴なアタクシには耐えられないわ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, inferior creatures are so terribly crude! You always try to settle everything with violence, don't you? I suppose that's what makes inferior creatures inferior, but a noble Elf such as myself simply cannot abide it!<end>"
KO: "아, 수준 낮아. 하등생물이란! 이렇게 뭐든 폭력으로 결정하려 하지! 뭐, 그게 하등생물이 하등생물인 이유겠지만 엘프인 고귀한 이 몸은 못 견디겠어!<end>"

## 950014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、世界は驚きに満ちている<end>"
EN: "No, the world is full of surprises<end>"
KO: "아니, 세상은 놀라움으로 가득해.<end>"

## 950015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "声をかけない<end>"
EN: "Say nothing<end>"
KO: "말을 걸지 않는다<end>"

## 950016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ～？<end>"
EN: "What?<end>"
KO: "하아~?<end>"

## 950017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっきの、頭の中まで冒険ロマンの<line-break>連中も、そこまで言わなかったわ！<line-break>　<line-break>あなた、あいつらに輪をかけてアホ？<line-break>信じられないうつけ者？　こぉ～んなに<line-break>つまらない世界が驚きに満ちている？<end>"
EN: "Even those adventure-addled fools from before didn't go that far! Are you even more stupid than they were? Some kind of unbelievable simpleton? You think this dreadfully dull world is full of wonders?<end>"
KO: "아까 그 머릿속까지 모험 로망으로 가득 찬 녀석들도 거기까진 말 안 했어! 당신, 걔들보다 더 멍청해? 믿을 수 없는 바보야? 이렇~게나 재미없는 세계가 놀라움으로 가득하다고?<end>"

## 950018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカじゃない？　アホじゃない？<line-break>どこへ行っても新しい驚きなんかない。<line-break>陳腐で何もかもがありふれてたわ。<end>"
EN: "Are you stupid? A complete fool? There's nothing new or surprising anywhere. Everything I saw was stale and commonplace.<end>"
KO: "바보 아니야? 멍청이 아니야? 어디를 가도 새로운 놀라움 같은 건 없어. 진부하고 모든 게 흔해 빠졌어.<end>"

## 950019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の何がつまらないの？<end>"
EN: "What's so boring about the world?<end>"
KO: "세상의 뭐가 재미없는 거야?<end>"

## 950020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、禁断の聖杯って知ってる？<end>"
EN: "Then do you know about the Forbidden Grail?<end>"
KO: "그럼 금단의 성배는 알아?<end>"

## 950021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールのお城はつまらなかったわ。<line-break>門番が立ってるだけだもん。ダメね。<end>"
EN: "The castle in Rostorl was dull. All it had was a guard standing at the gate. No good at all.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 성은 재미없었어. 문지기만 서 있더라. 별로야.<end>"

## 950022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あとは、ウルカーンもつまんない。<line-break>な、何もなかったもん。ダメダメよ！<end>"
EN: "A-and Ulkarne was dull too. There was n-nothing there. No good at all!<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고 울칸도 재미없어. 아, 아무것도 없었어. 완전 별로야!<end>"

## 950023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あとは…何って…、何もかもよ！！<line-break>下等生物のくせに細かくてよぉ！！<line-break>　<line-break>アタクシはつまんないって言う理由を<line-break>言ったんだから、今度はあなたが<line-break>世界はすばらしいって理由を言う番よ！<end>"
EN: "A-and what else...? Everything! For an inferior creature, you certainly do fuss over petty details! I've told you why I find the world dull, so now it's your turn to explain why it's wonderful!<end>"
KO: "아, 나머지는 뭐냐니… 전부 다야!! 하등생물 주제에 너무 세세해!! 이 몸이 재미없는 이유를 말했으니 이제 당신이 세계가 훌륭한 이유를 말할 차례야!<end>"

## 950024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯…？<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail...?<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배…?<end>"

## 950025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールのお城はつまらなかったわ。<line-break>門番が立ってるだけだもん。ダメね。<end>"
EN: "The castle in Rostorl was dull. All it had was a guard standing at the gate. No good at all.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 성은 재미없었어. 문지기만 서 있더라. 별로야.<end>"

## 950026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、し、知らないわけないでしょ。<end>"
EN: "O-of course I know what that is.<end>"
KO: "모, 모, 모를 리가 없잖아.<end>"

## 950027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アタクシは高貴で優雅なエルフなのよ！<line-break>何もかも知り尽くして飽き飽きだわ！<line-break>　<line-break>き、禁断の聖杯は…その…杯なのよ。<line-break>魔法の物品ね。そうよ魔法の杯よぉ！<line-break>禁断…って言うくらいだから、あ！<end>"
EN: "I am a noble and refined Elf! I know everything there is to know, and I'm thoroughly bored with it all! Th-the Forbidden Grail is... well... a cup. A magical object. Yes, a magical cup! And since it's forbidden... Ah!<end>"
KO: "이 몸은 고귀하고 우아한 엘프야! 모르는 게 없어서 지긋지긋하다고! 기, 금단의 성배는… 그… 잔이야. 마법 물품이지. 그래, 마법의 잔이라고! 금단…이라고 하니까, 아!<end>"

## 950028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁酒ができるのよ！　そうよ、酒ほど<line-break>イヤなものはないわ。ドワーフなんかが<line-break>いっつも酒の臭いをプンプンさせて…！<line-break>アレを世界から消し去るために存在する<line-break>杯だわ。酒臭いドワーフを一掃するの！<line-break>そうね…アレはいい線いってるかしら？<end>"
EN: "It can put a stop to drinking! That's right, nothing is more loathsome than alcohol. Dwarves are forever reeking of the stuff...! The grail exists to wipe it from the face of the world. It will rid us of every alcohol-reeking Dwarf! Yes... that sounds about right, doesn't it?<end>"
KO: "금주를 할 수 있는 거야! 그래, 술만큼 싫은 것도 없어. 드워프 같은 것들은 늘 술 냄새를 풍기고 다니잖아…! 저걸 세상에서 없애기 위해 존재하는 잔이야. 술 냄새 나는 드워프를 싹 쓸어버리는 거지! 그래… 저건 꽤 괜찮은데?<end>"

## 950029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ！　違うって、じゃ、じゃあ、<line-break>答えはなんなのよ！　答えなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "What? If that's wrong, th-then what is the answer? Tell me!<end>"
KO: "뭐야! 아니라면 대, 대답이 뭔데! 말해 봐!<end>"

## 950030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何で答えないのよ！<line-break>さっきの奴らの方がまだマシだわ！　　<line-break>　<line-break>あいつらは、バカだったけど、<line-break>何か説明しようとしたもの。<line-break>あなた、高貴なエルフをバカにする気？<end>"
EN: "Why won't you answer? Even those fools from before were better than you! They were idiots, but at least they tried to explain something. Are you mocking a noble Elf?<end>"
KO: "왜 대답 안 하는 거야! 아까 그 녀석들이 차라리 나았어! 걔들은 바보였어도 뭔가 설명하려고 했단 말이야. 너, 고귀한 엘프를 바보 취급할 셈이야?<end>"

## 950031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、待ちなさいよぉ！<line-break>アタクシを置いて行こうなんて、<line-break>１００年早いわ！<end>"
EN: "W-wait! You're a hundred years too early to think you can leave me behind!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐 기다려! 나를 두고 가다니 백 년은 일러!<end>"

## 950032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅についてくれば<line-break>証明できるですって？<line-break>上等じゃない！　よろしくてよ！<line-break>世界が驚きに満ちているって言った<line-break>あなたの言葉、ぜーったい、必ず、<line-break>旅で証明していただくわ！<end>"
EN: "You say you can prove it if I travel with you? Very well! I accept! You said the world was full of wonders, and you will most certainly prove it to me on this journey!<end>"
KO: "같이 여행하면 증명할 수 있다고? 좋아! 그러지! 세상은 놀라움으로 가득하다고 한 네 말, 반드시, 꼭, 여행으로 증명해 줘야 해!<end>"

## 950033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町を出るときに合流よ！　わかって？<line-break>逃げないで来なさいよ、ド下等生物！<end>"
EN: "We'll meet when you leave town. Understood? You'd better show up, you utterly inferior creature!<end>"
KO: "마을을 나갈 때 합류하는 거야! 알겠지? 도망치지 말고 와, 최하등 생물!<end>"

## 950034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷で呼び出すぅ？<end>"
EN: "You'll summon me at Cat Manor?<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택에서 부를까?<end>"

## 950035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、知ってるわよ。そのくらい。<line-break>わかったわ、待っていてあげてよ！<end>"
EN: "O-of course I knew that much. Very well, I'll be gracious enough to wait for you!<end>"
KO: "아, 알고 있어. 그 정도는. 알았어, 기다려줄게!<end>"

## 950037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あなた、他の人間みたいに<line-break>小理屈で説明しないだけマシかもね！<end>"
EN: "Well, perhaps you're better than the other humans. At least you don't try to explain everything with petty arguments!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 당신은 다른 인간들처럼 잔소리 같은 논리로 설명하지 않는 것만 해도 낫네!<end>"

## 950038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、ほめただけで<line-break>調子にのらないようにね！<end>"
EN: "Don't get carried away just because I paid you one little compliment!<end>"
KO: "조금 칭찬했다고 우쭐대지 마!<end>"

## 950039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "名を名乗りなさいよ！　エルフに対して<line-break>名乗らないなんて無礼な下等生物ね！<line-break>　<line-break>言っておきますけど、忘れてたんじゃ<line-break>絶対、絶対、絶対、なくてよ！<line-break>とにかく、早く名乗りなさい、無礼者！<end>"
EN: "State your name! Only a thoroughly ill-mannered inferior creature would fail to introduce oneself to an Elf! And let me make one thing clear: I absolutely, positively, most certainly did not forget to ask! Now hurry up and give me your name, you boor!<end>"
KO: "이름을 밝혀! 엘프 앞에서 이름도 안 대다니 무례한 하등생물이네! 말해 두지만 잊고 있었던 건 절대, 절대, 절대 아니야! 어쨌든 빨리 이름을 대, 무례한 녀석!<end>"

## 950040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>？　いいわね。<line-break>下等生物らしい名前だわ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>アタクシはフェティ。尊敬の念を込めて<line-break>フェティ様と呼んでよくてよ！<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>? Very nice. A fitting name for an inferior creature. I am Fety. You may address me as Lady Fety, with all the proper respect! See you, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>? 괜찮은 이름이네. 하등생물다운 이름이야. 난 페티. 존경을 담아서 페티 님이라고 불러도 좋아! 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 950041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャー！<end>"
EN: "Eek!<end>"
KO: "꺄악!<end>"

## 950042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近は、酒場の中で平気で武器を出す<line-break>物騒な連中が増えてきたなあ…。<line-break>　<line-break>ああ、でも、あんたの行動は<line-break>間違っちゃいないと思うぜ。<line-break>仕方ないって時はあるものさ。<end>"
EN: "These days, more and more dangerous sorts think nothing of drawing weapons inside the tavern... Ah, but I don't think what you did was wrong. Sometimes you don't have a choice.<end>"
KO: "요즘은 술집 안에서 아무렇지도 않게 무기를 꺼내는 험한 녀석들이 늘었군…. 아, 그래도 네 행동이 틀렸다고는 생각 안 해. 어쩔 수 없는 때도 있는 법이지.<end>"

## 950043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "からまれてる女の子を　　　　　　　<line-break>助けに入るなんて<line-break><value:$28>さんらしいですね。<line-break>……私が同じようになっても、<line-break>助けに入って………。<line-break>あ、い、いえ、なんでもないです…。<end>"
EN: "Stepping in to help a girl being harassed... That's just like you, <value:$28>. If the same thing ever happened to me, would you step in and... Ah, n-no, it's nothing...<end>"
KO: "곤란한 여자아이를 도와주러 들어가다니 <value:$28> 씨답네요. ……제가 똑같은 일을 당해도 도와주러……. 아, 아뇨, 아무것도 아니에요….<end>"

## 950044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…うるさいハエが！<end>"
EN: "Gah... You irritating fly!<end>"
KO: "큭… 시끄러운 파리 같으니!<end>"

## 950045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我こそはウルグの円卓騎士、　　　　<line-break>打ち砕く者、ダナン。<line-break>いにしえのドワーフの勇者…。<line-break>我が城に、<line-break>のこのこ入り込む者がいようとは、<line-break>このダナンも、なめられたものよ…。<end>"
EN: "I am Danan the Crusher, one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table. An ancient Dwarf Hero... To think anyone would dare stroll into my castle. It seems even I, Danan, have been sorely underestimated...<end>"
KO: "나는 울그의 원탁기사, 분쇄하는 자 다난. 고대의 드워프 용사…. 내 성에 이렇게 태연히 들어오는 자가 있다니, 이 다난도 우습게 보였군….<end>"

## 950046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が異名の由来を知らぬか！？<line-break>ダナンの前に出た者は、ひとり残らず、<line-break>この闇の神器、憤怒の鎚の餌食よ…。<end>"
EN: "Do you not know how I earned my epithet?! Every last one who has stood before Danan has fallen prey to this Dark Artifact, the Hammer of Wrath...<end>"
KO: "내 이명의 유래를 모르는가!? 다난 앞에 나선 자는 한 명도 남김없이 이 어둠의 신기, 분노의 망치의 제물이 된다….<end>"

## 950047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "熟れた果実のごとく、<line-break>その頭、<line-break>かち割ってくれようぞ！<end>"
EN: "I shall split your skull like a piece of ripe fruit!<end>"
KO: "잘 익은 과일처럼 그 머리를 박살 내 주마!<end>"

## 950048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐぁ…！<line-break>バ、バカな…<end>"
EN: "Gaaah...! I-impossible...<end>"
KO: "크아…! 마, 말도 안 돼….<end>"

## 950049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2…終わったわね。<line-break><value:$28>、大丈夫？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4この人、あんなに強がってたのに<line-break>かなりカッコわるいかも。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5へへっ、ざまあみろってんだ。<line-break>俺たちを甘く見るなっての。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6終わったね、<line-break><value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7…終わったわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11終わったわね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13フッ、口ほどにもない…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10ふぅ、危なかったぜ。<line-break>姉ちゃん、大丈夫か？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14さすがは伝説のドワーフの戦士よ。<line-break>やりおるのう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15…やった。<line-break>危ないところだったね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17フン！　フン！　フンっだ！<line-break>ドワーフなんて、大口たたいたって<line-break>こんなものよね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ふぅ、びっくりした。<line-break>突然おそってくるんだもん。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20………………。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21…ふん、<line-break>威勢がいいのは最初だけか…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23大丈夫か？　<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…終わったな。<line-break>大丈夫か？　<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26ふぅ…。<line-break>危ないところじゃったな<end><if><value:$01><equal>24危ないところでしたね。<line-break>大丈夫でしたか？<line-break><value:$28>様。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25ふぅ…。<line-break>いきなり襲ってくるなんて<line-break>ひどいわよね<end><if><value:$01><equal>192お怪我は、ありませんか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193大丈夫ですか、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>と、突然襲ってきたから<line-break>わ、私、ビックリしてしまって…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194円卓の騎士といっても<line-break>大したことはなかったな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191あたし…無事なのね。<line-break>もう魔人は、相手したくないわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190無事みたいだな。<line-break>何事もなくてよかった。<end>大丈夫、<line-break><value:$28>？ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2It's over... Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4All that bluster, only to end up looking this pathetic.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Heh, serves you right. That's what you get for underestimating us.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6It's over, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7It's over...<end><if><value:$01><equal>11It's over.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hmph. All talk...<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Whew, that was close. You all right, sis?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Just what I'd expect from a legendary Dwarf warrior. Well fought!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Yes... That was close.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Hmph! Hmph! Hmph! Dwarves can boast all they like, but this is all they're capable of!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Whew, that scared me. That attack came out of nowhere!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20………………。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Hmph... So the bravado didn't last long.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27It's over... Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Whew... That was a close one.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24That was a close call. Are you unharmed, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Whew... Attacking without warning like that was just awful.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Are you hurt?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Are you all right, <value:$28>? Th-the attack was so sudden, and I... I was so startled...<end><if><value:$01><equal>194For a Knight of the Round Table, that was nothing special...<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I'm... safe. I never want to face another demon.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Looks like all is well. I'm glad nothing happened.<end>Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2…끝났네. <value:$28>, 괜찮아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4이 사람 그렇게 허세를 부렸는데 꽤 멋없을지도.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5헤헤, 꼴 좋다. 우리를 얕보지 말라고.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6끝났네, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7…끝났어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11끝났네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13훗, 말만 번지르르했군….<end><if><value:$01><equal>10후우, 위험했어. 누나, 괜찮아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14역시 전설의 드워프 전사로군. 제법이야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…해냈다. 위험했네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17흥! 흥! 흥이다! 드워프 같은 건 큰소리쳐도 이 정도네!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19후우, 깜짝 놀랐어. 갑자기 덤벼들잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>21…흥, 기세 좋은 건 처음뿐인가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>23괜찮나? <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…끝났군. 괜찮나? <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26후우…. 위험했구나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24위험할 뻔했습니다. 괜찮으셨습니까? <value:$28> 님.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25후우…. 갑자기 덤벼들다니 너무하잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192다치신 곳은 없습니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193괜찮으세요, <value:$28> 씨. 가, 갑자기 덤벼들어서 저, 저 깜짝 놀라 버렸어요….<end><if><value:$01><equal>194원탁의 기사라 해도 별것 아니었군….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191나… 무사하구나. 이제 마인은 상대하고 싶지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190무사한 모양이군. 아무 일 없어서 다행이다.<end>괜찮아, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 950050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2早くここを出ましょ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5あばよ、ダナン。<line-break>お前もかわいそうな奴だナン。<line-break>…ププププッ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6帰ろう、<value:$28>。<line-break>なんだか、早くここを出たいよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7それじゃあ、<line-break>帰りましょう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…帰るぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10そんじゃ、行こうぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14さぁて、帰って酒でも飲むかの。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>伝説ではボルキスに敗れたあと<line-break>邪竜シャンマと融合したというのに<line-break>あっけなかったのお！　ガハハ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15さあ、<value:$28>、<line-break>帰ろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17さあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>このカビ臭い城から<line-break>１秒でも早く出るのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>21帰るぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23さあ、帰ろう<line-break><value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27さあ、帰ろう<end><if><value:$01><equal>26さあ、ここを出ようか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24それじゃあ、<line-break>帰りましょう、<value:$28>様<end><if><value:$01><equal>25それじゃあ、<line-break>帰りましょう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>192…嫌な予感がします。<line-break>早くここから離れましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193さあ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>早く帰りましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194邪竜シャンマと融合した者が<line-break>この程度で…？<line-break><line-break>…いや考えすぎか。<line-break>さあ、帰ろう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191本当に倒せたのかしら…？<line-break><line-break><line-break>…ふ。私、魔人を過剰に恐がりすぎよね。<line-break>とにかく、ここを出ないと。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…倒せたのか？<line-break>経験上、魔人は油断できない。<line-break>倒せたと思ってもな。<line-break>…いずれにせよ、<line-break>急いでここを離れたほうがいいな。<end>それじゃあ、<line-break>帰ろうよ、<value:$28>。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Let's hurry and get out of here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5So long, Danan. You're one sorry Danan... Pffft!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Let's go home, <value:$28>. I don't know why, but I want to get out of here as soon as we can.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7Well then, let's go home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13We're leaving.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10All right, let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Well then, shall we go home and have a drink? The legends say Danan merged with the Evil Dragon Shanma after being defeated by Borkis, yet that was over in no time! Gahaha!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Come on, <value:$28>! We're getting out of this musty castle as fast as we can!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21We're leaving.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Come on, let's go home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Well, shall we get out of here...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Well then, let us return, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Well then, let's go home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192I have a bad feeling about this... We should get away from here quickly.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Come, <value:$28>. Let's hurry home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Could someone who merged with the Evil Dragon Shanma really be defeated that easily...? No, I'm probably overthinking it. Come on, let's go home.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Did we really win...? Hm. I suppose I'm too afraid of demons. In any case, we need to get out of here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Is it really over...? In my experience, you can never let your guard down around demons, even when you think the fight is finished. Either way, we'd better leave this place quickly.<end>Well then, let's go home, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2빨리 여기서 나가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5잘 가라, 다난. 너도 불쌍한 녀석이구나난. …푸푸풉.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6돌아가자, <value:$28>. 왠지 빨리 여기서 나가고 싶어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7그럼 돌아가죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…돌아간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그럼 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14자, 돌아가서 술이나 마실까. 전설에서는 볼키스에게 패한 뒤 사룡 샨마와 융합했다는데 싱거웠구나! 가하하!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15자, <value:$28>, 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17자, <value:$28>. 이 곰팡내 나는 성에서 1초라도 빨리 나가는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21돌아간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23자, 돌아가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27자, 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26자, 여길 나갈까….<end><if><value:$01><equal>24그럼 돌아가죠, <value:$28> 님.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25그럼 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…불길한 예감이 듭니다. 빨리 여기서 벗어나죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193자, <value:$28> 씨. 빨리 돌아가요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194사룡 샨마와 융합한 자가 이 정도로…? …아니, 너무 생각했나. 자, 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191정말 쓰러뜨린 걸까…? …후. 내가 마인을 너무 무서워하나 봐. 아무튼 여길 나가야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…쓰러뜨린 건가? 경험상 마인은 방심할 수 없어. 쓰러뜨렸다고 생각해도 말이지. …어쨌든 서둘러 여기서 벗어나는 게 좋겠군.<end>그럼 돌아가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>5Who's there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Who's there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Who's there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Who goes there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Who is it?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Who's there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Who's there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Who goes there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Who?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Who is it?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Who is it?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193W-who is it?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Who's there?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Who?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Who's there?!<end>Who?!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>5누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19누구야아!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24누구야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25누구야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192누구시죠?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193누, 누구세요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194누구냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191누구야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190누구냐!?<end>누구야!?<end>"

## 950053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　余は邪竜シャンマ…　　　　　<line-break>　　　　いにしえ、ボルキスに敗れ、<line-break>　　そのおり、ダナンに力を盗み取られ<line-break>　　　　　　悠久の年を経しが…<end>"
EN: "I am Shanma, the Evil Dragon... Long ago, Borkis defeated me, and in that moment Danan stole my power. Countless ages have passed, but...<end>"
KO: "나는 사룡 샨마… 오래전 볼키스에게 패하고, 그때 다난에게 힘을 빼앗긴 채 유구한 세월을 보냈으나…<end>"

## 950054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　うぬの働きにより　　<line-break>　　　　今ここに解放された…<line-break>　　　まずは、うぬを喰い裂き…<line-break>　　世界を破壊してくれようぞ…！<end>"
EN: "Your deeds have set me free here and now... First, I shall rip you apart... and then destroy the world...!<end>"
KO: "네 활약으로 지금 이곳에 해방되었다…. 우선 네놈부터 찢어 먹고… 세계를 파괴해 주마…!<end>"

## 950055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜シャンマ…！<end>"
EN: "Shanma, the Evil Dragon...!<end>"
KO: "사룡 샨마…!<end>"

## 950056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>4う～ん、アカデミーで習ったかも…。<line-break>ボルキスに敗れて王になり損なった<line-break>ドワーフの戦士ダナンは…<line-break>えと、確か…、<line-break>ボルキスに退治された邪竜シャンマと<line-break>融合し、力を得て…<end><if><value:$01><equal>8さて、いよいよ…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9おっさん、おっさん。<line-break>あんた、ほんとにダナン？<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…ダナン？<line-break>彼、さっき、<line-break>ドワーフのダナンって言ったわよね。<line-break>ということは、彼が、<line-break>邪竜シャンマと融合した<line-break>ドワーフの戦士ダナン…！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>16さて…、これからだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…感じる。<end>終わった…のか？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Hmm, I think we learned about this at the Academy... Danan, the Dwarf warrior who failed to become king after Borkis defeated him... Um, if I remember right, he gained power by merging with Shanma, the Evil Dragon defeated by Borkis...<end><if><value:$01><equal>8Now, at last...<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Hey, old man. Are you really Danan?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11...Danan? He said he was Danan the Dwarf, didn't he? Then he's Danan, the Dwarf warrior who merged with Shanma, the Evil Dragon...?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>16Well... here we go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...I can feel it.<end>Is it... over?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4음~ 아카데미에서 배운 것 같기도…. 볼키스에게 패해 왕이 되지 못한 드워프 전사 다난은… 어, 분명… 볼키스에게 퇴치된 사룡 샨마와 융합해 힘을 얻고…<end><if><value:$01><equal>8자, 드디어….<end><if><value:$01><equal>9아저씨, 아저씨. 당신 정말 다난이야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…다난? 저 사람, 아까 드워프 다난이라고 했지. 그렇다면 저 사람이 사룡 샨마와 융합한 드워프 전사 다난…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>16자… 이제부터군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…느껴져.<end>끝난… 건가?<end>"

## 950057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! Could it be...?!<end>Shh...!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4꺅! 혹시…!<end>쉿…!<end>"

## 950058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、余を…<line-break>余を引きずり出すとは…<line-break>うつけた者どもよ…<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... To think you would drag me out... You fools...<end>"
KO: "후후, 짐을… 짐을 끌어내다니… 어리석은 자들이여….<end>"

## 950059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3正体を現しおったな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃん、やっぱり大当たり！<line-break>アカデミーで習ったことでも<line-break>たまには役に立つのかも！<line-break>だけど、ここで<line-break>食べられちゃったら<line-break>実は役に立ってないかも！　きゃっ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>8真打ち登場ですか？<line-break>邪竜シャンマ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>9フフ、雪辱戦ってわけ？<line-break>こうじゃなきゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>11…邪竜シャンマ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>12やれやれ、やっと、<line-break>真打ち登場ってわけだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>16…きたか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…きたか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23…邪竜シャンマ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26正体を現したな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>24邪竜シャンマ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>25邪竜シャンマ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>192…邪竜。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>193あ、あれが邪竜！？<line-break>あんな恐ろしいのと<line-break>戦っちゃうんですか！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194邪竜シャンマ！<line-break><if><value:$01><equal>191………………！<line-break>まだ死んでなかったのね…。<line-break><line-break>…いいわ、<line-break>何度でも倒してあげようじゃないの！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190やはり正体を隠していたか！<line-break>いくぞ、<value:$28>。<end>ようやく正体を現したか！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3So you've shown your true form!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! I knew I was right! Maybe the things they teach at the Academy really do come in handy sometimes! Though if I get eaten here, I suppose they didn't help after all! Eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8So the main attraction has arrived? Shanma, the Evil Dragon!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Heh. A chance to settle the score, huh? That's more like it!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…Shanma, the Evil Dragon!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Good grief. At last, the main attraction.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16So, you've come.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20So, you've come.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…Shanma, the Evil Dragon!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26So you've shown your true form!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Shanma, the Evil Dragon!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Shanma, the Evil Dragon!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…The Evil Dragon.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Th-that's the Evil Dragon?! We're going to fight something that terrifying?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Shanma, the Evil Dragon!<line-break><if><value:$01><equal>191…! So you weren't dead after all... Fine, then. I'll defeat you as many times as I have to!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190So you were hiding your true form after all! Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>So you've finally shown your true form!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3정체를 드러냈구나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅, 역시 정답! 아카데미에서 배운 것도 가끔은 쓸모가 있나 봐! 하지만 여기서 잡아먹히면 사실 쓸모없었던 걸지도! 꺅!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8진짜 주인공 등장인가요? 사룡 샨마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9후후, 설욕전이란 거야? 이래야지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…사룡 샨마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12이런, 드디어 진짜가 등장한 셈이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16…왔나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…왔나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…사룡 샨마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26정체를 드러냈구나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24사룡 샨마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25사룡 샨마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…사룡.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193저, 저게 사룡!? 저렇게 무서운 것과 싸우는 건가요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194사룡 샨마!<if><value:$01><equal>191………………! 아직 죽지 않았구나…. …좋아, 몇 번이든 쓰러뜨려 주겠어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190역시 정체를 숨기고 있었군! 간다, <value:$28>.<end>드디어 정체를 드러냈나!<end>"

## 950060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだだよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>かつて、ダナンはボルキスと<line-break>ドワーフ王の座をかけて闘い敗れたんだ。<line-break>ダナンはくやしさのあまり闇に落ちた。<end>"
EN: "It's not over yet, <value:$28>. Long ago, Danan fought Borkis for the Dwarf throne and lost. The bitterness of defeat drove Danan into darkness.<end>"
KO: "아직이야, <value:$28>. 옛날 다난은 볼키스와 드워프 왕 자리를 놓고 싸웠다가 패배했어. 다난은 분한 나머지 어둠에 떨어졌지.<end>"

## 950061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして力を求め、<line-break>ボルキスに倒された邪竜シャンマと<line-break>融合した…。<line-break>ダナンが倒れた今、<line-break>シャンマが立ち上がるよ。<line-break>気をつけて。<end>"
EN: "Seeking power, Danan fused with Shanma, the Evil Dragon defeated by Borkis... Now that Danan has fallen, Shanma will rise. Be careful.<end>"
KO: "그리고 힘을 원해 볼키스에게 쓰러진 사룡 샨마와 융합했어… 다난이 쓰러진 지금, 샨마가 일어설 거야. 조심해.<end>"

## 950062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　さすがは地の巫女よ…　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　余のこと、よう知っておる…。<end>"
EN: "Just what I would expect from the Priestess of Earth... You know me well.<end>"
KO: "역시 땅의 무녀로구나…. 짐을 아주 잘 알고 있어….<end>"

## 950063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　余は邪竜シャンマ、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　ダナンを滅ぼしたること、感謝する…。<line-break>　　　　うぬが解放したる余の力、<line-break>　　　その身でとくと味わうがよい…。<end>"
EN: "I am Shanma, the Evil Dragon. You have my thanks for destroying Danan... Now taste for yourself the power of mine that you have unleashed...<end>"
KO: "나는 사룡 샨마. 다난을 멸망시켜 준 것에 감사한다…. 네가 해방한 나의 힘, 그 몸으로 똑똑히 맛보아라….<end>"

## 950064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁーりぃー！<line-break>ま、ボクたちにかかれば<line-break>こんなもんさ！<line-break>さ、行こう、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We did it! Well, that's about what you'd expect from us! Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "해냈다! 뭐, 우리한테 걸리면 이 정도지! 자, 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 950065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わぁああ！<line-break>な、何？　なんなの！？<end>"
EN: "Aaaah! Wh-what? What's happening?!<end>"
KO: "와아아! 뭐, 뭐야? 대체 뭐야!?<end>"

## 950066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、邪竜シャンマぁ！？<end>"
EN: "Sh-Shanma, the Evil Dragon?!<end>"
KO: "사, 사룡 샨마아!?<end>"

## 950067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（やった…！）<end>"
EN: "(Yes...!)<end>"
KO: "(해냈다…!)<end>"

## 950068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…<line-break>フフフ…<end>"
EN: "Heh... Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "후후… 후후후…<end>"

## 950069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　人の子ごときに敗れるとは…。　　　　<line-break>　　我ら、神々とともに生まれし生き物の時代は…<line-break>　　　　　　　　終わったの…か…？<end>"
EN: "To think I could be defeated by a mere human... Has the age of creatures such as us, born alongside the gods... come to an end...?<end>"
KO: "인간 따위에게 패하다니…. 우리, 신들과 함께 태어난 생물들의 시대는… 끝난 것인가…?<end>"

## 950072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　逃げるか…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　それもよかろう。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　我は解放されさえすれば、<line-break>　　　　　あとは力をたくわえるのみ。<line-break>　　　　　いずれ世界を滅してみせよう。<end>"
EN: "So, you flee... Very well. All I need is my freedom; then I need only gather my strength. In time, I shall destroy the world.<end>"
KO: "도망치겠는가…. 그것도 좋겠지. 나는 해방되기만 하면 그 뒤엔 힘을 모을 뿐이다. 언젠가 세계를 멸망시켜 주마.<end>"

## 950073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec095-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 960000
