## 980002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キス…<line-break>してくれました。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>おかえりなさい。<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>…不安だったです。<end>"
EN: "You kissed me... Welcome back, <value:$28>. I was worried.<end>"
KO: "키스… 해 줬어요. 돌아오셨군요, <value:$28>님. …불안했어요.<end>"

## 980003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう一度…。<end>"
EN: "One more time...<end>"
KO: "다시 한 번….<end>"

## 980004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日も飲むぞお！<line-break>今日も飲むぞお！！<line-break>今日も飲むぞお！！！<end>"
EN: "Gonna drink again today! Gonna drink again today!! Gonna drink again today!!!<end>"
KO: "오늘도 마신다! 오늘도 마신다!! 오늘도 마신다!!!<end>"

## 980005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "飲むのはいいけど、勘定のときになると<line-break>酔いつぶれるのはやめてくれよな。<line-break>いっつも俺ばかり、払わされてるよ。<end>"
EN: "I don't mind you drinking, but quit passing out when the bill comes. I'm always the one who ends up paying.<end>"
KO: "마시는 건 좋은데 계산할 때만 되면 취해 쓰러지는 건 그만해. 늘 나만 돈을 내게 되잖아.<end>"

## 980006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃいませー。<end>"
EN: "Welcome!<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요.<end>"

## 980007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、行っちゃうんですか…？　　　　<line-break>あ、あの…、<line-break>　<line-break>いえっ！　ありがとうございましたー！<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね！<end>"
EN: "Oh... are you leaving...? Um, I... N-never mind! Thank you very much! Please come again!<end>"
KO: "아… 가시는 건가요…? 저, 저기… 아니에요! 감사합니다! 또 와 주세요!<end>"

## 980008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪悪を切り裂く青い稲妻、それは俺！<line-break>♪愛の奇跡をかなえる男、それも俺！<line-break>　<line-break>♪真実の愛の気高さが<line-break>♪君との愛の輝きが<line-break>♪未来の勇者に力をく・れ・る～！<end>"
EN: "♪The blue bolt of lightning that cleaves evil―that's me! ♪The man who makes miracles of love come true―that's me too! ♪The nobility of true love, ♪The radiance of the love we share, ♪Give strength to the Hero of the fu-ture!<end>"
KO: "♪악을 가르는 푸른 번개, 그건 바로 나! ♪사랑의 기적을 이루는 남자, 그것도 나! ♪진실한 사랑의 고귀함이 ♪너와의 사랑의 빛이 ♪미래의 용사에게 힘을 주·는·거야~!<end>"

## 980009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪愛されすぎて最・強！<line-break>♪強すぎるのも愛・嬌！<line-break>　<line-break>♪俺の名前はガルドラ～ン<line-break>♪未来の勇者ガルドラ～ン<line-break>♪愛の翼を　君に～<end>"
EN: "♪So adored, I'm in-vin-cible! ♪Too strong, but that's part of my cha-arm! ♪My name is Girldlan! ♪Girldlan, Hero of the Fu-ture! ♪I give you the wings of love!<end>"
KO: "♪사랑받아 최강! ♪너무 강한 것도 애교! ♪내 이름은 갈드란~ ♪미래의 용사 갈드란~ ♪사랑의 날개를 너에게~<end>"

## 980010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪ウォウウォウ、きれいだぜー<line-break>♪君の指、その１本１本までー<line-break>　<line-break>♪ウォウウォウ、勇者の槍もー<line-break>♪ウォウウォウ、海に沈むよー<line-break>♪俺の愛という海の中ー<end>"
EN: "♪Whoa, whoa, you're beautiful― ♪Every last one of your fingers― ♪Whoa, whoa, even the Hero's spear― ♪Whoa, whoa, sinks beneath the waves― ♪Into the sea that is my love―<end>"
KO: "♪워우워우, 아름다워- ♪너의 손가락, 그 하나하나까지- ♪워우워우, 용자의 창도- ♪워우워우, 바다에 가라앉아- ♪내 사랑이라는 바닷속에-<end>"

## 980011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪俺は歌うよ<line-break>♪俺はその愛の海の幻の人魚ー<line-break>♪君の指をそっと歌でからめ取るー<line-break>♪結構、ムチャな感じでー<line-break>♪果ての見えない愛の海の中にー<line-break>ヘイ！<end>"
EN: "♪I sing to you― ♪I'm the phantom mermaid in that sea of love― ♪Gently ensnaring your fingers with my song― ♪In a rather reckless sort of way― ♪And drawing them into love's endless sea― Hey!<end>"
KO: "♪나는 노래하네♪나는 그 사랑의 바다에 사는 환상의 인어ー♪그대의 손가락을 살며시 노래로 휘감네ー♪꽤나 무리한 느낌으로ー♪끝이 보이지 않는 사랑의 바닷속으로ー헤이!<end>"

## 980012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪愛　それが俺の生きる力<line-break>♪夢　君と生きていけるなら<line-break>　<line-break>♪いつまでも　離しはしない<line-break>♪俺の愛の翼が　折れることはない<line-break>♪君との　愛が神話になる日まで<end>"
EN: "♪Love―that is the strength I live by. ♪Dream―if I can go on living with you. ♪I'll never let you go. ♪The wings of my love shall never break, ♪Until the day our love becomes legend.<end>"
KO: "♪사랑, 그것이 내가 살아가는 힘 ♪꿈, 너와 함께 살아갈 수 있다면 ♪언제까지나 놓지 않아 ♪내 사랑의 날개는 꺾이지 않아 ♪너와의 사랑이 신화가 되는 날까지<end>"

## 980013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪歌うよ　君への想いを<line-break>♪欲しいよ　君の愛がもっと<line-break>♪フェルム　君を愛しているから<line-break>"
EN: "♪I'll sing of all I feel for you. ♪I want more, more of your love. ♪Ferme, because I love you."
KO: "♪노래할게 너를 향한 마음을 ♪원해 네 사랑을 더 많이 ♪페름, 널 사랑하니까"

## 980014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェルムちゃん、<line-break>俺様にはわかっている。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>シャイな君が恥ずかしがって<line-break>隠れながら、俺様の愛の歌に<line-break>心ときめかせていることを！<end>"
EN: "Dear Ferme, I know the truth. You're shy, so you hide away in embarrassment, your heart racing as you listen to my song of love!<end>"
KO: "페름, 이 몸은 알고 있다. 수줍은 네가 숨어서도 이 몸의 사랑 노래에 두근거리고 있다는 걸!<end>"

## 980015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、君に必要なのは勇気だ。<line-break>ただ、君は俺様の前に<line-break>姿を現すだけでいい。<line-break>そうすれば、俺様の愛の波が<line-break>君を愛の海へとさらっていく。<line-break>さあ、俺様の胸に飛び込んでこい！<end>"
EN: "What you need now is courage. You need only show yourself before me. Then the waves of my love will sweep you away into the sea of love. Come, leap into my arms!<end>"
KO: "지금 너에게 필요한 건 용기다. 그저 내 앞에 모습을 드러내기만 하면 돼. 그러면 이 몸의 사랑의 파도가 너를 사랑의 바다로 휩쓸고 갈 것이다. 자, 이 몸의 품으로 뛰어들어 와라!<end>"

## 980016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、誰もが俺様のように<line-break>愛の勇者になれるわけはないか。<line-break>　<line-break>俺様は行くよ。だが、また来る。<line-break>君が勇気を出して俺様の胸に<line-break>飛び込んでくる日まで。何万度でも！<end>"
EN: "Heh. I suppose not everyone can be a Hero of Love like me. I shall go now, but I will return. Until the day you find the courage to leap into my arms, I will return tens of thousands of times!<end>"
KO: "후, 누구나 나님처럼 사랑의 용사가 될 수 있는 건 아닌가 보군. 나님은 간다. 하지만 또 오겠다. 네가 용기를 내 나님의 품으로 뛰어드는 날까지. 몇만 번이라도!<end>"

## 980017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>さん！<line-break>恐かったです！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! I was so scared!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 씨! 무서웠어요!<end>"

## 980018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>さんのおかげで<line-break>少し、落ち着きました。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, <value:$28>, I've calmed down a little.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님 덕분에 조금 진정됐습니다.<end>"

## 980019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、来てくださいね。<line-break>最近、あの人よく来るんです。<end>"
EN: "Please come again. That person has been coming by a lot lately.<end>"
KO: "또 오세요. 요즘 그 사람 자주 오거든요.<end>"

## 980020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってよ。聖杯は<line-break>まだ取り返してないのよ？<line-break>　<line-break>それで、おめおめと<line-break>ロストールに帰るつもり？<end>"
EN: "Wait. We still haven't recovered the Grail, have we? Are you really planning to slink back to Rostorl like this?<end>"
KO: "잠깐. 성배는 아직 되찾지 못했잖아? 그런데 이대로 로스톨로 돌아갈 생각이야?<end>"

## 980021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでもロストールに入る<end>"
EN: "Enter Rostorl anyway<end>"
KO: "그래도 로스톨로 들어간다<end>"

## 980022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンに従ってロストールを出る<end>"
EN: "Follow Irene and leave Rostorl<end>"
KO: "아이린을 따라 로스톨을 떠난다<end>"

## 980023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3じゃ、行けば。<line-break>みっともない。<line-break>私は街には入らないで待ってる。<line-break>よーっぽど、<line-break>フェルムちゃんのことが<line-break>気になるみたいね。<end>あ、そ。<line-break>じゃ、行けば？<line-break>私は外で待ってる。<line-break>あなたがフェルムちゃんと仲よく<line-break>酒場で働きたいって言うんなら、<line-break>私にはとめられないもんね。<end>私は街には入らない。<line-break>ひとりで行きなさいよ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、フェルムちゃんだって、<line-break>いつまでもあなたのこと、<line-break>好きでいるとは思えないけど？<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Then go. You're making a fool of yourself. I'm not going into town; I'll wait here. Ferme must really be on your mind.<end>Oh, really? Then go. I'll wait outside. If you want to work at the tavern and be all friendly with Ferme, I can't stop you.<end>I'm not going into town. Go by yourself. But I don't think Ferme's going to keep liking you forever.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3그럼 가든가. 꼴사납네. 난 마을에는 안 들어가고 기다릴 거야. 페름이 그렇게나 신경 쓰이는 모양이네.<end>아, 그래. 그럼 가든가? 난 밖에서 기다릴게. 네가 페름과 사이좋게 술집에서 일하고 싶다는 거라면 내가 막을 수는 없지.<end>난 마을에는 안 들어갈 거야. 혼자 가. 하지만 페름도 언제까지나 널 좋아하고 있을 거라고는 생각하지 마.<end>"

## 980024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当たり前でしょ。<line-break>さ、行くわよ。<end>"
EN: "Of course. Come on, let's go.<end>"
KO: "당연하잖아. 자, 가자.<end>"

## 980025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうかしたの<value:$28>。<line-break>眠れないの？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong, <value:$28>? Can't you sleep?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이야, <value:$28>. 잠이 안 와?<end>"

## 980026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、どうしたのよ、突然。<line-break>なんで騎士になりたいのか、だなんて。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Where did that come from all of a sudden―asking why I want to be a knight?<end>"
KO: "후훗, 갑자기 왜 그래. 왜 기사가 되고 싶냐니.<end>"

## 980027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうねえ…。やっぱり、一番大きいのは<line-break>父さんが騎士だったからかな。<line-break>だから私も当然騎士になると思ってた。<line-break>ロストールでは女は騎士になれない<line-break>なんてこと、知らなかったしね。<line-break>あとは…。<end>"
EN: "Well... I suppose the main reason is that my father was a knight. I always assumed I'd become one too. I didn't know women couldn't be knights in Rostorl. Then there's...<end>"
KO: "글쎄…. 가장 큰 이유는 역시 아버지가 기사였기 때문일까. 그래서 나도 당연히 기사가 될 줄 알았어. 로스톨에선 여자가 기사가 될 수 없다는 것도 몰랐고. 그리고 또….<end>"

## 980028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ先生が前に言ってたでしょ？　　<line-break>世界は痛みに満ちているって…。<line-break>だから、私、知っておきたいの。<line-break>どうして痛みが存在するのか。<line-break>そして、どうすれば<line-break>痛みを乗り越えられるのかを…。<end>"
EN: "Remember what Master Ossi said before? That the world is full of pain... I want to understand why pain exists, and how to overcome it.<end>"
KO: "오시 선생님이 전에 말했잖아? 세상은 고통으로 가득하다고…. 그래서 난 알고 싶어. 왜 고통이 존재하는지, 그리고 어떻게 해야 고통을 극복할 수 있는지를….<end>"

## 980029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…んー、<line-break>すっごくわかりやすく言うとね…。<end>"
EN: "Mm... To put it as simply as I can...<end>"
KO: "…음, 아주 쉽게 말하자면 말이지….<end>"

## 980030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…守りたい、のよ。<end>"
EN: "...I want to protect.<end>"
KO: "…지키고 싶어.<end>"

## 980031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（アイリーンがギルドで待っている。<line-break>　早く行かないと、<line-break>　何を言われるかわからない…。）<end>"
EN: "(Irene's waiting at the Guild. If I don't hurry, there's no telling what she'll say...)<end>"
KO: "(아이린이 길드에서 기다리고 있다. 빨리 가지 않으면 무슨 말을 들을지 모르겠어….)<end>"

## 980032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（たしか、ゴブリンたちは<line-break>　スラムに行くって言っていた。<line-break>　スラムに戻ってみよう。）<end>"
EN: "(If I remember right, the goblins said they were going to the Slums. I'll head back there and see.)<end>"
KO: "(분명 고블린들은 슬럼으로 간다고 했어. 슬럼으로 돌아가 보자.)<end>"

## 980033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が一緒にいることは　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>のためにならない…。<line-break>　<line-break>心の中では気づいていたことだけど、<line-break>はっきりと言葉にして認めると<line-break>かなり、つらいものね…。<end>"
EN: "Being with me isn't good for <value:$28>... I knew it deep down, but admitting it out loud really hurts.<end>"
KO: "내가 함께 있는 건 <value:$28>에게 도움이 되지 않아…. 마음속으로는 알고 있었지만 이렇게 말로 인정하고 나니 꽤 괴롭네….<end>"

## 980034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は独り立ちすべき。<line-break>だけど、私はそばにいるとつい…。<line-break>だから、私は離れた方がいい。　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> needs to become independent. But whenever I'm around, I always end up... That's why it's better if I stay away.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 독립해야 해. 하지만 내가 곁에 있으면 자꾸…. 그러니 내가 떨어져 있는 편이 나아.<end>"

## 980035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…頭では理解できるわ。<line-break>だけど、心が…。<end>"
EN: "...Intellectually, I understand. But my heart...<end>"
KO: "…머리로는 이해해. 하지만 마음이….<end>"

## 980036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、<line-break>そこの冒険者。<end>"
EN: "Hey, adventurer―you there.<end>"
KO: "저기, 거기 모험자.<end>"

## 980037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、あなたよ。<line-break>冒険者その１。<end>"
EN: "Yes, you. Adventurer Number One.<end>"
KO: "그래, 당신 말이야. 모험자 1번.<end>"

## 980038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>？<line-break>それが、あなたの名前？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ずいぶん、もっともらしい名前ね。<line-break>あなたなんて冒険者その１で十分よ。<line-break>来て。聞きたいことがあるの。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>? That's your name? It certainly sounds convincing. But Adventurer Number One will do for you. Come here. I have something to ask.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>? 그게 네 이름이야? 꽤 그럴듯한 이름이네. 너 같은 건 모험자 1이면 충분해. 따라와. 묻고 싶은 게 있어.<end>"

## 980039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、しゃべるゴブリンを<line-break>知らない？　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>先に言っておきますけれど、ふつうは<line-break>モンスターがしゃべったりしない<line-break>なんてことは、わかってるわよ。<end>"
EN: "Do you know any talking Goblins? And before you say anything, yes, I know monsters don't normally talk.<end>"
KO: "당신, 말하는 고블린 몰라? 미리 말해 두는데 보통 몬스터는 말을 하지 않는다는 정도는 나도 알고 있어.<end>"

## 980040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、言葉をしゃべるゴブリンが<line-break>実在するの。３匹組でゴブゴブ団<line-break>って名乗ってるわ。知ってる？<end>"
EN: "But there really are Goblins that can speak. There are three of them, and they call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang. Do you know them?<end>"
KO: "그런데 말을 하는 고블린이 실제로 있어. 세 마리가 한 조가 되어 고브고브단이라고 자칭해. 알고 있어?<end>"

## 980041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…知らないんなら、別にいいのよ。<line-break>行っていいわ…。<end>"
EN: "If you don't know them, never mind. You can go...<end>"
KO: "…모른다면 됐어. 가도 돼….<end>"

## 980042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…って、知ってるの！？<line-break>どこよ、どこで見かけたのよ！<end>"
EN: "Wait, you do know them?! Where? Where did you see them?!<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 알고 있어!? 어디야, 어디서 봤어!<end>"

## 980043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったわ。<line-break><value:$28>だっけ？<line-break>ありがとうね。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>私はアイリーン。３匹のゴブリンに<line-break>盗まれた禁断の聖杯を追ってるの。<line-break>縁があったらまた会いましょ！<end>"
EN: "Got it. <value:$28>, was it? Thank you. I'm Irene. I'm searching for the Forbidden Grail those three Goblins stole. Perhaps our paths will cross again!<end>"
KO: "알았어. <value:$28>였지? 고마워. 나는 아이린. 세 마리 고블린에게 도둑맞은 금단의 성배를 쫓고 있어. 인연이 있으면 또 만나자!<end>"

## 980044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>どう？　うまくやってる？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. How are things? Doing well?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>잖아. 어때? 잘 지내고 있어?<end>"

## 980045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…。私も負けてられないわね。<end>"
EN: "I see... Then I'd better not let myself fall behind.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 나도 질 수만은 없겠네.<end>"

## 980046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　私？　ああ、そうね。<line-break>私は、騎士を目指してがんばってるわ。<line-break>話してなかったっけ、騎士志望だって。<line-break>…死んだ父さんも騎士だったから。<line-break>ほら、そういうの、よくあるじゃない？<end>"
EN: "Me? Oh, right. I'm working hard to become a knight. Didn't I tell you that was what I wanted? My late father was a knight too. You know how these things go.<end>"
KO: "응? 나? 아, 그래. 난 기사가 되려고 노력하고 있어. 기사 지망이라고 말 안 했던가? …돌아가신 아버지도 기사였거든. 있잖아, 그런 거 흔하잖아?<end>"

## 980047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私の生まれたロストールでは　　<line-break>女は騎士になれないの。<line-break>頭にきちゃうけど、しょうがないわ。<end>"
EN: "But women aren't allowed to become knights in Rostorl, where I was born. It infuriates me, but there's nothing I can do about it.<end>"
KO: "하지만 내가 태어난 로스톨에서는 여자는 기사가 될 수 없어. 화나지만 어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 980048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからね、こうして、聖杯を追いながら<line-break>私を騎士として登用してくれる国を<line-break>探しているってわけ。<end>"
EN: "So while I'm pursuing the Grail, I'm also looking for a country willing to take me on as a knight.<end>"
KO: "그래서 이렇게 성배를 쫓으면서 나를 기사로 받아줄 나라를 찾고 있다는 거야.<end>"

## 980049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私の生まれたロストールでは<line-break>女は騎士になれないの。<line-break>頭にきちゃうけど、しょうがないわ。<line-break>だからね、こうして、聖杯を追いながら<line-break>私を騎士として登用してくれる国を<line-break>探しているってわけ。<end>"
EN: "But women aren't allowed to become knights in Rostorl, where I was born. It infuriates me, but there's nothing I can do about it. So while I'm pursuing the Grail, I'm also looking for a country willing to take me on as a knight.<end>"
KO: "하지만 내가 태어난 로스톨에서는 여자는 기사가 될 수 없어. 화가 나지만 어쩔 수 없지. 그래서 이렇게 성배를 쫓으면서 나를 기사로 등용해 줄 나라를 찾고 있는 거야.<end>"

## 980050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の腕なら、<line-break>どこの軍でも通用するんだけど、<line-break>この美貌がじゃましてくれちゃってね。<line-break>でも、その程度の目しか<line-break>持っていない連中とは<line-break>一緒に戦いたくないじゃない？<end>"
EN: "With my skill, I'd be an asset to any army, but my beauty keeps getting in the way. Then again, I wouldn't want to fight alongside people who can't see past that, would I?<end>"
KO: "내 실력이라면 어느 군대에서도 통하지만 이 미모가 방해해서 말이야. 그래도 그 정도 안목밖에 없는 녀석들과는 함께 싸우고 싶지 않잖아?<end>"

## 980051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な～に？　あなたも私の腕を疑うの？<line-break>こう見えても、オッシ先生の道場では<line-break>敵なしだったのよ。試してみる？<end>"
EN: "What? You doubt my skill too? I may not look it, but no one at Master Ossi's training hall could beat me. Care to put me to the test?<end>"
KO: "뭐~야? 당신도 내 실력을 의심해? 이래 보여도 오시 선생님 도장에서는 적수가 없었어. 시험해 볼래?<end>"

## 980052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、冗談よ。心配しなくてもいいわ。<line-break>こんな往来で振り回せるほど<line-break>私の剣は安くないの。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm joking. Don't worry. My sword isn't so cheap that I'd brandish it in the middle of the street.<end>"
KO: "후후, 농담이야. 걱정 안 해도 돼. 이런 길거리에서 휘두를 만큼 내 검은 싸구려가 아니거든.<end>"

## 980053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>お互い、がんばりましょう。<end>"
EN: "See you, <value:$28>. Let's both do our best.<end>"
KO: "그럼 또 봐, <value:$28>. 서로 힘내자.<end>"

## 980054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3あ、<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>久しぶりね？<end>あら、<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>偶然ね。<end>あ、<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>元気そうね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Oh, <value:$28>! It's been a while, hasn't it?<end>Why, if it isn't <value:$28>. What a coincidence.<end>Oh, <value:$28>! You look well.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3아, <value:$28> 아니야. 오랜만이네?<end>어머, <value:$28> 아니야. 우연이네.<end>아, <value:$28> 아니야. 건강해 보이네.<end>"

## 980055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　オッシ先生？<line-break>あなた、先生のことを知っているの？<end>"
EN: "What? Master Ossi? You know Master Ossi?<end>"
KO: "네? 오시 선생님? 당신, 선생님을 알고 있어요?<end>"

## 980057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなこと、<line-break>先生には言ったことないけど、<line-break>先生に学べて光栄に思っているわ。　<line-break>あなたも機会があったら<line-break>一度会ってみるといいんじゃない？<line-break>ロストールではちょっとした有名人よ。<end>"
EN: "I've never told Master Ossi this, but I'm honored to be his student. If you ever get the chance, you should meet him. He's something of a celebrity in Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "이런 말 선생님께 한 적은 없지만 선생님께 배울 수 있어 영광이라고 생각해. 너도 기회가 있으면 한번 만나 봐. 로스톨에서는 꽤 유명한 사람이야.<end>"

## 980058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>例のゴブリンを見かけたら、教えてね。<end>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたも気をつけるのよ。<end>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>聖杯のことを聞いたら教えてね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3See you later, <value:$28>. Let me know if you spot that Goblin.<end>See you later, <value:$28>. You be careful too.<end>See you later, <value:$28>. Let me know if you hear anything about the Grail.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 그 고블린을 보면 알려 줘.<end>그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 너도 조심해.<end>그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 성배 이야기를 들으면 알려 줘.<end>"

## 980059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3それよりもスゴイと思わない？<line-break>ノエルって子がリーダーのパーティ。<line-break>最近、大活躍みたいじゃない？<line-break>私も夢に向かって<line-break>がんばらなくっちゃ。<line-break>おなじロストール人の女の子だもんね。<end>ねえ、聞いた？<line-break>ノエルって子がリーダーのパーティ、<line-break>また、大活躍だったみたいよ？<line-break>彼女、勇者ネメアの向こうを張って<line-break>小さな勇者様ってあだ名まであるのよ。<line-break>私も負けてられないわよね。<end>やるわよねえ。<line-break>最近、ギルドで評判のノエルって子。<line-break>大活躍みたいよ。<line-break>私も、剣を使わせたら<line-break>ロストールの平民街じゃ無敵だったけど<line-break>もっとスゴイ人物がたくさんいるのね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3But isn't Noel even more impressive? The party she leads seems to be making quite a name for itself lately. I need to keep working toward my own dream. After all, she's another girl from Rostorl, just like me.<end>Have you heard? Noel's party has pulled off something impressive again. She's even earned the nickname “the Little Hero” for rivaling Hero Nemea. I can't afford to fall behind.<end>That Noel is really something. She's the talk of the Guild lately, with all her exploits. I used to be unbeatable with a sword in Rostorl's common quarter, but I suppose there are plenty of people out there who are even more remarkable.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3그보다 대단하지 않아? 노엘이라는 아이가 리더인 파티. 요즘 크게 활약하고 있잖아? 나도 꿈을 향해 열심히 해야겠어. 같은 로스톨 출신 여자아이니까.<end>있지, 들었어? 노엘이라는 아이가 리더인 파티가 또 크게 활약했다나 봐. 그 애, 용자 네메아에 맞서 ‘작은 용자님’이라는 별명까지 생겼대. 나도 질 수 없지.<end>대단하네. 요즘 길드에서 평판이 좋은 노엘이라는 아이. 크게 활약하고 있대. 나도 검을 잡으면 로스톨 평민가에서는 무적이었는데, 더 대단한 사람이 정말 많구나.<end>"

## 980060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　聖杯？　<line-break>残念だけど、まだ、ね。<line-break>騎士になる前に、取り返さないとね。<end>"
EN: "What? The Grail? I'm afraid I still haven't recovered it. I'll have to get it back before I can become a knight.<end>"
KO: "응? 성배? 아쉽지만 아직이야. 기사가 되기 전에 되찾아야지.<end>"

## 980061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　どうしてそんなに<line-break>騎士になりたいのか、ですって？<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know why I'm so determined to become a knight?<end>"
KO: "네? 왜 그렇게 기사가 되고 싶냐고요?<end>"

## 980062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうねえ…。前にも話したと思うけど<line-break>最初はやっぱり、父さんが<line-break>騎士だったから、かな。<end>"
EN: "Well... I think I've told you this before. At first, I suppose it was because my father was a knight.<end>"
KO: "글쎄…. 전에도 말했던 것 같은데, 처음엔 역시 아버지가 기사였기 때문일까.<end>"

## 980063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから私も当然騎士になると思ってた。<line-break>ロストールでは女は騎士になれない<line-break>なんてこと、知らなかったしね。<end>"
EN: "So naturally, I assumed I'd become one too. I didn't know women weren't allowed to be knights in Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "그래서 나도 당연히 기사가 될 거라고 생각했어. 로스톨에서는 여자가 기사가 될 수 없다는 걸 몰랐거든.<end>"

## 980064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとは…、<line-break>そう、私、守れなかったのよ。<line-break>もう、だいぶ昔の話になるけどね。<end>"
EN: "And then... I failed to protect someone. It was a long time ago now.<end>"
KO: "그리고 또…그래, 나는 지키지 못했어. 이제 꽤 오래전 이야기지만.<end>"

## 980065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっと、そいつのそばにいたのに。<line-break>私がいつも守ってあげてたのに…。<end>"
EN: "I'd always been there. I was always the one who kept that person safe...<end>"
KO: "계속 그 녀석 곁에 있었는데. 내가 늘 지켜 줬는데….<end>"

## 980066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、私は守れるようになりたいの。<line-break>そいつはもう、いないけど…、<line-break>二度と、あんなことは繰り返さないわ。<end>"
EN: "That's why I want to become strong enough to protect people. That person is gone now, but... I won't ever let something like that happen again.<end>"
KO: "그래서 난 지킬 수 있는 사람이 되고 싶어. 그 사람은 이제 없지만…, 두 번 다시 그런 일은 반복하지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 980067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふふ、ごめんなさい。<line-break>うまく話せてないね、私。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Sorry. I'm not explaining this very well, am I?<end>"
KO: "…후후, 미안해요. 저, 말을 잘 못하고 있네요.<end>"

## 980068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の方は、残念だけど、<line-break>まだ聖杯を取り戻せていないわ。<line-break>　<line-break>これじゃあ、騎士になる以前に、<line-break>先生に顔向けもできない…。　　<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid I still haven't recovered the Grail. Forget becoming a knight―I can't even face my teacher like this...<end>"
KO: "내 쪽은 아쉽지만 아직 성배를 되찾지 못했어. 이래선 기사가 되기는커녕 선생님 얼굴도 못 보겠어….<end>"

## 980069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生、とても強いんだけど、<line-break>右腕のケガが悪くてね。<line-break>時々、無理をするから心配なのよ。<line-break>先生は話してくれないんだけど、<line-break>そのケガ、闇討ちでやられたんだって<line-break>聞いているわ。許せない話よね。<end>"
EN: "My teacher is very strong, but his right arm is badly injured. Sometimes he pushes himself too hard, and I worry. He won't discuss it with me, but I heard the injury was inflicted in a sneak attack. It's unforgivable, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "선생님은 정말 강하지만 오른팔 부상이 심해. 가끔 무리해서 걱정돼. 선생님은 말해 주지 않지만 그 상처, 기습을 당해서 생긴 거라고 들었어. 용서할 수 없는 일이야.<end>"

## 980070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…その、闇討ちをした奴って、<line-break>今どこで何をしてるのかなあ？<end>"
EN: "...I wonder where the person who carried out that sneak attack is now, and what they're doing.<end>"
KO: "…그 습격한 녀석은 지금 어디서 뭘 하고 있을까?<end>"

## 980071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　ふふ、いやねえ。<line-break>敵討ちとかなんて考えていないわよ。<line-break>たぶん先生も、望んでないわ。<line-break>そんなことより、もっと剣の腕を磨いて<line-break>早く騎士にならないとね。<end>"
EN: "What? Heh, don't be silly. I'm not thinking about revenge. He probably wouldn't want that either. I need to focus on honing my swordsmanship and becoming a knight as soon as I can.<end>"
KO: "응? 후후, 싫다. 복수 같은 건 생각하지 않아. 아마 선생님도 바라지 않을 거야. 그보다 검술을 더 갈고닦아서 빨리 기사가 되어야지.<end>"

## 980072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$3D><equal>4あなたと一緒にいる<line-break>ユーリスってホントにヘンな子ね。<line-break>　<line-break>騎士になるって、他の人のために<line-break>剣を振るんですね、スゴイ！　だって。<line-break>くすっ。わけわからないわ。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>5あ、さっき、ヴァンに<line-break>いきなりダジャレを言われて<line-break>凍え死にそうだったわ。<line-break>あのダジャレ魔人、<line-break>本当になんとかしてよ。<line-break>彼のシャレ、寒すぎるのよ！<end><if><value:$3D><equal>6あのエステルって<line-break>妙に人なつっこいヘンな子、<line-break>あなたの連れだったわよね？<line-break>どう見ても大したことなさそうなのに<line-break>なんか、ふしぎな魔力みたいのを<line-break>感じるのよねえ。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>12あなたがうらやましいわ。<line-break>あの剣狼のゼネテスと一緒に<line-break>旅ができるなんて。<line-break>町の門のところで彼を見かけたの。<line-break>一見、だらしなさそうだったけど、<line-break>まったくスキがなかったわ…。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>13あなたって、確かにセラに比べたら<line-break>強くないんでしょうけど<line-break>彼より、よっぽど好感が持てるわ。<line-break>さっきだって…！<line-break>ホントにセラって失礼ね。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>10そうそう、あなたの弟にあったわ。<line-break>ふふ。<line-break>ホントにお姉ちゃん子ね。<line-break>…また、ちょっと<line-break>あいつのこと思い出しちゃった。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>14ねえ、聞いて。<line-break>さっき当代一の名工といわれる<line-break>あのデルガドを見かけたのよ。<line-break>私もがんばって、<line-break>彼の作った剣を持つような<line-break>立派な騎士になりたいな。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>15あのナッジっていう<line-break>コーンスの男の子<line-break>あなたの連れでしょう？<line-break>むこうでフラフラしてたわ。<line-break>気をつけないと悪い冒険者に<line-break>彼、襲われちゃうわよ。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>17あなたのパーティの生意気エルフ<line-break>あいかわらずね！<line-break><line-break>なーにが高貴なエルフよ。<line-break>なーにが１６００年生きているよ。<line-break>ホント気に入らないわ！<end><if><value:$3D><equal>18あ、さっき、ボゲーに<line-break>いきなりダジャレを言われて<line-break>脱力しちゃったわ。<line-break>あのダジャレボルダン、<line-break>本当になんとかしてよ。<end><if><value:$3D><equal>19さっき、ルルアンタにあったの。<line-break>彼女のほほえみを見てたら、<line-break>悩んでた気持ちがラクになって…。<line-break>リルビーって<line-break>ホント不思議な種族よね。<end>禁断の聖杯は、<line-break>まだ取り戻せていないわ。<line-break>がんばらなくちゃね。<line-break>聖杯を取り戻さないことには<line-break>みっともなくて<line-break>ロストールに帰れないもの。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$3D><equal>4That Julls traveling with you is a very strange girl. She said, “So becoming a knight means wielding a sword for other people? That's amazing!” Heh. I just don't get her.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>5Van sprang a pun on me just now. I nearly froze to death. Please do something about that fiend and his awful puns. His jokes are just too cold!<end><if><value:$3D><equal>6That oddly friendly, peculiar girl Esther is traveling with you, isn't she? She doesn't look like much, but I sense something like a strange magical power in her.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>12I'm envious. Imagine getting to travel with Xenetes, the Sword Wolf. I saw him by the town gate. He looked rather sloppy at first glance, but he didn't leave a single opening...<end><if><value:$3D><equal>13You may not be as strong as Sera, but you're much more likable. Just now, for instance...! Honestly, Sera is so rude.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>10Oh, that's right, I met your little brother. Heh. He really does adore his big sister. It made me think of that person again, just for a moment.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>14Listen! I just saw Dergado, the man they call the greatest craftsman of our age. I want to work hard and become a knight worthy of carrying one of his swords.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>15That Konsu boy named Nadge is traveling with you, isn't he? He was wandering around over there. You'd better keep an eye on him, or some nasty adventurer might attack him.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>17That insolent Elf in your party hasn't changed a bit! A “noble Elf,” indeed. And sixteen hundred years old―so what? I really can't stand that Elf!<end><if><value:$3D><equal>18Bogee suddenly sprang a pun on me just now. It drained the life right out of me. Please do something about that pun-crazed Boldan.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>19I met Lulluantha just now. Seeing her smile made all my worries feel lighter... Lilubees really are a mysterious race, aren't they?<end>I still haven't recovered the Forbidden Grail. I need to keep trying. I'd be too ashamed to return to Rostorl without it.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$3D><equal>4너와 같이 다니는 유리스라는 애, 정말 이상하더라. 기사가 되면 다른 사람을 위해 검을 휘두르는 거냐며 대단하대. 후후, 무슨 소린지 모르겠어.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>5아까 반이 갑자기 아재개그를 해서 얼어 죽는 줄 알았어. 그 개그 마인 좀 어떻게 해 줘. 너무 썰렁하다고!<end><if><value:$3D><equal>6그 에스텔이라는 묘하게 붙임성 좋은 애, 네 동료지? 별거 없어 보이는데 이상한 마력 같은 게 느껴져.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>12네가 부러워. 그 검랑 제네테스와 같이 여행하다니. 성문에서 봤는데 겉보기엔 흐트러져도 빈틈이 전혀 없더라….<end><if><value:$3D><equal>13넌 세라보다는 약하겠지만 그보다 훨씬 호감이 가. 아까도…! 세라는 정말 무례해.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>10그래, 네 동생 만났어. 후후, 정말 누나밖에 모르더라. …또 그 애가 조금 생각났어.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>14있잖아, 아까 당대 최고의 명공이라는 델가도를 봤어. 나도 열심히 해서 그의 검을 들 만한 훌륭한 기사가 되고 싶어.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>15나지라는 콘스 남자아이, 네 동료지? 저쪽에서 혼자 돌아다니더라. 조심하지 않으면 나쁜 모험자에게 당할 거야.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>17네 파티의 건방진 엘프, 여전하더라! 뭐가 고귀한 엘프야. 뭐가 1600년을 살았다는 거야. 정말 마음에 안 들어!<end><if><value:$3D><equal>18아까 보게가 갑자기 아재개그를 해서 맥이 풀렸어. 그 개그 보르단 좀 어떻게 해 줘.<end><if><value:$3D><equal>19아까 루루안타를 만났어. 그 아이의 미소를 보고 있자니 고민하던 마음이 편해졌어…. 릴비는 정말 신기한 종족이야.<end>금단의 성배는 아직 되찾지 못했어. 힘내야지. 성배를 되찾지 못하면 창피해서 로스톨로 돌아갈 수 없잖아.<end>"

## 980073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、また会ったわね。　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>今日、私、とっても機嫌がいいの。<line-break>ね、暇があったら<line-break>ちょっと、つきあってくれない？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, we meet again, <value:$28>. I'm in a wonderful mood today. If you have some time, would you keep me company for a while?<end>"
KO: "후후, 또 만났네, <value:$28>. 오늘 나 기분이 아주 좋아. 있지, 시간 있으면 잠깐 어울려 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 980074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この間、カルラって人に会ったの。　　<line-break>すごく、カッコいい女の子なのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ロセンで愚王ペウダの手下が<line-break>村娘をさらおうとしてたの。<line-break>それに単身挑んでいたのがカルラよ。<end>"
EN: "I met a girl named Karula the other day. She was incredibly cool. In Rocen, that fool King Peuda's men were trying to abduct a village girl, and Karula stood against them all by herself.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전에 카를라라는 사람을 만났어. 정말 멋진 여자아이더라. 로센에서 우왕 페우다의 부하들이 마을 처녀를 납치하려 했는데 거기에 혼자 맞서고 있던 게 카를라였어.<end>"

## 980075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラはそいつらをあっさり倒すと、<line-break>元凶であるペウダを倒すって言って<line-break>ロセンの王宮に乗り込んでいったの。<line-break>私？　もちろんついていったわ。<line-break>で、途中ちょっと面倒もあったけど<line-break>ふたりでペウダをこらしめてやったの。<end>"
EN: "Karula made short work of them, then said she was going after Peuda, the one behind it all, and stormed into the royal palace in Rocen. Me? Of course I went with her. We ran into a little trouble along the way, but the two of us gave Peuda a good thrashing.<end>"
KO: "카를라는 녀석들을 가볍게 쓰러뜨리더니 원흉인 페우다를 쓰러뜨리겠다며 로센 왕궁으로 쳐들어갔어. 나? 물론 따라갔지. 그리고 도중에….<end>"

## 980076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女、すごく強かったわ。<line-break>でね、腰に騎士章を下げてたの。<line-break>あれはディンガルの黒鎧騎士のものね。<end>"
EN: "She was so strong. And she had a knight's insignia hanging at her waist. It was the insignia of Dyneskal's Black-Armored Knights.<end>"
KO: "그녀, 정말 강했어. 그리고 허리에 기사장을 달고 있었지. 그건 딘갈의 흑갑기사 문장이야.<end>"

## 980077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルって実力主義なのよ。<line-break>私も、ディンガルでなら<line-break>騎士になれるかもしれないわ。<end>"
EN: "Dyneskal rewards ability above all else. Maybe I could become a knight there, too.<end>"
KO: "딘갈은 실력주의야. 나도 딘갈에서라면 기사가 될 수 있을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 980078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとりで話しちゃってごめんなさい。<line-break>でも、なんだか、進むべき道が<line-break>見えた気がして、うれしくって…。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, I've been doing all the talking. I just feel as though I've caught a glimpse of the path I should take, and it makes me so happy...<end>"
KO: "혼자 떠들어서 미안해. 하지만 왠지 앞으로 가야 할 길이 보인 것 같아서 기뻐서….<end>"

## 980079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあね。<line-break>縁があったら、また会いましょ。　　<end>"
EN: "Goodbye, then. If fate allows, let's meet again.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕. 인연이 있으면 또 만나자.<end>"

## 980080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ。っるさいわねー。<line-break>お酒なんか飲んでないわよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もう、余計なところで<line-break>干渉しないでよ。<line-break>なんか用？<end>"
EN: "What? Oh, stop nagging. I haven't been drinking. And quit meddling where you're not wanted. Did you need something?<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 시끄럽네. 술 같은 거 안 마셨어. 정말 쓸데없는 데서 간섭하지 마. 무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 980081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…用もないのに<line-break>ただ、声をかけただけなのね。　<line-break>　<line-break>まあ、いいわ。<line-break>今日はゆっくりと語ってあげる。<line-break>そこに座りなさい。<end>"
EN: "So you didn't actually need anything―you just called out to me. Well, fine. Today, I'll take my time and tell you a story. Sit down.<end>"
KO: "…볼일도 없는데 그냥 말을 건 거였구나. 뭐, 좋아. 오늘은 천천히 이야기해 줄게. 거기 앉아.<end>"

## 980082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "す・わ・り・な・さ・い！<end>"
EN: "Sit. Down. Now!<end>"
KO: "앉·으·세·요!<end>"

## 980083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には幼なじみがいてね。<line-break>泣き虫でさ、頼りない奴だったわ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>そいつが小さいころ、両親を亡くして<line-break>うちに引き取られることになったのね。<end>"
EN: "I had a childhood friend. A crybaby, and utterly hopeless. My friend lost both parents at an early age and was taken in by my family.<end>"
KO: "나에겐 소꿉친구가 있었어. 울보에 못 미더운 녀석이었지. 그 녀석이 어릴 때 부모를 잃고 우리 집에 맡겨졌어.<end>"

## 980084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でさ、そいつがうちに来た日<line-break>私、いたずらして、<line-break>そいつを倉の中に閉じこめたの。　　　<end>"
EN: "The day my friend came to live with us, I played a prank and locked the kid in the storehouse.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그 녀석이 우리 집에 온 날 내가 장난으로 그 녀석을 창고 안에 가둬 버렸어.<end>"

## 980085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで閉じこめたかなんて<line-break>知らないわよ。子供のすることだもの。<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、そうしたら、そいつ<line-break>泣いちゃって。さすがに悪い気がして<line-break>私、そいつを倉の中から助け出したの。<end>"
EN: "I don't know why I did it. I was a child; children do things like that. Anyway, my friend started crying. That made me feel guilty, so I let the kid out of the storehouse.<end>"
KO: "왜 가뒀는지는 나도 몰라. 애가 한 짓이잖아. 아무튼 그랬더니 그 녀석이 울어 버렸어. 나도 미안해져서 창고에서 꺼내 줬지.<end>"

## 980086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしたら、そいつ、私に感謝しちゃって<line-break>すっかり、私になついちゃってね。<line-break>それからは、ずっと私と一緒。<line-break>私も、そいつの保護者みたいな感じで<line-break>そいつのこと、守ってたのよ。<line-break>ホントにそれが当たり前だと思ってた。<end>"
EN: "My friend was so grateful and grew completely attached to me. From then on, we were always together. I was like a guardian, always keeping my friend safe. I truly thought that was simply the way things were.<end>"
KO: "그랬더니 그 녀석이 나한테 고마워하면서 완전히 따르게 됐지. 그 뒤론 줄곧 나와 함께였어. 나도 보호자처럼 녀석을 지켜 줬고. 정말 그게 당연하다고 생각했어.<end>"

## 980087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのとき？<line-break>謝ってないわ。<line-break>謝る機会を逸しちゃったのよ。　　<line-break>ま、そいつとあなたが<line-break>外見がよく似てたの。<line-break>ホント、瓜ふたつよ。<end>"
EN: "Back then? No, I never apologized. I missed my chance. Anyway, you look just like my friend. The resemblance is uncanny.<end>"
KO: "그때? 사과 안 했어. 사과할 기회를 놓쳐 버렸거든. 뭐, 그 녀석과 네가 외모가 꽤 비슷했어. 정말 판박이였지.<end>"

## 980088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最初、声をかけたのも似てたから。<line-break>だから、ちょっと強引だったし、<line-break>くすっ、横柄だったでしょ？<end>"
EN: "That's why I spoke to you in the first place. So I was a little pushy and, heh, rather high-handed, wasn't I?<end>"
KO: "처음 말을 건 것도 닮아서였어. 그래서 좀 막무가내였고, 후훗, 거만했지?<end>"

## 980089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少しは、気を使って、<line-break>そうでもないとかフォローしてよ。<line-break>もう！<end>"
EN: "You could at least spare my feelings and say I wasn't. Honestly!<end>"
KO: "조금은 눈치껏, 그렇지도 않다고 달래 줘. 정말!<end>"

## 980090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつ？<line-break>さっきから過去形で語ってるでしょ？　<line-break>察しなさいよ。<line-break>死んじゃったわ。<line-break>私の目の前で<line-break>…私をかばって。<end>"
EN: "My friend? I've been speaking in the past tense this whole time. Take the hint. My friend died right in front of me... shielding me.<end>"
KO: "그 사람? 아까부터 과거형으로 말했잖아? 눈치 좀 채. 죽었어. 내 눈앞에서… 나를 감싸다가.<end>"

## 980091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの平民街に<line-break>突然、怪物が出現した事件があって…　<line-break>貴族のいたずら…らしいんだけどね。<line-break>で、一緒に怪物を退治に出たんだけど<line-break>そのとき、ね…。<line-break>ったく、弱いのにカッコつけて…。<end>"
EN: "There was an incident in Rostorl's commoners' quarter. A monster suddenly appeared... Apparently, it was a prank by the nobility. We went out together to defeat it, and that was when... Damn fool. So weak, but still trying to act tough...<end>"
KO: "로스톨 평민가에 갑자기 괴물이 나타난 사건이 있었어…. 귀족의 장난…이었다나. 그래서 같이 괴물을 퇴치하러 갔는데 그때 말이지…. 정말, 약하면서 폼은 잡고….<end>"

## 980092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのとき、ふと、倉に閉じこめたこと、<line-break>思い出しちゃって…。あ…、もう<line-break>謝ることができないんだ…って。<line-break>空気みたいな奴だったけど<line-break>結構、大きな存在だったんだなって<line-break>そのとき、気づいて…。<end>"
EN: "At that moment, I suddenly remembered locking my friend in the storehouse. I thought, “Oh... now I'll never be able to apologize.” My friend had always just been there, like the air. Only then did I realize what a large place my friend had held in my life...<end>"
KO: "그때 문득 창고에 가둔 일이 떠올랐어…. 아… 이제 사과할 수도 없구나 하고. 공기 같은 녀석이었는데 꽤 큰 존재였다는 걸 그때 깨달았지….<end>"

## 980093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "守ってやれなかった…。　　　　　　　<line-break>もう少し、力があれば<line-break>守ってやれたのに…。<end>"
EN: "I couldn't protect my friend... If I'd only been a little stronger, I could have protected my friend...<end>"
KO: "지켜 주지 못했어…. 조금만 더 힘이 있었다면 지켜 줄 수 있었는데….<end>"

## 980094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "守りたかった…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…ごめんね。<line-break>変なこと、しゃべったね。<end>"
EN: "I wanted to protect my friend... I'm sorry. That was a strange thing to tell you.<end>"
KO: "지키고 싶었어…. …미안. 이상한 소리를 했네.<end>"

## 980095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、まだグズグズ言ってる。<line-break>私、ダメね。自己嫌悪しちゃう。　<line-break>　<line-break>ホント、ゴメンね。<line-break>あなたってそいつに似てるから<line-break>つい、甘えちゃうのかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "And here I am, still going on about it. I'm hopeless. I hate myself for this. I'm really sorry. You remind me so much of my friend that maybe I end up leaning on you.<end>"
KO: "아휴, 아직도 투덜거리네. 나 참 못났다. 자기혐오하게 돼. 정말 미안해. 네가 그 녀석이랑 닮아서 나도 모르게 응석 부리는 걸지도 몰라.<end>"

## 980096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 980097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んにゃーん。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Mrrrow.<end>"
KO: "냐옹.<end>"

## 980098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰なの、あなた！<line-break>私たちの邪魔をしないで！<end>"
EN: "Who are you? Stay out of our way!<end>"
KO: "누구야, 당신! 우리를 방해하지 마!<end>"

## 980099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、オルファウスさん。<line-break>私たち、もう行きます。<line-break>本当にありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Orphaus, we'll be going now. Thank you so much for everything.<end>"
KO: "그럼 오르파우스 씨. 저희는 이제 가 볼게요. 정말 감사했습니다.<end>"

## 980100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………！<line-break>え？　えええええっ！？<end>"
EN: "...! What? Whaaaaat?!<end>"
KO: "………………! 어? 어어어어!?<end>"

## 980101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、３人様ご１泊ということで　　<line-break>よろしいですね？<end>"
EN: "So, that's three guests for one night. Is that correct?<end>"
KO: "그럼 세 분이 하룻밤 묵으시는 걸로 괜찮으시죠?<end>"

## 980102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうことで、頼むゴブ。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Please do, gob.<end>"
KO: "그런 거니까, 부탁한다 고브.<end>"

## 980103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かしこまりました。<line-break>それではお部屋のご用意を…。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Very good. I'll prepare your room...<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 그럼 방을 준비해 드리겠습니다…<end>"

## 980104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ご主人。すまないゴブが、<line-break>今日の宿泊はキャンセルするゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>我々も忙しい身ゴブ。<line-break>その辺りの事情をくんでほしいゴブ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Ah, sir. Sorry, gob, but we'll have to cancel our stay tonight. We're busy people, you see. I trust you'll understand, gob.<end>"
KO: "아, 주인장. 미안하지만 오늘 숙박은 취소할 고브. 우리도 바쁜 몸이니 그 사정을 헤아려 줬으면 고브.<end>"

## 980105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今のお客さん、何かやらかしたんですか？<line-break>お知り合いみたいですが。<end>"
EN: "Did that guest do something wrong? You seemed to know each other.<end>"
KO: "방금 손님, 무슨 사고라도 쳤나요? 아는 사이 같던데.<end>"

## 980106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待たせしました。<line-break>それではお部屋にご案内…<end>"
EN: "Thank you for waiting. Now, if you'll follow me to your room...<end>"
KO: "기다리셨습니다. 그럼 방으로 안내해 드리겠습니다….<end>"

## 980107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと言ってる場合じゃないわ。<line-break>うだうだ言わないで、さっさと来る！<end>"
EN: "We don't have time for this. Stop dawdling and get over here!<end>"
KO: "그런 말 할 때가 아니야. 투덜대지 말고 얼른 와!<end>"
